Delivery states are derived from the recipient statuses under the message lock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Idempotency | `transitionKey` per producer (`ingest:<id>:received`, `evt:<eventId>:<to>`, `signal:<deliveryId>:<signalKey>:<to>`, `recover:…`, `expire:<id>:<version>`, `api:<org>:<operation>:<Idempotency-Key>`), unique per message in the history; keys older than the 3-month history retention are decided by the state rules only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Lock order  | `delivery_reinjections` row (queue-id binding only) → `email_messages` row → `email_deliveries` rows → recipients                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

**Transition table** (summary of [L3](../plan/steps/08-message-lifecycle.md#l3-the-transition-table-data); **bold** = produced in step 3.3):

| Edge             | From → to                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Roles                | Producer                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **E1**, **E2**   | (creation) → `received` → `persisted`                                                                                                                                                                                                            | ingestion            | **smtp-adapter commit**                                                                                                                                              |
| **E3**           | `persisted` → `scanning`                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | scanner              | **email-worker scan role (4.1)**                                                                                                                                     |
| ~~E4~~           | `persisted` → `approved` — **retired in step 4.1** (the id is never reused)                                                                                                                                                                      | —                    | —                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **E5**           | `persisted` → `policy_evaluated` (parse failed fail-safe)                                                                                                                                                                                        | policy               | **policy-engine (5.2)**                                                                                                                                              |
| **E6**, **E7**   | `scanning` → `scanned` \| `scan_failed`                                                                                                                                                                                                          | scanner              | **email-worker scan role (4.1)**                                                                                                                                     |
| **E8, E9**       | `scanned` \| `scan_failed` → `policy_evaluated`                                                                                                                                                                                                  | policy               | **policy-engine (5.2)**                                                                                                                                              |
| **E10–E13**      | `policy_evaluated` → `approved` \| `quarantined` \| `held_for_review` \| `rejected` (5.3: E10 also `policy_partial` for a per-recipient split with allowed recipients)                                                                           | policy               | **policy-engine (5.2, 5.3)**                                                                                                                                         |
| **E14**, **E15** | `quarantined` \| `held_for_review` → `approved` (new round)                                                                                                                                                                                      | operator, quarantine | **policy-engine release executor (5.3)**; the api operator route answers 409 since 5.3 (Q30)                                                                         |
| **E16–E18**      | `held_for_review` → `quarantined` (`review_quarantined`); `quarantined` \| `held_for_review` → `rejected` (`quarantine_deleted`, `review_rejected`; when the last open entry is rejected/deleted and no recipient is pending/deferred/delivered) | quarantine           | **api (manual quarantine, reject, delete, deny) (5.3)**                                                                                                              |
| **E19**          | `scanning`, `scanned`, `scan_failed`, `policy_evaluated`, `quarantined`, `held_for_review`, `expired` → `scanning` (new round)                                                                                                                   | operator             | **api rescan**                                                                                                                                                       |
| **E20**          | `approved` → `delivery_pending`                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | delivery             | **delivery-worker planner**                                                                                                                                          |
| **E21**–**E24**  | `delivery_pending` ⇄ `delivery_retry`; → `delivered` \| `rejected` (derived)                                                                                                                                                                     | delivery, recovery   | **shared recorder; event-relay lifecycle check**                                                                                                                     |
| ~~E25~~          | `delivered` \| `rejected` → `archived` — **retired in step 8.1** ([ADR-0064](adr/0064-journal-capture-intents-and-archive-consumer.md), A25; the id is never reused, `archived` stays a reserved state, the `archive` actor role stays reserved) | —                    | —                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **E26**          | every expirable state → `expired`                                                                                                                                                                                                                | sweeper, quarantine  | **event-relay expiry sweeper** (`deadline_exceeded`); **quarantine sweeper (5.3)** (`quarantine_expired`, after the message's last entries expired; never a release) |
| **E28**          | `delivered` \| `rejected` \| `expired` → `approved` (new round, `quarantine_partial_release`): releasing a held recipient after the rest of the message finished                                                                                 | quarantine           | **policy-engine release executor (5.3)**                                                                                                                             |
| **E29**          | `rejected` → `quarantined` (same round, `manual_quarantine`; only from `policy_reject`/`policy_discard`)                                                                                                                                         | quarantine           | **api manual quarantine (5.3)**                                                                                                                                      |
| ~~E27~~          | `scanned` → `approved` — **retired in step 5.2** with the dev pass-through (the id is never reused; `dev_passthrough` stays a valid actor role and reason code for history rows only)                                                            | —                    | —                                                                                                                                                                    |

The MIME stage (step 3.1) runs within `persisted` and changes no lifecycle state; its outcome event decides the next transition by the stage that consumes it (`parsed.v1` → the scan stage, E3; `parse_failed.v1` → the policy-engine, E5 then E11: quarantined by the floor `policy.floor.not_scanned`). **Bold** in the table above: produced by step 3.3, 4.1, 5.2 or 5.3. **Step 5.3 ([Q5](../plan/steps/17-quarantine.md#q5-lifecycle-mapping-recipient-state-vs-message-state-adr-0046-decision-3), [ADR-0046](adr/0046-quarantine-store-and-per-recipient-decisions.md)):** the message state is the delivery-phase view; each recipient's fate is `email_recipients.status` plus its quarantine entry, written in the same writer transaction as the message transition (one door). Held recipients are `quarantined`, rejected recipients `discarded` (5.2 left both `pending`); `deriveDeliveryState` counts both as settled, so a split message ends `delivered/delivery_partial` while its held entries stay `held`. `LIFECYCLE_REASON_CODES` += `policy_partial`, `quarantine_partial_release`, `manual_quarantine`, `quarantine_expired`; E26 pairs `deadline_exceeded` with role `sweeper` and `quarantine_expired` with role `quarantine`. ADR-0025's final states stay final for automatic processing; E28/E29 are explicit, operator-caused re-entries like E14.

## 22. Scan pipeline (step 4.1)

Step 4.2 adds the `attachment` engine on `scan.attachment` ([design](../plan/steps/10-attachment-scanning.md), [ADR-0028](adr/0028-attachment-scanning-engine.md), [runbook](../runbooks/attachment-scanning.md)); the orchestrator is unchanged except for the A12 extensions (engine dependencies, child rows, six failure codes). The rows marked 4.2 below are its additions.

[Design](../plan/steps/09-rspamd.md), [ADR-0026](adr/0026-security-scan-pipeline.md), [runbook](../runbooks/scan-pipeline.md), [diagram 09](diagrams/09-scanning-sequence.md), [diagram 23](diagrams/23-scan-pipeline.md). IMPLEMENTED.

| Aspect                              | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Trigger                             | `mail.message.parsed.v1` (round 0, `scan_kind = initial`) and `mail.message.rescan_requested.v1` (its round ≥ 1, `rescan`) on every registered stage-1 engine's queue (`scan.spam`, `scan.attachment`, `scan.url`); since step 4.4 a **stage-2** engine (catalog `dependsOn`, `bec`) is triggered only by `scan_stage_ready.v1`, written once per round by the transaction that inserts the last dependency final (§24). `parse_failed.v1` never starts a scan                   |
| Round                               | One `email_scans` row per `(message_id, lifecycle_round, received_at)` (unique): `status` (`running`, `completed`, `failed`, `timed_out`), `scan_verdict`, `reason_codes`, `required_engines` (snapshot), `trigger_event_id`, `deadline_at`. The first engine handler opens it and applies E3 (`scan-open:<id>:0`); old rounds are never updated by a newer one                                                                                                                  |
| Engine results                      | `scan_engine_results`: failed attempts are non-final rows (`is_final = 0`, outcome `timeout`/`error` only: CHECK), one final row per `(scan, engine)` (unique on the generated `final_engine`); `attempt`, `engine_version`, `score`, `required_score`, `action`, `reason_codes`, `error_code`, `settings_version`, `evidence` (`signals[]` with bounded options, `rcptTruncated?`)                                                                                              |
| Authentication                      | `email_auth_results`: one row per `(scan, engine)` (Rspamd): `spf`/`dkim`/`dmarc`/`arc` outcomes, `spf_domain`, `dkim_signatures`, `dmarc_policy`, `dmarc_domain`. From the engine's own checks with the trusted envelope, never from message headers                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Completion                          | Under the message lock (message → scan): when every required engine has a final row, aggregate (`failedRequired` → `scan_failed`, else `scanned`), update `email_scans`, transition E6/E7 (`scan-complete:<id>:<round>`, `expectedRound`), write `scanned.v1` — one transaction with the ledger row. A superseded round completes its row but writes no event. The deadline sweeper finalizes overdue rounds with `timeout`                                                      |
| Idempotency                         | `processOnce` ledger per `(email-worker.scan.<engine>, eventId)` (`rspamd`, `attachment`); the scan-row and final-row unique keys; the completion transition key; step 4.2 child rows (`attachment_scan_results`, `sandbox_submissions`, unique per `(scan_id, attachment_id, received_at)`) and the `sandbox_requested.v1` outbox rows are written only in the transaction that inserts the final row                                                                           |
| Read API                            | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans` (`message:read`, replica, newest round first, ≤ 20 per page): per round the status, verdict, reasons, required engines, times, `auth`, and per engine the final result, symbols with options, attempts, version and settings version. Another organization's message → `404 message_not_found`                                                                                                                                          |
| Settings API                        | `GET /api/v1/scan-settings` (`policy:read`); `PUT /api/v1/scan-settings/{engine}` and `DELETE …?expectedVersion=n` (`scan:settings_manage`, organization admin and security analyst): `scan_engine_settings` row per `(organization, engine)`, validated by `resolveRspamdSettings`, version compare-and-set (`409 version_conflict`), `422 scan_settings_invalid`, audited `scan_settings.updated`; workers apply within `SCAN_SETTINGS_CACHE_TTL_MS`                           |
| Training API                        | `POST /api/v1/platform/organizations/{organizationId}/messages/{messageId}/scan-training` (`platform:scan_training`, users only, MFA, 60/min): `learn_spam`, `learn_ham`, `fuzzy_add`, `fuzzy_delete` (flag `spam` 11 / `phishing` 12); the EML is streamed from S3 to the controller with the enable password; `503 rspamd_unavailable`, `409 content_unavailable`; audited `scan_training.<operation>` and `email_events` `scan.training`                                      |
| Rescan                              | `POST /api/v1/messages/{id}/rescan` (E19, unchanged) now produces a real scan of the new round on every registered engine                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Attachment results (4.2)            | `attachment_scan_results`: one row per `(scan, attachment)` of the final attachment row (hashes, sizes, type ids, verdict, reason codes, ClamAV signature and versions, YARA bundle and matches, archive summary without entry names, known-hash severity, cache result); `sandbox_submissions` + `sandbox_requested.v1` for suspicious/unknown attachments (A11). The read API returns them under the `attachment` engine with the sanitized filename and the submission status |
| Threat hashes and rescan jobs (4.2) | `GET/POST /api/v1/platform/threat-hashes`, `DELETE …/{sha256}?reason=` (`platform:threat_intel_manage`, users only, MFA): the platform-wide `malware_signatures` catalog; `POST/GET /api/v1/platform/attachment-rescans`, `GET …/{jobId}`, `POST …/{jobId}/cancel` (`platform:scan_rescan`, users only, MFA): job rows only; the email-worker runner executes them through E19 (`scanner`, `signature_rescan`)                                                                   |
| Dev pass-through (4.2 A15)          | **Removed in step 5.2**: a `malicious` round carries a risk hard block, so the policy floor rejects it (§28). Until then: `outcome = scanned` with `verdict = malicious` is held in `scanned` (no approval, `delivery_approvals_skipped_total{reason="malicious_verdict"}`); development and CI stand-in only, policy (5.2) decides in production                                                                                                                                |
| Metrics                             | email-worker `scan_engine_results_total`, `scan_engine_duration_seconds`, `scan_engine_failures_total`, `scan_engine_circuit_state`, `scan_rounds_completed_total`, `scan_round_duration_seconds`, `scan_event_lag_seconds`, `scan_events_skipped_total`, `scan_deadline_finalized_total`, `scan_engine_info`, …; alert rules `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/scanning.rules.yml` (a contract for the series names)                                                             |

## 23. URL protection (step 4.3)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/11-url-protection.md), [ADR-0029](adr/0029-url-security-engine.md) (engine), [ADR-0030](adr/0030-url-rewriting-and-click-tokens.md) (rewriting and tokens), [ADR-0031](adr/0031-time-of-click-service.md) (click-service), [ADR-0032](adr/0032-url-browser-isolation.md) (browser isolation); runbooks [url-scanning](../runbooks/url-scanning.md), [click-service](../runbooks/click-service.md), [url-browser-worker](../runbooks/url-browser-worker.md); [deployment](../deployment/url-protection.md).

| Aspect                 | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Engine                 | Catalog entry `url` (stage `url`, queue `scan.url`, required for every tenant, not disableable); `SCAN_ENGINES` default `rspamd,attachment,url`. Ledger consumer `email-worker.scan.url` (user `eg-scanner`); production Deployment `email-worker-url` (`SCAN_CONSUMERS=url`). Reads the parse row, body and text/html attachment objects from S3 (prefix, size and SHA-256 verified), extracts and normalizes URLs (`url-extract/1`, U5 identity/redirect forms), reputation (`local_feed` uncached, `dnsbl`, `rdap` through the HMAC-authenticated Redis cache), lookalikes, the SSRF-guarded fetcher (`url-fetch/1`), verdict map `url-map/1`; no URLs → `skipped` (`url.none`) |
| Child rows             | Written by `ScanRepository.insertFinal` in the final row's transaction only: `message_urls` (seq stable per message), `url_scan_results` (one per URL per round), `url_rewrites` (one per round), `url_dynamic_inspections` (+ one `inspection_requested.v1` outbox row each). Unique keys end with `received_at`; no content columns                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Rewriting              | In the engine at the end of a definite scan: the rewritten copy (§11) with links `URL_CLICK_BASE_URL/c/<token>` in rewritable `text/html` (`a`/`area` `href` splice only) and `text/plain` parts; headers and untouched nodes byte-identical; signed/encrypted messages, DKIM policy `skip`, oversize and structure mismatches are skipped (never a failure). Delivery uses the copy per §18                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Click token            | 46 bytes base64url (62 characters): version, key id, organization, message, `received_at` ms, `url_seq`, expiry, HMAC-SHA256/128 under `URL_TOKEN_KEY_CURRENT` (previous key for verification only); no URL, tenant name or recipient inside; the URL is read server side (`message_urls.url`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Click-service          | Public `GET /c/{token}`, `GET /c/{token}/continue?n=` on 3080, admin 3081 (`/health/*`, `/metrics`, never exposed). MySQL account `gateway_click` (SELECT on six tables, INSERT on `url_click_events`, `message_outbox`), Redis; no S3, no broker. 302 only to the stored URL; warn 200 / block 403 / invalid 404 / expired 410 / rate limited 429 / unavailable 503 pages from constant templates; one `url_click_events` row + one `mail.url.clicked.v1` outbox row per decided click                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Browser worker / proxy | `url-browser-worker` (profile `url-browser`): broker user `eg-url-browser` only (no MySQL, S3, Redis, token or cache keys), Chromium headless shell through `url-egress-proxy` (3128, token-authenticated, the same SSRF guard and restricted resolver, admin 3091)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Read API               | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans`: `urls[]` and `rewrite` on the `url` entry (replica, tenant-scoped; no cache flags, no per-URL durations; url engine `durationMs` null, times whole seconds)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Settings API           | `/api/v1/scan-settings/url` (U13; domains normalized by `url-security`, public suffixes refused; audited `scan_settings.updated` / `.reset`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Threat indicators      | `GET/POST /api/v1/platform/threat-indicators`, `GET …/{id}` (step AI-4: the list item's view by decimal id, replica, expired included), `DELETE …/{id}?reason=` (`platform:threat_intel_manage`, users only, MFA, 60/min): platform rows of `threat_indicators` (`organization_id` NULL, `source = analyst`), values normalized (url identity form, ASCII domain never a public suffix, canonical IP), `value_sha256`; audited `threat_indicator.added` / `.removed` (before-image) in the same writer transaction; `409 indicator_exists`; an unknown id, a tenant-scoped or a non-URL row → `404 threat_indicator_not_found`                                                     |
| Metrics                | email-worker `scan_url_*`, `url_reputation_*`, `url_provider_breaker_state`, `url_fetch_*{component}`, `url_cache_total`; click-service `click_*`; browser `url_browser_*`; proxy `url_egress_*`; delivery-worker `delivery_approvals_total{source, object}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

## 24. Phishing and BEC detection (step 4.4)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/12-phishing-bec.md), [ADR-0033](adr/0033-bec-engine-and-stage-dependencies.md) (engine, stage dependencies, topology v6), [ADR-0034](adr/0034-bec-ai-layer-invariant.md) (AI layer and aggregation invariant), [ADR-0035](adr/0035-bec-communication-context-privacy.md) (communication history); runbook [bec-detection](../runbooks/bec-detection.md); [deployment](../deployment/bec-detection.md). IMPLEMENTED (the LLM provider is implemented and unit-tested against a fake transport; no real LLM call was made, §13 of the design).

| Aspect                        | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Engine                        | Catalog entry `bec` (stage `bec`, queue `scan.bec`, required for every tenant, not disableable, `dependsOn: ['rspamd', 'url']`); `SCAN_ENGINES` default `rspamd,attachment,url,bec`. Ledger consumer `email-worker.scan.bec` (user `eg-scanner`); production Deployment `email-worker-bec` (`SCAN_CONSUMERS=bec`), the only holder of `BEC_CONTEXT_HMAC_KEY` and `BEC_LLM_API_KEY`. Inbound only; `outbound`/`internal` → `skipped` (`bec.not_inbound`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Stage-2 trigger (generic, B2) | An engine with `dependsOn` (depth 1, validated by `validateCatalog`) is a stage-2 engine. `ScanRepository.complete()` (message and scan row locks held, finals read after the locks) writes **one** `mail.message.scan_stage_ready.v1` outbox row per stage-2 engine `E` of the round's required set when every dependency of `E` in that set has a final row (definite **or** failure), `E` has none, and the final inserted by this transaction is one of the dependencies. The deadline sweeper never writes a trigger. Counted `scan_stage_triggers_total{engine}`                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `scan_stage_ready.v1`         | Strict schema (`messageScanStageReadyV1`): `messageId`, `receivedAt`, `direction`, `emlS3Key` (`org/<id>/…`), `lifecycleRound` 0–65 535, `scanId`, `scanKind` (`initial` ⇔ round 0, `rescan` ⇔ ≥ 1), `engine` (the stage-2 engine), `dependencies` 1–8 `{ engine, outcome }` (unique, never `engine`), `readyAt`. Ids, codes and keys only (content guard). Producer email-worker (outbox → event-relay → `email.security` → `scan.bec`; also forwarded to `email.events` → the dormant `email.analytics` firehose)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Consumption                   | The stage-2 queue binds only the stage event. The round service: `payload.engine` ≠ this engine → ledger no-op `other_stage_engine`; the round row of `payload.lifecycleRound` must exist with `id = payload.scanId`, else ledger no-op `stage_round_missing` (counted, warn) — a stage trigger **never creates** an `email_scans` row; `decideOpen(…, 'stage')` never opens. Attempts, breaker, final row and completion are those of every engine                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Inputs and missing inputs     | The engine reads the round's dependency finals itself (writer, echo-checked): `auth` `available` \| `failed` (rspamd final `timeout`/`error`) \| `missing` (no auth row) \| `not_in_round`; `url` `available` (definite or `skipped`) \| `failed` \| `not_in_round`; `text` `complete` \| `incomplete` \| `absent`; `history` `enabled` \| `disabled` \| `error`. Any unavailable input adds `bec.input_unavailable.*` / `bec.content_incomplete` / `bec.history_unavailable` and the verdict is **at least `unknown`, never `clean`**; `bec.content_incomplete` also covers an incomplete stored header set (`stored_headers_truncated`, or more stored header rows than the 200 read)                                                                 |
| Result                        | Standard final row in `scan_engine_results` (`outcome`, `score`, `required_score` = suspicious threshold, reason codes ≤ 32, `signals` `bec:<rule id>`, `evidence.thresholds = { suspicious, malicious }`) plus one `bec_scan_results` row per round (unique `(scan_id, received_at)`), written in the final row's transaction only when the final row was inserted: versions (`bec/1;lex=…;fm=…;br=…`, `bec-map/1`, settings, lexicon, `bec-text/1`), deterministic and final score/verdict, `inputs`, fired `rules` (≤ 32, evidence ≤ 5 entries each: codes, header names, parsed addresses/domains/display names, profile ids, lexicon ids and **offsets only**), `features`, `ai` / `ai_shadow`, `identity`. Never body text, excerpts or span text |
| Verdict (B6, B9)              | Deterministic first (`evaluateBecRules`, 50 rules): `malicious` needs score ≥ malicious threshold **and** identity **and** intent evidence; `suspicious` from the suspicious threshold or a malicious score without both classes. The optional AI layer can never produce `clean`, never lowers, raises to at most `suspicious` alone, and corroborates `malicious` only within `aiMaliciousBand`; shadow never changes anything                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| History (B12, ADR-0035)       | Opt-in per tenant (`history.enabled`, default off). `bec_contact_history`: HMAC-SHA256 keyed hashes only (org- and scope-separated), counts and first/last seen; written once per message (round 0 of the initial scan, also for outbound/internal mail); inbound rows only for mail that authenticated as its From with a final verdict `clean`/`unknown` (spoofed or flagged mail never makes a sender known); retention sweeper; purge `DELETE /api/v1/bec/history`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| LLM (B10, ADR-0034)           | `@anthropic-ai/sdk` 1.1.0, model `BEC_LLM_MODEL` (default `claude-opus-5-5`), strict JSON-schema output with enumerated indicators and offsets only; gated to borderline/intent-bearing inbound messages; per-tenant daily and platform per-minute Redis budgets (no call without a budget check), breaker, timeout, concurrency; the request carries only the redacted excerpt and derived features. Platform cap `BEC_LLM_MODE` default `off`, tenant opt-in `ai.llm`; failures are recorded (`bec.ai.unavailable`), never fail the attempt                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Read API (B15)                | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans`: the `bec` engine entry carries `bec` (versions, verdicts, scores, thresholds, inputs, `rules[]` with `evidence[]`, `ai[]`, `aiShadow[]`, `feedback[]`) and every round its `scanId`. Replica, tenant-scoped; profile references as this tenant's public ids; no text, spans text, response hashes, AI durations or history hashes; bec `durationMs` null, times whole seconds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Tenant APIs (B18)             | `/api/v1/bec/executives`, `/bec/vendors`, `/bec/trusted-senders` (CRUD, `bec:read` / `bec:manage`, version compare-and-set, caps, audited with before-images), `POST /messages/{messageId}/scans/{scanId}/bec-feedback` (`bec:feedback`, users only; optional bounded suppression), `GET /bec/feedback`, `GET /bec/feedback/export` (`bec:feedback_export`, MFA, NDJSON features only), `GET`/`DELETE /bec/suppressions`, `DELETE /bec/history` (MFA); step AI-4: `GET /bec/trusted-senders/{id}` and `GET /bec/suppressions/{id}` (`bec:read`, API keys accepted, replica, the list item's view, expired entries included). Organization from the authenticated scope only; another tenant's id → `404`                                                |
| Settings API (B13)            | `/api/v1/scan-settings/bec`: thresholds (25..60 / 60..95), `freemailExtraDomains`, `history.*`, `ai.*` within the platform caps; audited `scan_settings.updated` / `.reset`; workers re-validate at use                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Dev pass-through (B17)        | **Removed in step 5.2**: the platform default's rule D2 quarantines a deterministic `suspicious`/`malicious` bec verdict (§28). Until then, §18: a `bec` outcome `suspicious`/`malicious` in a `scanned` round is held (`delivery_approvals_skipped_total{reason="bec_verdict"}`). **Contract for policy 5.2:** policy reads the `bec` engine's outcome from `scanned.v1.engines` explicitly and decides per tenant policy (deliver, tag, quarantine); a missing, failed or `unknown` bec result is never treated as clean                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Metrics                       | email-worker `scan_stage_triggers_total{engine}`, `scan_bec_results_total{verdict,deterministic_verdict}`, `scan_bec_rules_total{code}`, `scan_bec_inputs_total{input,state}`, `scan_bec_ai_total{layer,mode,status,effect}`, `bec_llm_*`, `bec_model_info`, `scan_bec_inconsistent_reads_total{read}`, `bec_history_*`; api `bec_feedback_total{kind}`; alert rules `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/bec-scanning.rules.yml`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## 25. Data loss prevention (step 4.5)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/13-dlp.md), [ADR-0036](adr/0036-dlp-engine-and-extraction.md) (engine, extraction, topology v7), [ADR-0037](adr/0037-dlp-policy-actions-and-lifecycle-binding.md) (policies, actions, exceptions, lifecycle binding), [ADR-0038](adr/0038-dlp-evidence-masking-and-aadhaar-gating.md) (masking, evidence, Aadhaar gating); runbook [dlp](../runbooks/dlp.md); [deployment](../deployment/dlp.md); [performance](../performance/dlp.md). IMPLEMENTED (policy 5.2 / quarantine 5.3 that apply the action are PLANNED; the dev pass-through holds meanwhile).

| Contract                | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Engine                  | Catalog entry `dlp` (stage `dlp`, queue `scan.dlp`, required for every tenant and direction, not disableable, **no `dependsOn`**: stage 1); `SCAN_ENGINES` default `rspamd,attachment,url,bec,dlp`. Ledger consumer `email-worker.scan.dlp` (user `eg-scanner`); production Deployment `email-worker-dlp` (`SCAN_CONSUMERS=dlp`), the only email-worker holding `DLP_EVIDENCE_KEY`. `outbound`/`internal` always scanned; `inbound` only with the tenant setting `inboundEnabled` (else `skipped`, `dlp.not_inbound`, with a `dlp_scan_results` row, action `allow`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Inputs                  | Writer reads, every one echo-checked (mismatch → attempt failure `inconsistent_read`, retryable): the message, the parse row, the attachment rows (`DLP_MAX_ATTACHMENTS + 1`), the tenant's active domains, the tenant data (policies, rules, unexpired exceptions, patterns, dictionaries, the compliance row; per-organization cache, TTL `DLP_TENANT_DATA_CACHE_TTL_MS` 30 s — a change applies within that time) and the stored parts in S3 (SHA-256 checked). Attachments are extracted in the isolated extractor child pool (`dlp-extract/1`) with the 4.2 walker and limits                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Result                  | Standard final row in `scan_engine_results` (`outcome` = the DLP verdict `clean` \| `suspicious` \| `unknown` \| `skipped` — **never `malicious`/`spam`**; reason codes ≤ 32: the action code first, then detector codes, then coverage codes; `engine_version` `dlp/1;dict=<8 hex>;data=<8 hex>;ext=dlp-extract/1`) plus one **`dlp_scan_results`** row per round (unique `(scan_id, received_at)`), written in the final row's transaction only when the final row was inserted: versions (`dlp-map/1`, settings, `pol/1;h=…`, `dlp-extract/1`), `direction`, `verdict`, `action`, `action_source`, `matched_policy_id`/`matched_rule_id` (internal), `coverage` and `coverage_detail` (`{ aadhaar, sources[] }`), `categories`, `findings` (≤ 64, **masked samples only**, ≤ 3 each), `policy` (`{ winner, matched, matchedTruncated, defaultPolicyMatched, exceptionsApplied }` with public ids), `finding_count`, `evidence_status`. A failure final writes no dlp row |
| Never clean by accident | Anything not extracted (encrypted, unsupported, image, no text layer, oversize, not stored, parse error, archive/resource limit, attachment cap), truncated text, a parse that may hide parts, a tenant pattern that could not run → a coverage code, `coverage = partial`, verdict at least `unknown`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Policy evaluation       | `evaluateDlpPolicy` (pure, deterministic, permutation-invariant): enabled policies of the direction, rules by (`policy.priority`, `policy.id`, `rule.priority`, `rule.id`), the strongest action of `enforce` matches (`allow` < `alert` < `require_approval` < `quarantine` < `block`), `monitor` matches recorded only; no tenant policy → the platform default `dlp-default/1` (`defaultPolicy` `alert` \| `allow`). Exceptions remove findings (sender scopes) or recipients (recipient scopes) for their targets and are recorded in `exceptionsApplied`; truncated recipient lists never apply recipient exceptions. The action is **message-level**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Incident                | `mail.message.dlp_incident.v1` (§6) in the dlp final's transaction for every action other than `allow`; none for a message deleted while its round is open; a coverage-only match (a rule selecting `coverageReasons` without a finding) writes an incident with an empty `categories` list, `coverage: partial` and the coverage codes in `detectorCodes` (the schema allows no category only with partial coverage)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Evidence                | When a reportable finding exists and evidence is enabled: ≤ 50 matches (≤ 5 per detector) with UTF-16 offsets and ≤ 48 code units of redacted context, sealed with a fresh data key (AES-256-GCM, AAD `eg-dlp-evidence/v1\0<org>\0<message>\0<scan>`), the data key wrapped with `DLP_EVIDENCE_KEY` (SecretBox format `v1.<kid>.…`; `DLP_EVIDENCE_KEY_PREVIOUS` opens during rotation); the object in S3 (§11), the row `dlp_evidence` (key, SHA-256, size, wrapped key, key id, match count, `expires_at` = `received_at` + retention ≤ 180 days, purge state). Card numbers first 6 + last 4 only, Aadhaar numbers, private keys and secrets masked, CVV/CVC never stored, also inside context windows                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Evidence access         | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans/{scanId}/dlp-evidence`: `dlp:evidence_read` + MFA + users only, 30/min per user; tenant-scoped writer read (another tenant → 404; purged/expired → 410); **audit row `dlp_evidence.read` committed before the object is fetched**; key prefix, size and SHA-256 checked; decrypted, validated with `dlpEvidenceDocumentSchema`; `Cache-Control: no-store`; a failure afterwards writes `dlp_evidence.read_failed` (reason only). Purges (`DELETE …/dlp-evidence`, `DELETE /api/v1/dlp/evidence`, `dlp:manage` + MFA): crypto-shred first (`wrapped_key = NULL`), then delete the object                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Read API (D20)          | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans`: the `dlp` engine entry (outcome, reason codes, no symbols, `durationMs` null) for `message:read`; its `dlp` section (versions, verdict, action, coverage detail, categories, masked findings, policy match with public ids, evidence status) **only for callers that also hold `dlp:read`**, validated with `dlpResultViewSchema` (an invalid row is `null`, never partial). Never values, context, offsets, dictionary terms or pattern text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Tenant APIs (D21)       | `/api/v1/dlp/{policies,exceptions,patterns,dictionaries,incidents,compliance}` (step AI-4 adds `GET /dlp/exceptions/{id}`: `dlp:read`, API keys accepted, replica, the list item's view, expired and disabled entries included), `POST /dlp/patterns/test`, evidence routes; `dlp:read` / `dlp:manage` / `dlp:evidence_read` / `dlp:compliance_manage`; organization from the authenticated scope only (another tenant's id → 404); version compare-and-set, caps under the organization row lock, audited with before-images. Aadhaar detection runs only with a recorded basis (`PUT /dlp/compliance/aadhaar`, MFA)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Settings API (D18)      | `/api/v1/scan-settings/dlp`: `inboundEnabled`, per-category thresholds 0.5–0.95, the disableable noisy detectors (`PI1`, `PI2`, `BA3`, `SC1`, `CF2`), `defaultPolicy`, `evidence.enabled` (≤ the platform cap), `evidence.retentionDays` (1–180); audited; workers re-validate at use                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Dev pass-through (D13)  | **Removed in step 5.2**: the DLP binding is part of the policy floor (§28). Until then, §18: `allow`/`alert` approved; `require_approval`/`quarantine`/`block`/missing row held (`dlp_action`). **Contract for 5.2/5.3:** at least as strict as the round's `action`, never weaker                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Metrics                 | email-worker `scan_dlp_results_total{verdict,action}`, `scan_dlp_findings_total{detector}`, `scan_dlp_coverage_total{reason}`, `dlp_extractions_total`, `dlp_extract_duration_seconds`, `dlp_detect_duration_seconds`, `dlp_extractor_kills_total`, `dlp_policy_evaluations_total`, `dlp_evidence_writes_total{result}`, `dlp_evidence_purged_total{reason}`, `dlp_evidence_expired_unpurged`, `scan_dlp_custom_invalid_total`, `scan_dlp_inconsistent_reads_total{read}`, `dlp_extractor_info`; api `dlp_evidence_reads_total{result}`, `dlp_pattern_tests_total{result}`; delivery-worker `delivery_dlp_holds_total{action}`, `delivery_dlp_alerts_approved_total`; alert rules `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/dlp-scanning.rules.yml`. No label carries a value                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## 26. Sandbox engine (step 4.6)

[Design](../plan/steps/14-sandbox.md), [ADR-0039](adr/0039-sandbox-security-boundary-and-backends.md) (the `sandbox-controller` boundary and the isolation backends), [ADR-0040](adr/0040-sandbox-engine-dispatch-results-artifacts.md) (asynchronous engine, dispatch, results, `sandbox-map/1`, sealed artifacts, topology v8), [threat model](../security/sandbox-threat-model.md). **Execution of unknown files is disabled** (`SANDBOX_BACKEND=disabled` by default; production accepts `disabled`/`static` only; `hardened-container` is a local/CI harness, not a VM boundary; `vm` refuses in 4.6). Status: WP1 IMPLEMENTED the contracts below, the migration, topology v8, the broker and MinIO users and the controller's backend interface; the controller (WP2), the email-worker engine (WP3), the local runner (WP4) and the api/stack integration (WP5) are PLANNED.

| Contract                                                        | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Engine                                                          | Catalog entry `sandbox` (stage `sandbox`, queue `scan.sandbox`, `requiredByDefault: false`, `tenantMayDisable: true`, `mode: 'asynchronous'`). The round never waits for it and `aggregateRound` ignores its finals (X10, WP3); its final row is written when every submission of the round is terminal, before or after the round completes, and never changes the round verdict or the lifecycle                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Vocabularies (`@email-gateway/security-contracts` `sandbox.ts`) | `SANDBOX_VERDICTS` (`clean`, `suspicious`, `malicious`, `unknown`, `error`, `timeout`; aggregation malicious > suspicious > error > timeout > unknown > clean), `SANDBOX_BACKENDS`, `SANDBOX_ISOLATION_CLASSES` (+ `SANDBOX_BACKEND_ISOLATION`), `SANDBOX_PROFILES`, `SANDBOX_NOT_EXECUTED_REASONS`, `SANDBOX_ERROR_CODES`, `SANDBOX_SKIP_REASONS`, `SANDBOX_LIMITS`, `SANDBOX_FINDING_SEVERITY` (every `static.*`, `behaviour.*`, `browser.*`, `limit.*`, `evasion.*` code with a fixed severity), `SANDBOX_REASON_CODES` (every code `sandbox-map/1` can emit; the metric label set), `ARTIFACT_KINDS` / `ARTIFACT_CONTENT_TYPES`, task limit bounds                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Observations                                                    | `sandboxObservationsSchema` (strict, bounded: ≤ 32 processes by basename and argv hash, ≤ 50 each DNS names / HTTP hosts (path and User-Agent as SHA-256) / TLS SNI / connections by canonical IP, ≤ 32 dropped files by hash, ≤ 32 findings with their fixed severity, ≤ 8 limits, browser counters). Same shape as the `task_completed.v1` `observations`; the event accepts any dotted finding code of that shape, the results consumer re-validates with the security-contracts enums                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Stored report                                                   | `sandboxReportViewSchema` / `buildSandboxReportView` (`sandbox_analyses.report`): findings by severity, deduplicated indicator hosts and IPs, process count and image basenames, dropped-file hashes, denied writes, network totals, limits, browser counters; no names, paths, URLs or content                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Settings (X17)                                                  | Platform `SANDBOX_TENANT_DEFAULT_ENABLED` (false), `SANDBOX_FILE_TYPES`, `SANDBOX_MAX_SAMPLE_BYTES` (≤ 64 MiB), `SANDBOX_BROWSER_ANALYSIS` (api and email-worker, `sandboxPlatformSettingsFromEnv`); tenant override (strict): `enabled` (needs the plan feature `attachment_sandbox`), `fileTypes` (non-empty subset), `maxSampleBytes` (1..platform), `browserAnalysis` (only when the platform allows it); `resolveSandboxSettings`, `sandboxSettingsBounds`, `sandboxSettingsVersion` (`sandbox-map/1;p=…;t=…`). Backend, limits and timeouts are platform-only service configuration                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Envelopes (`@email-gateway/sandbox`)                            | `sealSample` / `openSample` (`sandbox-sample/1`, `SANDBOX_SAMPLE_KEY` + `_PREVIOUS`, plaintext SHA-256 checked); `sealArtifact` (public key only) / `openArtifact` (PKCS#8 private key, tests and the `artifact:open` ops script only: no service holds it in 4.6); errors carry no detail                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Result MAC (X11)                                                | `resultMac = hex(HMAC-SHA256(HKDF(SANDBOX_RESULT_KEY, "eg-sandbox-result/v2 mac"), "eg-sandbox-result/v2\0" ‖ SHA-256(token) ‖ "\0" ‖ organizationId ‖ "\0" ‖ canonicalJson(payload without resultMac)))`: the whole result body and the envelope organization, keyed to the task's **one-time result token** (32 random bytes drawn at dispatch; only its SHA-256 is stored, in `sandbox_analyses.result_token_sha256`; the token travels only **sealed** in `task_requested.v1.resultToken` = `t1.<iv>.<tag>.<ct>`, AES-256-GCM under `HKDF(SANDBOX_RESULT_KEY, "eg-sandbox-result/v2 seal")`, AAD `eg-sandbox-result-token/v1\0<taskId>`). `verifyResultMac` is timing-safe. Only the email-worker and the controller hold the key; forging needs the key **and** the sealed task (delivered only through `scan.sandbox`) or the stored hash |
| Dedup (X13)                                                     | `analysisKey` = first 16 hex of SHA-256 over canonical `{ profile, limits, epoch: SANDBOX_ANALYSIS_EPOCH, map: 'sandbox-map/1' }`; `dedupeKey` = `<sha256>:<analysisKey>` (`sandbox_analyses.dedupe_key`, unique, nullable)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Verdict map (X14)                                               | `mapAnalysis` (`sandbox-map/1`, pure): F1 failed → `error`; F2 expired → `timeout`; N1 not executed → `unknown`; M1 a critical/high finding → `malicious`; S1 a medium finding or a raising limit → `suspicious`; U1 static only, U2 executed without VM isolation, U3 VM run incomplete/evasive → `unknown`; C1 only for a complete VM-isolated run without findings ≥ low → `clean` (unreachable in 4.6). Codes = union of the fired rules (analysis-level codes first, then severity, then name; ≤ 16); `aggregateSubmissions` → the round final (`skipped` when every submission was skipped)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Controller backends (X2)                                        | `apps/sandbox-controller/src/backends/backend.ts`: `SandboxBackend { name, isolation, version, executes, selfTest, supports, run, reap, close }`, `BackendObservation` (status, executed, reason / error code, observations, raw artifacts, cleanup report), `BackendRefusedError`. A backend never throws for a sample property; no backend produces a verdict                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Broker                                                          | Topology v8 (§4): `eg-sandbox` reads only `scan.sandbox` and publishes only `org.<id>.mail.sandbox.task_completed.v1` and its queue's `.org.`-anchored retry/dead-letter hops; `eg-scanner` consumes `scan.sandbox.submissions` and `scan.sandbox.results`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Storage                                                         | Restricted bucket `SANDBOX_S3_BUCKET` (versioning off, anonymous none, lifecycle on `samples/` and `artifacts/`); MinIO user `eg-sandbox-controller` with exactly `s3:GetObject` on `samples/*` and `s3:PutObject` on `artifacts/*` (verified: it cannot list the bucket, read the mail bucket, read `artifacts/` or write `samples/`); the email-worker uses its own S3 credentials. Keys §11                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Data                                                            | `sandbox_analyses`, `sandbox_artifacts` (unpartitioned), `sandbox_submissions` + `analysis_id`, `skip_reason`, `sandbox_reason_codes`, `settings_version`, status `skipped` ([schema-reference](../database/schema-reference.md#step-46-changes-sandbox-engine))                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

## 27. Risk scoring (step 5.1)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/15-risk-scoring.md), [ADR-0041](adr/0041-risk-scoring-model.md) (the model: signals, groups, failures, hard blocks, trust, levels, versioned profiles), [ADR-0042](adr/0042-risk-scoring-placement-and-policy-handoff.md) (placement, per-round rows, hand-off); runbook [risk-scoring](../runbooks/risk-scoring.md); [deployment](../deployment/risk-scoring.md); [calibration](../performance/risk-scoring-calibration.md) (synthetic data; **not** detection accuracy). IMPLEMENTED in step 5.1: scoring, storage and the api. Since step 5.2 the policy side of the binding below is IMPLEMENTED by the policy-engine (§28: the platform default `pol-default/1` follows the recommendation; the floor enforces hard blocks, failures, missing rows and the DLP binding); the quarantine (5.3) side is IMPLEMENTED (§29: entries record the decisive row's facts; a release re-validates against the latest completed round's risk revisions 0 and 1 and the current policy; hard blocks need a second person's approval).

| Contract                                          | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Library                                           | `@email-gateway/risk-scoring` (pure; zod, `@email-gateway/security-contracts`, `node:crypto` SHA-256 only). Versions `risk-score/1` (scorer), `risk-signals/1` (signal map), `risk-input/1` (input), platform profiles `RISK_PLATFORM_PROFILES` (`rsk-default/1`). `buildScoringInput(RoundRows, revision)` is the one input builder of the email-worker and the api dry-run; `scoreRisk(input, resolved)` never throws on a schema-valid input; `riskResultToRowJson` / `rowJsonToRiskView` are the one row ↔ result mapping (R11). `RSPAMD_REASON_CODES` in security-contracts is the Rspamd code list (R18)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Snapshot at round open                            | `ScanRepository.openRound` copies `risk_scoring_profiles.active_version_id` of the round's organization (writer, echo-checked) into `email_scans.risk_profile_version_id` (NULL = platform default). Completion and revision 1 use this snapshot, never the current pointer: a profile change applies to rounds opened afterwards only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `message_risk_scores` revision 0                  | Written in the round's completion transaction (`ScanRepository.complete`, also the deadline sweeper), under the message and `email_scans` row locks, **before** the lifecycle transition and the `mail.message.scanned.v1` outbox row, whether or not the transition applies; with it `email_scans.verdict`, `risk_score`, `scoring_version`. One row per `(scan_id, revision)` (unique `(scan_id, revision, received_at)`; `createMany … skipDuplicates`). Every consumer of `scanned.v1` for a round completed by a 5.1 email-worker finds the row (a missing row is a defect: alert `RiskScoreMissing`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Revision 1                                        | The same round inputs plus the non-skipped `sandbox` final, written exactly once by whichever of completion and the sandbox `writeRoundFinal` comes second (both under the `email_scans` row lock). Never changes `email_scans` columns, the round verdict, the lifecycle or delivery                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Row content                                       | `status` (`scored` \| `error`), `score` 0–1000, `level`, `verdict` (`MessageVerdict`), `recommended_action`, `hard_blocks`, `reason_codes` (≤ 32, `risk.*` and engine codes), `contributions`, `groups`, `engines`, `failures`, `floors`, `trust`, `evidence`, `explanation` (line codes and integers) and `explanation_text` (≤ 2048), `profile_source`, `profile_version_id` (internal, never in an api response), `profile_id_text` (`rsk-default/1` or `rsk-default/1+t:<version public id>#<n>`), `profile_sha256`, `input_sha256`, the three versions, `duration_us`. **Never message content**: no address, domain, URL, subject, file name or body text                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Never clean by accident                           | `timeout`/`error`/missing final or a missing bec/dlp/authentication child row → `engine_failed_<engine>`, `risk.engine.<engine>.<timeout\|error\|missing>` or `risk.input.<auth\|bec\|dlp>_row_missing`, level ≥ `medium`, action ≥ `quarantine` (a `scan_failed` round never recommends `deliver` or `tag`); `unknown` → level ≥ `low`, action ≥ `failures.unknownAction` (`tag`), verdict never `clean`; a scoring failure → the fail-safe **error row** (`status = error`, `high`, `unknown`, `quarantine`, `risk.scoring_error`); a profile that cannot be used → the platform default with `risk.profile.<invalid\|missing\|foreign>_fallback`                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Dev pass-through (R17)                            | **Removed in step 5.2** (the policy floor and platform default replace it, §28).
