Until then, §18: after the unchanged `scan_failed`, `malicious_verdict`, `bec_verdict` and `dlp_action` checks, with `DELIVERY_DEV_PASSTHROUGH_RISK_HOLD=true` (default): read the round's **revision-0** row (writer, ledger transaction, every key column echo-checked; mismatch → `delivery_inconsistent_reads_total{read="risk_score"}`, redelivered). `require_approval`/`quarantine`/`block`, a non-empty `hard_blocks` or `status = error` → not approved (`reason="risk_recommendation"`, `delivery_risk_holds_total{action}`); missing row → not approved (`reason="risk_missing"`); `deliver`/`tag` → approved. Never reads revision 1. `false` restores the 4.6 behaviour (diagnosis only)                                                                       |
| Read API                                          | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans` (`message:read`, replica, tenant-scoped): each round gains `risk: RiskScoreView[]` (revisions ascending; `[]` before 5.1 or while running), the R11 result plus `revision` and `createdAt`, with profile public ids only. `evidence.dlp` shows the round's DLP action, coverage and maximum severity, the same facts already visible to `message:read` on the `dlp` engine entry; never findings or samples                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Profile API                                       | `/api/v1/risk-scoring/platform-profile` and `/profile[/versions[/{versionId}]]` (`policy:read`, replica); drafts (`policy:manage`, writer, `validateOverrides` → `400 risk_profile_invalid` with violations by path, `RISK_PROFILE_MAX_VERSIONS` → `409 risk_profile_limit_reached`, compare-and-set `409 risk_profile_version_conflict`); publish / activate / reset (`policy:publish`; published versions immutable → `409 risk_profile_version_immutable`). Organization from the authenticated scope only; every change audited (`risk_profile.*`, ids, numbers, hashes and changed paths only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Dry-run                                           | `POST /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans/{scanId}/risk/dry-run` (`policy:manage` and `message:read`, replica): `{ profile: 'active' \| 'platform' \| { versionId } \| { overrides }, revision?: 0 \| 1 }` → `{ result, stored, reproducible }`. `active` = the round's own snapshot: reproduces the stored `input_sha256` and result (verified in the stack). **Writes nothing** (no row, no audit, no cache entry). `409 scan_round_not_complete` while running or not yet replicated; another tenant's message or scan → `404`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **Binding for policy 5.2**                        | **IMPLEMENTED in step 5.2** (§28; design P9). (ADR-0042 decision 7, design §4.) Read `message_risk_scores` revision 0 of the event's `scanId`; **a missing row is a hold**. The final action must be **at least as strict** as every fired hard block, the failure floor and the DLP binding of ADR-0037 (`allow → deliver`, `alert → tag`, `require_approval`, `quarantine`, `block`); it may be stricter than the recommendation; it may be weaker than a **level-only** recommendation only through an explicit, audited tenant policy rule, never for hard blocks or failures; an AI-only raise (`risk.ai.raise_only`) never justifies a hold; revision 1 may be read if present and may only make the decision stricter. Policy owns `email_messages.final_verdict`, `final_action`, `risk_score` (copied from the decisive row) and transitions E8–E13 |
| **Binding for quarantine 5.3** (IMPLEMENTED, §29) | A quarantine entry records the recommendation and the explanation of the decisive row; a release re-reads the latest round's score and re-validates; releasing a hard-blocked message requires the 5.3 approval path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Events and topology                               | Unchanged: no new event, no new queue; `scanned.v1` is unchanged (strict, ADR-0034 amendment)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Metrics                                           | email-worker `scan_risk_scores_total{revision,level,action,direction,status}`, `scan_risk_score{revision}`, `scan_risk_hard_blocks_total{indicator}`, `scan_risk_engine_failures_total{engine,status}`, `scan_risk_trust_total{status}`, `scan_risk_profile_fallback_total{reason}`, `scan_risk_errors_total{reason}`, `scan_risk_unmapped_codes_total{engine}`, `scan_risk_duplicates_total{revision}`, `scan_risk_skipped_total{revision,reason}`, `scan_risk_inconsistent_reads_total{read}`, `scan_risk_duration_seconds`; api `risk_dry_runs_total{result}`, `risk_profile_changes_total{operation}`; delivery-worker `delivery_risk_holds_total{action}` (removed in 5.2 with the pass-through). No tenant id in any label                                                                                                                             |

## 28. Policy engine (step 5.2, extended by step 5.3)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/16-policy-engine.md), [ADR-0043](adr/0043-policy-dsl-and-deterministic-evaluator.md) (the DSL, the evaluator, conflict resolution, the strictness floor, storage), [ADR-0044](adr/0044-policy-evaluation-placement-and-delivery-gating.md) (placement, delivery gating, removal of the development pass-through, topology v9), [ADR-0045](adr/0045-policy-action-semantics.md) (what each action does in 5.2); DSL reference [policy-dsl.md](policy-dsl.md); runbooks [policy-engine](../runbooks/policy-engine.md), [policy-notifications](../runbooks/policy-notifications.md); [deployment](../deployment/policy-engine.md). IMPLEMENTED in step 5.2. **Enforced:** the final disposition of every scanned, scan-failed and parse-failed round (ALLOW → approved and delivered; QUARANTINE → `quarantined`; REQUIRE_APPROVAL → `held_for_review`; REJECT → `rejected`, nothing delivered), the strictness floor, TAG/WARN headers and subject prefixes in a delivered policy copy, delivery of the round's URL-rewritten copy. **Requested** (another stage performs it): ARCHIVE (`email.archive`, consumer 8.1), ALERT and the outbound sender non-delivery notice (notification-worker, at least once). **Recorded only:** WARN body banners (not supported), TAG/WARN when headers are suppressed or the message is too large for a copy, URL_REWRITE when the round was not rewritten and the rule says `deliver`. **Not in 5.2:** the quarantine store, end-user quarantine, release re-validation and digests (5.3), archiving and journaling (8.1), per-recipient dispositions, enabling URL rewriting after the scan. **Step 5.3 ([17-quarantine.md](../plan/steps/17-quarantine.md) Q4, Q5, Q22, Q23; [ADR-0046](adr/0046-quarantine-store-and-per-recipient-decisions.md), [ADR-0049](adr/0049-policy-copy-body-edits-banners-and-url-rewrite.md); evaluator `policy-eval/2`)** adds per-recipient dispositions, WARN body banners and URL rewriting after the scan (rows below), and the quarantine store (§29); ADR-0049 supersedes the WARN, URL_REWRITE and QUARANTINE rows of ADR-0045. Recorded only since 5.3: a WARN banner on a signed/encrypted message, an uneditable part or a message over the size bound (`skipped_*`); URL_REWRITE when the message cannot be rewritten (signed, encrypted, too large, structure mismatch).

| Contract                                         | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DSL                                              | `@email-gateway/policy-dsl` (pure: zod, `re2js`, `tldts`; no I/O, clock or logger). Documents `policy-dsl/1`: JSON data from a closed catalog of fields, operators, quantifiers and nine actions (`ALLOW`, `REQUIRE_APPROVAL`, `QUARANTINE`, `REJECT`, `TAG`, `WARN`, `URL_REWRITE`, `ARCHIVE`, `ALERT`), strict objects, limits `POLICY_LIMITS`; regexes only through RE2, globs by a linear matcher, never `eval`/`new Function`/`vm`/SQL. `validatePolicyDefinition` returns every violation by JSON path. Exceptions (`unless`, policy `exceptions`) and the `when` of ALLOW/override rules see sender identity only through authenticated facts (`sender.authenticated.*`, `sender.trusted`, `sender.department`, `sender.user`); AI-derived fields cannot carry a hold. Three-valued logic: a missing fact is _unknown_, never matches and never exempts                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Evaluation                                       | `evaluatePolicy(input, compiledSnapshot)` is pure and deterministic (`inputSha256` of the canonical `policy-input/1`, `snapshotSha256` of the snapshot). Order `(policy priority, policy id, rule priority, rule id)`; tenant disposition = strictest matched; recommendation = the platform default `pol-default/1` (risk `recommended_action` max over revisions: `deliver`/`tag` → allow (+ platform TAG for `tag`), `require_approval`, `quarantine`, `block` → reject; D2: deterministic BEC `suspicious`/`malicious` → quarantine); a tenant disposition weaker than the recommendation applies only with a rule `override: true` **and** `allowRecommendationOverrides` **and** a level-only recommendation (else clamped and recorded); final = max(base, **floor**)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Strictness floor                                 | Never weakened by any rule, exception, override or setting: every non-level-only risk revision's mapped action (hard blocks, engine failures, unknown engines, missing child rows, error rows: `policy.floor.hard_block`, `…engine_failure`, `…unknown_engine`, `…risk_error`); the DLP binding (ADR-0037: `alert` → allow + mandatory TAG `dlp-alert`, `require_approval`, `quarantine`, `block` → reject; definite dlp final without its row → quarantine); no revision-0 risk row → quarantine (`policy.floor.risk_missing`); `scan_failed` → ≥ quarantine; `parse_failed` → quarantine (`policy.floor.not_scanned`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Snapshot and generation                          | `policy_settings.generation` (per organization, 0 without a row) is incremented in the api transaction of every change evaluation sees (publish with activation, activate/rollback, enable/disable/delete of an active policy, settings), under that row's lock. The engine reads it in the evaluation transaction (consistent read, echo-checked) and caches compiled snapshots per `(organization, generation)`: a committed change applies to the **next evaluation only**; a decided round is never re-evaluated by a change (verified in the stack)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Consumer                                         | policy-engine, `email.policy.evaluate` (§4), user `eg-policy-engine`, ledger `policy-engine.evaluate`, budget `POLICY_DB_TX_TIMEOUT_MS` (≥ 120 s). One writer transaction per event: E8 (`policy_decided`) / E9 (`scan_failed_fail_safe`) / E5 (`parse_failed_fail_safe`) with key `evt:<eventId>:policy_evaluated` and the event's round (not applied → ledger row only); P8 rows echo-checked (mismatch → retryable `InconsistentPolicyReadError`); evaluation; delivered object and policy copy; the `policy_execution_logs` row; `email_messages.final_verdict`, `final_action`, `risk_score`; the disposition edge E10–E13 (P18); outbox `mail.policy.decided.v1`, approvals (§18), `archive_requested.v1`, `notification_requested.v1`; `email_events` `policy.evaluated` (+ `delivery.approved`). A malformed row (`PolicyInputError`) is a defect: dead letter, the message stays held (`PolicyInputErrors`). Outage: messages stay `scanned`/`scan_failed`/`persisted`, never approved; the backlog is evaluated at restart under the then-current generation                                                                                                                                         |
| Evaluation row                                   | `policy_execution_logs` (partitioned monthly, 6 months `core`), one per `(message_id, evaluation_seq = lifecycle round, received_at)` (unique key; duplicates absorbed by the ledger, the lifecycle edge and the key; a rescan or release is a new round, earlier rows never change): trigger, direction, disposition, decision source (`tenant_rule` \| `platform_default` \| `floor`), decisive policy/version/rule, final action and verdict, risk score, override flag, `actions` (each with `applicability` and `status` ∈ `enforced` \| `requested` \| `recorded` \| `not_applicable`), matched rules, trace (≤ 200), floor, recommendation, override, clamps, reason codes, delivery block (headers, prefix, banner, URL rewrite — policy data only), delivered object (`original` \| `rewritten` \| `policy_copy` \| `none`) and copy key, explanation (fixed templates over codes, ids, integers), generation, snapshot and input hashes, versions, tzdata, duration. **Never message content**: no address, domain, URL, subject, file name or header value (asserted on every row of the stack suite)                                                                                               |
| Delivered object                                 | ALLOW without header edits: the round's `url_rewrites` copy with `status = rewritten` (echo-checked, prefix `org/<org>/rewrite/`) else the original EML. With TAG/WARN edits: the policy copy of that base (§11): existing `X-EG-Policy-*` headers removed, `X-EG-Policy-Tag: <labels>` / `X-EG-Policy-Warning: <level>; <text>` prepended, subject prefix inserted once, line endings and every other byte kept; larger than `POLICY_COPY_MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES` or a header block over `POLICY_COPY_MAX_HEADER_BYTES` → the base object is delivered and TAG/WARN are `recorded`. Outbound platform/floor tag headers are suppressed unless `tagging.platformTagOutbound` (recorded)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Management API                                   | api modules `policies` and `directory` (OpenAPI tags `policies`, `departments`): policies and versions (drafts editable with `expectedRowVersion` CAS, published versions immutable, publish/activate/rollback/enable/disable/delete), settings (`allowRecommendationOverrides`, `notices.senderNonDelivery`, `alerts.enabled`, `tagging.*`), departments and members; `policy:read` / `policy:manage` / `policy:publish`, `user:read` / `user:manage`; organization from `CurrentScope` only; writer transactions under the `policy_settings` lock; every change audited (`policy.*`, `department.*`; ids, numbers, hashes, changed paths — never definitions' values, notes, names or member addresses)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Read APIs                                        | `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans` (each round gains `policy`), `GET /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/policy-evaluations` (`message:read`), `GET /api/v1/policies/executions` (`policy:read`, bounded range, cursor). Replica, tenant-scoped, public ids only, no addresses                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Test and dry-run                                 | `POST /api/v1/policies/test` (synthetic facts, `policy:manage`) and `POST /api/v1/messages/{messageId}/scans/{scanId}/policy/dry-run` (`policy:manage` + `message:read`; targets `active`, `platform`, a version or an inline definition): **write nothing** (no row, audit, outbox or cache entry; verified with scoped checksums in the stack); `reproducible: true` when `target = active` reproduces the stored `inputSha256` and `snapshotSha256` (verified on a stored round with a department exception)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Notifications                                    | notification-worker, `email.notifications`, user `eg-notification`, ledger `notification-worker.policy`: `policy_alert` → the organization's active, verified users holding `policy:publish` (≤ `NOTIFICATION_MAX_RECIPIENTS`, Bcc); `sender_non_delivery` → the message's envelope sender only when its domain is an active domain of the organization. Fixed plain-text templates (organization, message public id, time, direction, disposition, decisive policy and rule, portal link) — never subject, body, file names, URLs or other recipients. Deterministic `Message-ID: <policy-notice.<eventId>@<from domain>>`; SMTP inside the handler before the ledger commit: **at least once** (a crash may send twice with the same Message-ID)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **Binding for 5.3 (quarantine)**                 | **IMPLEMENTED in 5.3 (§29), differently from the 5.2 wording:** the store is written by the policy-engine's evaluation transaction itself (not a consumer of `mail.policy.decided.v1`, which stays a firehose event; ADR-0046 decision 1); a release re-evaluates the latest completed round (risk revisions 0 and 1) against the current policy and needs a second person for floor reasons; a release delivers a release copy of the re-validated round's base object (§18). The step 3.3 api operator release answers `409 quarantine_workflow_required` (Q30)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Per-recipient decisions (5.3)                    | `policyTenantSettingsSchema.recipientSplit` (default `true`, part of the snapshot hash). When an enabled candidate rule of the direction (or a policy-wide exception) reads `recipient.address`/`.domain`/`.local_part`/`.department`/`.user`, the message has 2–200 recipients and the recipient list is complete, `evaluatePolicyPerRecipient` evaluates each recipient alone (`restrictToRecipient`; the floor and the recommendation are message-level, so every per-recipient result is at least the floor). The row keeps the message-level decision record plus `recipient_split` and `recipient_decisions` (`rcptSeq`, disposition, source, decisive rule ref, ≤ 8 reason codes); `final_action` = `partial` for a split. Lifecycle per Q5: any allowed recipient → E10 `policy_partial` (allowed recipients `pending` + approvals, held `quarantined` + entries, rejected `discarded`); none allowed → E11/E12 by the strictest held kind. One delivered copy per round: the non-terminal edits of every allowed recipient are merged (a recipient may see a TAG triggered by another recipient's rule); ARCHIVE/ALERT and the sender notice come from the message-level record (5.3 WP3 deviation 6) |
| WARN body banners (5.3)                          | The evaluator sets `delivery.bodyBanner = requested`; the policy-engine inserts a fixed-template banner (`[CAUTION]`/`[WARNING]`/`[DANGER]` + the rule's escaped text, never message data) at the top of the first inline `text/plain` part and right after `<body>` of the first `text/html` part of the delivered copy (`@email-gateway/mail-safety` `insertWarningBanner`, original transfer encoding kept, every other part byte-identical) and records the outcome: `applied`, or `skipped_signed`, `skipped_encrypted`, `skipped_too_large`, `skipped_no_text_part`, `skipped_part` (the object is delivered unchanged). WARN is `enforced` when a banner or header was written. A banner (like URL rewriting) invalidates an inbound DKIM body signature downstream                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| URL rewriting after the scan (5.3)               | A round whose scan-time rewrite was skipped only because the tenant setting is off (`disabled`, `direction`) is _policy-rewritable_: no `ifNotRewritten = quarantine` raise; the policy-engine rewrites the delivered copy itself with `rewriteEml` and click tokens signed with `URL_TOKEN_KEY_CURRENT` (**sign-only key custody widened to the policy-engine**, ADR-0049 amends ADR-0030; the click-service resolves them unchanged) and records `delivery.urlRewrite.policyAttempt`; a deterministic skip of that rewrite under `ifNotRewritten = quarantine` re-evaluates once with that skip reason (held); `no_urls` is satisfied; every other scan-time skip raises as in 5.2. Pipeline order: base → URL rewrite → banner → header edits; `POLICY_URL_REWRITE_ENABLED=false` keeps the 5.2 behaviour (recorded)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **Binding for 8.1 (archive)** — delivered in 8.1 | The archive-worker consumes `mail.message.archive_requested.v1` from `email.archive` (ledger `archive-worker.archive`) and reconciles from `policy_execution_logs` rows whose `actions` contain `ARCHIVE` with status `requested` (cursor `archive:policy-reconcile`); ARCHIVE stays `requested` in the evaluation row (the record is the archive's own fact, §31)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **Binding for 6.2 (notifications)**              | **IMPLEMENTED in 6.2 (§30):** `email.notifications` gained `notification.alert_requested.v1` and the notification-worker its security alert handler; the two policy kinds, their recipient rules and their privacy rules stayed; `policy_alert` skips users who opted out (`/notifications/preferences`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Metrics                                          | policy-engine `policy_evaluations_total{trigger,disposition,source,direction}`, `policy_evaluation_duration_seconds`, `policy_floor_raises_total{reason}`, `policy_overrides_total{result}`, `policy_actions_total{type,status}`, `policy_lifecycle_skipped_total{result}`, `policy_inconsistent_reads_total{read}`, `policy_input_errors_total`, `policy_snapshot_cache_total{result}`, `policy_snapshot_invalid_versions_total`, `policy_copy_total{result}`, `policy_risk_missing_total`; notification-worker `notification_policy_messages_total{kind,result}`, `notification_send_duration_seconds`; api `policy_changes_total{operation}`, `policy_dry_runs_total{kind,result}`; delivery-worker `delivery_approvals_total{source,object}` (`object="policy_copy"`). Alerts `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/policy-engine.rules.yml`. No tenant id in any label                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## 29. Quarantine (step 5.3)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/17-quarantine.md), [ADR-0046](adr/0046-quarantine-store-and-per-recipient-decisions.md) (the store, per-recipient decisions, message vs recipient state, lifecycle edges, topology v10), [ADR-0047](adr/0047-quarantine-release-revalidation-and-approvals.md) (release re-validation, the one approval model, separation of duties, self-service), [ADR-0048](adr/0048-quarantine-preview-and-download-controls.md) (sanitized preview, DLP masking, download grants), [ADR-0049](adr/0049-policy-copy-body-edits-banners-and-url-rewrite.md) (banners and URL rewriting after the scan, §28); runbook [quarantine](../runbooks/quarantine.md); [deployment](../deployment/quarantine.md); api reference [Quarantine](../api/README.md#quarantine-quarantine-tag-step-53). IMPLEMENTED in step 5.3. **Enforced:** per-recipient entries for every QUARANTINE and REQUIRE_APPROVAL disposition, written atomically with the lifecycle edge; manual quarantine; tenant-scoped search; a sanitized, DLP-masked preview; single-use download grants after re-authentication, never of malicious content; release with authoritative re-validation and a second person's approval for floor reasons; reject; delete refused under legal hold or retention; bulk limits; expiry that never releases; digests and admin alerts; an audit trail of every view and action. **Recorded only:** banner/rewrite skips (§28). **Not in 5.3:** admin-portal screens (7.x), webhooks and a preference center (6.2), legal-hold/retention management and archiving (8.1), post-delivery clawback (8.2), defanged-archive downloads, one-click release links.

| Contract                               | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Store                                  | `quarantine_messages`: one entry per `(message, lifecycle round, recipient)` (unique `(message_id, lifecycle_round, rcpt_seq)`), `kind` (`quarantine` \| `review`), `origin` (`policy` \| `manual` \| `adopted`), reason, primary engine, risk facts, hard blocks, decisive policy/rule, explanation codes and text (content-free), the ORIGINAL `eml_object_key`, `expires_at`, `row_version`. **No message content in MySQL**: `subject` is retired (never written; the api reads `email_messages.subject` at response time only for `quarantine:preview` holders and self routes, sanitized); no body, attachment bytes or URL in any column of the step. Created by the policy-engine's evaluation transaction (ADR-0046 decision 1) and by the sweeper's adoption of messages held before 5.3; by the api for manual quarantine                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| States                                 | `held`, `pending_approval`, `releasing` (a release is queued for the executor), `released`, `rejected`, `deleted`, `expired`, `superseded` (a later round re-decided the recipient); transitions are data (`ENTRY_TRANSITIONS`, `@email-gateway/quarantine`), anything else `409 quarantine_state_conflict`. Every transition: message lock (`lockLifecycle`) → entry `FOR UPDATE` (echo-checked) → approval request; CAS on `row_version`; one `quarantine_actions` row; the recipient status (`email_recipients`) and the message transition in the same writer transaction (one door, §21)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Per-recipient model                    | Message state = the delivery-phase view; recipient fate = `email_recipients.status` (`pending`, `quarantined`, `discarded`, …) + its entry (§21, §28). A rescan's evaluation supersedes every open entry of earlier rounds (pending requests `cancelled`) and returns `quarantined` recipients to `pending` before deciding again                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Search and views                       | `GET /api/v1/quarantine/entries` (replica, bounded `from`/`to` ≤ 62 days, filters, keyset cursor bound to the filter hash, ≤ 100 per page, no free text or subject search), `GET …/entries/{id}` (`allowedActions`, `contentAvailable`; writes a `view` action and audit), `…/history`, `…/summary`, `…/catalog`. Another tenant's entry → `404`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Preview                                | `GET …/entries/{id}/preview` (`quarantine:preview`, rate-limited, `no-store`): JSON text only (`preview/1`), built from the decoded parts of step 3.1 (bounded Range GET, never the raw EML), HTML converted to text (scripts, styles, frames, forms, SVG, objects dropped; images `[image]`; no URL is clickable, fetched or rewritten: links listed defanged `hxxp`/`[.]`), Unicode-sanitized, DLP values masked with the `@email-gateway/dlp` masks; audit row committed **before** the parts are read                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Downloads                              | Off by default (tenant `download.enabled` **and** `quarantine:download`, held only by organization/super admins). `POST …/download-grants` (user, MFA, password + TOTP re-authentication counted toward the login lockout) → a grant row and an HMAC token `qdl1.…` (key `QUARANTINE_DOWNLOAD_TOKEN_KEY`, **api only**, TTL 60 s); `GET …/downloads/{token}` binds organization and user, consumes the grant atomically (single use), re-runs the malware gate and streams `application/octet-stream` with `nosniff`, `no-store` and `CSP: sandbox`. Never served: hard blocks, malware/phishing verdicts, an attachment that is not `clean`, an EML with a non-clean attachment or a malicious URL. No presigned S3 URL is ever returned                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Release and re-validation              | `POST …/entries/{id}/release` (Idempotency-Key): replica pre-check (advisory) → `202 queued` (release log `pending`, entry `releasing`, outbox `mail.quarantine.release_requested.v1`) or `202 approval_requested` or `409 quarantine_rescan_required`. The **policy-engine release executor** (`email.quarantine`, ledger `policy-engine.quarantine`) re-validates authoritatively on writer facts: the latest completed scan round (risk revisions 0 and 1), the current published policy restricted to the entry's recipient, staleness (verdict age > 72 h with attachments/URLs, ClamAV signatures newer than the round by > 24 h). Approval reasons: `hard_block`, `engine_failure`, `risk_error`, `risk_missing`, `dlp_*`, `scan_failed`, `not_scanned`, `policy_reject`, `review`, `stale_verdict`, `no_scan_round`. Outcomes: release (copy, edge E14/E15/E28 or none, approvals §18, log `dispatched`), `approval_required` (new request), `rescan_required`, `refused`, `message_busy`; entries return to `held` when blocked. An executor outage leaves entries `releasing` (visible; alert `QuarantineReleaseExecutorStalled`) |
| Approvals                              | One table `quarantine_approval_requests` for `policy_review` (REQUIRE_APPROVAL, created by the policy-engine), `release_approval` (a release that needs approval) and `release_request` (a user's request). Approve/deny (`quarantine:approve`, user, MFA, Idempotency-Key): never the requester, never the entry's recipient (user id or verified address), never an end user or API key (`403 quarantine_approval_forbidden`); re-checked by the executor against the stored decision. Deny of `policy_review` rejects the entry; others return it to `held`. Requests expire (`release_*`: `approvals.requestTtlHours`; `policy_review`: the entry's expiry)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Self-service                           | Role `mailbox_user` (`quarantine:self` only). `/api/v1/quarantine/me/…` scoped to the caller's verified address (another address → `404`): list, view, preview (not for hard blocks or malware/phishing), self-release only within `endUser.selfRelease`, `selfReleaseReasons ⊆ {spam, bulk, policy}`, `selfReleaseMaxLevel ≤ medium`, no approval reason and fresh verdicts (`403 quarantine_self_release_not_allowed`), request-release, digest preferences. End users never approve, reject, delete, bulk-act or download                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Reject, delete, retention              | Reject → entry `rejected`, recipient `discarded`, E17/E18 when it was the last open entry and no recipient is pending/deferred/delivered; a sender notice (`sender_rejected`) only for outbound/internal entries, never inbound. Delete is refused while an active `archive_legal_holds` row matches (`409 quarantine_legal_hold`; criteria 5.3 cannot parse match everything) or retention applies (`409 quarantine_retention_active`); otherwise the entry is `deleted` and a `quarantine_object_purges` row lets the sweeper purge the EML, parts and copies once every entry of the message is terminal and not released and no recipient is pending/deferred/delivered (`shared`), and no ARCHIVE request waits (`archive_pending`, lifted by 8.1). The message rows stay                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Bulk                                   | `POST /api/v1/quarantine/bulk` (`release`, `reject`, `delete`, `approve`, `deny`): ≤ `QUARANTINE_BULK_MAX_ITEMS` (100) ids per request (`400 quarantine_bulk_limit`), ≤ `QUARANTINE_BULK_MAX_ITEMS_PER_MINUTE` (1 000) items per organization and minute (Redis fixed window, `429 quarantine_bulk_rate_limited` + `Retry-After`), per-principal request limit; each item through the single-item operation in its own transaction (same checks, SoD, audit), per-item results, the whole response stored under the Idempotency-Key                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Expiry and sweeper                     | policy-engine `quarantine` role, leader-elected (`GET_LOCK('eg:quarantine-sweeper')`), every `QUARANTINE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS`: entry expiry at `expires_at` (fixed at creation: hold days from `organization_settings.quarantine_retention_days` ≤ `QUARANTINE_MAX_HOLD_DAYS`, review days ≤ `QUARANTINE_MAX_REVIEW_DAYS`, both validated below the lifecycle deadlines) → `expired`, recipient `discarded`, E26 `quarantine_expired` when nothing is open — **never a release**; approval expiry; object purges (every object version); adoption of held messages without entries; digest and admin-alert scheduling; row retention (terminal entries after `QUARANTINE_ENTRY_RETENTION_DAYS`, ≥ 186)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Notifications                          | `mail.quarantine.notification_requested.v1` → `email.notifications` → notification-worker (ledger `notification-worker.policy`): `user_digest` (per user with held entries addressed to the verified address in the schedule window, at most one run per organization and hour; sender, sanitized subject ≤ 78 when `digest.includeSubject`, reason, expiry and a portal link that requires login — never body, attachments or URLs), `approval_requested`, `approval_decided`, `release_blocked`, `admin_alert` (rate-bounded), `sender_rejected`. Message-ID `<quarantine-notice.<eventId>@<from domain>>`, at least once. No one-click release links                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Audit                                  | Every user action writes an `audit_logs` row (ids, enums, counts, hashes, reason codes; the operator's free-text reason only in `quarantine_actions.reason`) and a `quarantine_actions` row per entry; system actions (creation, executor outcomes, expiry, adoption, supersede, purge) write `quarantine_actions` and `email_events`. Audit actions `quarantine.searched`, `.entry_viewed`, `.previewed`, `.preview_failed`, `.download_granted`, `.downloaded`, `.download_refused`, `.release_requested`, `.release_refused`, `.self_released`, `.rejected`, `.deleted`, `.delete_refused`, `.manual_quarantined`, `.approval_*`, `.bulk`, `.settings_updated`, `.preferences_updated`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Settings                               | `quarantine_settings.settings` (strict, every key optional; `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/quarantine/settings` with `expectedRowVersion`): `review.days`, `approvals.requestTtlHours`, `release.allowHardBlocks`, `endUser.*`, `digest.*`, `alerts.*`, `notices.senderOnReject`, `download.enabled` (default `false`). Hold days, digest on/off, schedule and time zone stay in `organization_settings`. Invalid stored settings → defaults + `quarantine_settings_invalid_total`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Metrics and alerts                     | policy-engine `quarantine_entries_created_total{origin,kind,reason,direction}`, `quarantine_releases_total{result,mode}`, `quarantine_release_duration_seconds`, `quarantine_expired_total{kind}`, `quarantine_purges_total{result}`, `quarantine_sweep_*`, gauges `quarantine_entries{state}`, `quarantine_pending_approvals`, `policy_recipient_split_total{result}`, `policy_body_banner_total{result}`, `policy_url_rewrite_total{result}`; api `quarantine_requests_total{operation,result}`, `quarantine_previews_total{result}`, `quarantine_downloads_total{stage,result}`, `quarantine_bulk_items_total{action,result}`, `quarantine_approval_decisions_total{decision}`, `quarantine_inconsistent_reads_total{read}`; notification-worker `notification_quarantine_messages_total{kind,result}`. Alerts `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/quarantine.rules.yml` (7). No tenant id, address or entry id in any label                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| **Binding for 6.1**                    | Done in 6.1: the quarantine routes kept their routes, v0 filter-bound cursors, `items` envelope and limits; they gained coded default errors, `X-Request-Id`, the global rate limits (all 11 keyed mutations in the `sensitive` class) and `x-eg-idempotency: required` (transactional marker). Field selection and ETags were not added (7.x may)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| **Binding for 6.2**                    | **IMPLEMENTED in 6.2 (§30):** webhooks `email.quarantined` (policy and manual) and `email.released`; the preference center `/notifications/preferences` reads and writes `digest_opt_out`, and quarantine `admin_alert` / `approval_requested` honour opt-outs; the 5.3 notification kinds and their privacy rules stay. Per-entry state changes (reject, delete, expiry) are not webhook events in 6.2                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **Binding for 7.x**                    | Portal screens consume the Q26 DTOs, `allowedActions` and `GET /catalog`; the preview is JSON text to be rendered as text, never as HTML                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **Binding for 8.1** — delivered in 8.1 | Legal-hold management (case-scoped holds, `archive-hold/1`, `legalHoldMatches` of `@email-gateway/archive`), archive retention no longer retains mail-bucket objects (`409 quarantine_retention_active` is no longer produced), quarantined mail journaled by tenant setting, `archive_pending` generalized (pending intent, ARCHIVE requested or journal-eligible without a record → purge deferred by 1 h) (§31, A23)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **Binding for 8.2**                    | Post-delivery clawback (manual quarantine of delivered mail) through mailbox connectors                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

## 30. Webhooks and notifications (step 6.2)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/19-webhooks-notifications.md), [ADR-0054](adr/0054-webhook-event-model.md) (event model, catalogue, envelope, filters, what is not an event), [ADR-0055](adr/0055-webhook-signing-and-delivery.md) (secrets, signatures, delivery guarantees, retries, dead letters, auto-disable, SSRF client, fairness, topology v11), [ADR-0056](adr/0056-security-alert-notifications.md) (security alert e-mails, preferences, throttling), [ADR-0057](adr/0057-idempotent-secret-bearing-routes-redacted-replay.md) (Idempotency-Key with redacted replay on the two secret routes). API guide: [docs/api/README.md](../api/README.md) (Webhooks, Notifications, verification snippet).

**Public HTTP contract (what a receiver sees).**

| Contract     | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Request      | `POST <subscription url>` (https; plain http only with `WEBHOOK_ALLOW_HTTP` in APP_ENV local/ci), body = the stored envelope, UTF-8 JSON ≤ 16 KiB, byte-identical on every attempt and replay                                                                                                                                              |
| Headers      | Exactly `Content-Type: application/json`, `User-Agent: EmailGateway-Webhooks/1`, `EG-Signature`, `EG-Event-Id`, `EG-Event-Type`, `EG-Delivery-Id` (new per replay), `EG-Delivery-Attempt` (1-based), `EG-Subscription-Id`, `Idempotency-Key` (= event id), plus `Content-Length`, `Host`, `Accept-Encoding: identity`, `Connection: close` |
| Signature    | `EG-Signature: t=<unix seconds>,v1=<hex HMAC-SHA256(secret, "<t>.<raw body>")>`; the key is the UTF-8 bytes of the whole `whsec_…` secret; a second `v1=` during a rotation overlap (current first). Verify before parsing, reject `\|now − t\| > 300 s`, compare in constant time, accept any `v1`                                        |
| Envelope     | `{ id, type, version: 1, occurredAt, organizationId, data }`; `organizationId` and every id in `data` are public ids (UUID); `id` is deterministic per source event, type and sub-key (dedupe on it)                                                                                                                                       |
| Answer       | 2xx within 10 s = delivered; anything else (including 3xx: redirects are never followed) is retried; `Retry-After` (seconds or HTTP date) honoured up to 1 h                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Guarantee    | At least once, unordered. Retries: 8 attempts over ≈ 1 h (30 s base, ×2, jitter 0.8–1.2, max 6 h, max age 24 h), then `dead` (listed, replayable). Auto-disable after 50 consecutive failures over ≥ 24 h                                                                                                                                  |
| Payload rule | Metadata only: no subject, body, attachment content or file name, local part, raw header other than `internetMessageId`, S3 key, URL from mail, secret or token (`assertWebhookPayloadSafe`; asserted on every received payload by the stack suite)                                                                                        |

**Catalogue v1** (`GET /api/v1/webhooks/event-types`; strict schemas in `@email-gateway/webhooks`).

| Type                      | Source (internal event, condition)                                                                                                                                                                              | Severity                                                          | `data` besides `message` / `domain`                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `email.received`          | `mail.message.received.v1`                                                                                                                                                                                      | info                                                              | —                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `email.scanned`           | `mail.message.scanned.v1`                                                                                                                                                                                       | by verdict (clean info … malicious high)                          | `scan` (round, kind, outcome, verdict, engines, reason codes)                                                                                                                                  |
| `email.threat.detected`   | `mail.policy.decided.v1` with a threat final verdict; `mail.attachment.sandbox_completed.v1` `malicious` (of a gateway message only from step API-4: a copy's sandbox verdict yields `mailbox.threat.detected`) | spam low, suspicious medium, malicious high                       | `detection` (stage scan/sandbox, verdict, risk score, reason codes, final action or attachment hash)                                                                                           |
| `email.policy.triggered`  | `mail.policy.decided.v1` with ≥ 1 matched tenant rule                                                                                                                                                           | medium for reject/quarantine/require_approval, else low           | `policy` (disposition, decisive and matched rules, action types, override, round)                                                                                                              |
| `email.quarantined`       | `decided.v1` with policy entries of the round; `state_changed.v1` `manual_quarantine`                                                                                                                           | medium                                                            | `quarantine` (origin, round, counts, reasons, entry ids, earliest expiry)                                                                                                                      |
| `email.released`          | `mail.delivery.approved.v1` `source = quarantine_release`                                                                                                                                                       | info                                                              | `release` (release id, mode, entry ids, recipient count, delivery seq)                                                                                                                         |
| `email.delivered`         | `mail.message.state_changed.v1` `delivered`                                                                                                                                                                     | info                                                              | `delivery` (complete/partial, round, recipient outcome counts)                                                                                                                                 |
| `email.rejected`          | `mail.message.state_changed.v1` `rejected` (policy, quarantine or delivery reason)                                                                                                                              | low                                                               | `rejection` (reason, stage, round)                                                                                                                                                             |
| `domain.verified`         | `domain.status.changed.v1` `verified`                                                                                                                                                                           | info                                                              | `previousStatus`, `verifiedAt`                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `domain.dns.failed`       | `domain.status.changed.v1` `failed`; `domain.dns.records_changed.v1` (`valid` → `invalid`/`missing`)                                                                                                            | verification medium; mx/spf/dkim/dmarc high; other records medium | `failure` (kind, reason code, records, checked at)                                                                                                                                             |
| `mailbox.threat.detected` | `mail.message.copy_detected.v1` with a threat verdict; `mail.attachment.sandbox_completed.v1` `malicious` of a copy message (step API-4, ADR-0106)                                                              | as `email.threat.detected`                                        | `detection` (stage scan/sandbox, verdict, risk score, reason codes, protection mode, direction, copy source, policy disposition, mailbox count, remediation counts, attachment, detection ids) |
| `mailbox.dlp.detected`    | `mail.message.copy_detected.v1` with a DLP action other than allow (step API-4, ADR-0106)                                                                                                                       | block/quarantine high, require_approval medium, alert low         | `dlp` (action, verdict, category codes, protection mode, direction, policy disposition, mailbox count, remediation counts, detection ids)                                                      |

`message` = `{ id, receivedAt, direction, domainId, internetMessageId, senderDomain, headerFromDomain, recipientCount, recipientDomains (≤ 20), sizeBytes, attachmentCount }`; `domain` = `{ id, name, status }`. `webhook.ping` is sent only by `POST /webhooks/{id}/test`.

| Contract              | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Registration          | 13 routes under `/api/v1/webhooks` (`webhook:read` / `webhook:manage`; API keys accepted): list (v1 cursor), create (Idempotency-Key, secret once, redacted replay), event types, get, patch (`expectedRowVersion`), delete (soft; Idempotency-Key), rotate-secret (overlap 0–168 h; Idempotency-Key, redacted replay), test ping, deliveries list (v1 cursor, bounded range) and detail (attempts + stored event), single and range replay (Idempotency-Key), replay status. Plan feature `webhooks` required to create, re-activate, test or replay (`403 webhooks_not_enabled`)                                                                                                                |
| URL rules             | Absolute https URL ≤ 2048, no userinfo or fragment, a port of `WEBHOOK_ALLOWED_PORTS`, host passing the SSRF guard's name and literal-address checks and not one of the platform's own hosts (`422 webhook_url_not_allowed` + `reason`); no DNS at registration — the worker checks every resolved address at every attempt (authoritative)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Fan-out               | webhook-worker `fanout` (queue `email.webhooks`, ledger `webhook-worker.fanout`): projection rules (ADR-0054), subscriptions of the **envelope's** organization only, `created_at ≤ occurredAt`, filters `directions`/`domainIds`/`minSeverity`/`verdicts`, index cached 5 s; one writer transaction for events, deliveries, alert windows and alert requests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Delivery              | webhook-worker `delivery`: fair claim per organization (at most 200 due organizations per tick through a rotating cursor; nothing sent for an organization that is not `active` or lacks the `webhooks` feature, its due rows `cancelled`; paused subscriptions' pending rows parked, `next_attempt_at = NULL`; Redis in-flight caps per organization 16 and subscription 4 as one expiring sorted-set member per reservation, 600 attempts per subscription per minute; fail closed), lease + CAS recording, guarded POST (every answer checked, IP literal dialed, no redirects, 3 s / 10 s, 4 KiB read), retries, dead letters, backlog bound 50 000, auto-disable, range replays, maintenance |
| Data                  | `webhook_subscriptions` (extended), `webhook_deliveries` (extended; monthly partitions, 2 months), new `webhook_events` and `webhook_delivery_attempts` (monthly, 2 months), `webhook_replays`, `notification_settings`, `notification_user_preferences`, `notification_alert_windows` ([data ownership](data-ownership.md))                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Security alerts       | `notification.alert_requested.v1` → notification-worker: `threat_detected` (`quarantine:approve` holders), `domain_dns_failed` (`domain:manage`), `webhook_disabled` (`webhook:manage`), plus the organization contact; one alert per organization, kind, scope and throttle window; fixed plain-text templates without message content                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Settings and opt-outs | `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/notifications/settings` (`organization:read` / `settings:manage`, CAS) and `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/notifications/preferences` (the user themself; opt-outs of the three new kinds, `policy_alert`, `quarantine_admin_alert`, `quarantine_approval_requested`; `quarantineDigestOptOut` = `quarantine_user_preferences.digest_opt_out`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
