| Metrics and alerts    | webhook-worker `webhook_fanout_events_total{result}`, `webhook_delivery_attempts_total{outcome}`, `webhook_deliveries_dead_total`, `webhook_due_backlog`, `webhook_oldest_due_age_seconds`, …; notification-worker `notification_alert_messages_total{kind,result}`, `notification_alerts_suppressed_total{kind,reason}`; rules `webhooks.rules.yml` (`WebhookFanoutBacklog`, `WebhookFanoutErrors`, `WebhookSsrfBlockedSpike`, `WebhookRateLimitStoreErrors`, `SecurityAlertNotificationFailures`, …); runbook [webhooks.md](../runbooks/webhooks.md)                                                                                                                                            |
| **Enforced**          | Tenant from the principal and the envelope only (another tenant's subscription, delivery, replay or domain id answers `404`); secrets encrypted with a key held by the api and the webhook-worker only, never logged, audited or stored by idempotency; signature on every request; metadata-only payloads (guard + stack assertion); SSRF guard on every attempt; at-least-once with deterministic ids; per-tenant fairness and caps; auto-disable with audit and alert                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| **Deferred**          | Mailbox connectors and remediation events (M365 / Google Workspace, 8.2); archive and journaling events (not produced by 8.1; [design §8](../plan/steps/23-journaling-archiving.md#8-not-in-scope-stated-plainly-and-where-it-goes)); per-recipient delivery events; events for SMTP-time refusals (no message row exists); URL click and DLP incident events; payload version 2, addresses in payloads, Standard-Webhooks headers, custom headers, mTLS client certificates, an egress proxy                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **Binding for 7.x**   | Portal screens: subscriptions (secret shown once with copy), delivery history with attempts and snippets (rendered as text, never HTML), replays, the event catalogue, notification settings and the personal preference center; the API is frontend-ready (OpenAPI tags `webhooks`, `notifications`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 31. Journaling and archiving (step 8.1)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/23-journaling-archiving.md), [ADR-0064](adr/0064-journal-capture-intents-and-archive-consumer.md) (capture intents, sources, capture points and categories, the `email.archive` consumer, topology v12, E25 retired), [ADR-0065](adr/0065-archive-storage-and-per-tenant-envelope-encryption.md) (archive layout, key hierarchy, `archive-object/1`), [ADR-0066](adr/0066-archive-immutability-s3-object-lock.md) (Object Lock), [ADR-0067](adr/0067-retention-legal-hold-and-disposal-consistency.md) (retention, holds, the disposal pipeline), [ADR-0068](adr/0068-archive-search-opensearch-and-authorization.md) (metadata search), [ADR-0069](adr/0069-ediscovery-cases-searches-and-exports.md) (eDiscovery); runbooks [archive](../runbooks/archive.md) and [eDiscovery](../runbooks/ediscovery.md); [deployment](../deployment/archive.md); api reference [Archive and eDiscovery](../api/README.md). IMPLEMENTED in step 8.1 (locally verified: MinIO Object Lock, a simulated replica bucket, OpenSearch 2.19.3 without its security plugin; AWS S3 Object Lock, CRR, KMS and Amazon OpenSearch fine-grained access control are documented, not verified).

**Capture (archive-worker `capture` role, writer only).**

| Contract            | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Capture intent      | One `archive_capture_intents` row per message in archive scope, unique `(message_id, received_at)`, upserted by any source (sources OR-ed): status `pending` → `captured_open` → `closed`, or `skipped` (`not_entitled`, `archiving_disabled`, `policy_archive_disabled`, `category_excluded`, `max_age`), `lost` (message or original gone), `failed` (deterministic: `integrity_mismatch`, `object_lock_unavailable`, `facts_invalid`; retry through the api)                                                                                                                                        |
| Source 1: journal   | Completeness sweep (leader `eg:archive-journal-sweep`, cursor `archive:journal-sweep` over `email_messages.id`, start `now`, safety lag `ARCHIVE_SWEEP_SAFETY_LAG_SECONDS` applied as a stop condition): every message row is visited once; entitled (`organization_features.archiving`), enabled organizations, `received_at ≥ journal_enabled_at`, direction (message or any recipient) and `scope` of `archive-settings/1`. An ineligible message gets no intent                                                                                                                                    |
| Source 2: queue     | `email.archive` consumer (ledger `archive-worker.archive`, user `eg-archive`, `processOnce`): `mail.message.archive_requested.v1` → writer re-read (organization, `received_at`, `emlS3Key` echo and `org/<org>/eml/` prefix) → intent with `source_policy`, `policy_rule_ref`. Unknown or foreign messages are acked and counted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Source 3: reconcile | Policy reconciler (leader `eg:archive-policy-reconcile`, cursor `archive:policy-reconcile` over `policy_execution_logs.id`, start `oldest`, safety lag ≥ the policy transaction budget): every evaluation whose `actions` hold `{ type: 'ARCHIVE', status: 'requested' }`. This is what makes the lossy queue safe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Capture points      | `decideCapture` of `@email-gateway/archive` on fresh writer facts: any recipient `delivered` → `delivered`; `quarantined` / `held_for_review` → `quarantined` (`include.quarantined` or a policy source, else wait); policy or quarantine `rejected` → `rejected`; `rejected/delivery_failed`, `expired` → `failed` (each `include.*` or a policy source, else `skipped category_excluded`); non-final states wait (1, 2, 5, 15, 30 min, then hourly). SMTP-time refusals have no message row and are never journaled                                                                                  |
| What is written     | Per attempt (`captureId` UUIDv7): the original EML streamed from the mail bucket (plaintext SHA-256 must equal `email_messages.eml_sha256`, else `failed integrity_mismatch`), the `archive-journal/1` report (envelope incl. BCC, header allow-list, attachment list, parse outcome, deliveries, bounded decoded text/html parts), one `archive-scan/1` snapshot per linked round and risk revision (the read-model rows of `packages/database/src/read-models/scan-read-model.ts`, never a rescan); then ONE transaction: `email_archives` row, intent `captured_open`, `archive_index_queue` upsert |
| Outcome refresh     | While `captured_open`: state, recipient statuses, verdict/action/risk/malicious, a source that joined the intent after the capture (policy or journal; a new source makes the intent due at once unless a replica holds its lease) and new rounds or revisions (≤ 16 links) are refreshed; the EML and journal objects never change; closes after the final +24 h check or `ARCHIVE_OUTCOME_WINDOW_DAYS`                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Idempotency         | Unique `(message_id, received_at)` on intents and records; leases (`lease_until` = `next_check_at`, CAS on every later write); attempt-scoped object keys, never overwritten; a lost race links the existing record; orphans (objects without a committed row) are deleted after 24 h by the lifecycle role                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Never               | Capture never sends mail, never changes `email_messages.status` (E25 retired, §21), never blocks SMTP, scanning, policy or delivery                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

**Storage, encryption, immutability.**

| Contract     | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| S3 keys      | §11 rows "Archived original", "Journal report, scan snapshot", "eDiscovery export"; every object `application/octet-stream`, no user metadata; every key read or written is checked to start with `org/<organizationId>/` of the row                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Keys         | Root key `ARCHIVE_ROOT_KEY` (worker and api only; `_PREVIOUS` during rotation, re-wrapped by the lifecycle role) → tenant key versions (`archive_encryption_keys`, wrapped, AAD with organization and version; rotation `POST /archive/keys/rotate`) → one DEK per record (`email_archives.wrapped_dek` only) or export → HKDF object keys per object (fresh salt)                                                                                           |
| Format       | `archive-object/1`: header `EGARC1` ‖ version ‖ kind (1 eml, 2 journal, 3 scan, 4 export) ‖ chunk size ‖ salt ‖ nonce prefix; AES-256-GCM chunks of `ARCHIVE_CHUNK_BYTES` with the header as AAD and a final flag; tampering, truncation, reordering and extension fail; constant-memory streaming                                                                                                                                                           |
| Object Lock  | The archive bucket is lock-enabled without a default rule. Per tenant `none` (default) \| `governance` \| `compliance`: every object of a locked tenant is uploaded with the mode and `retain_until`; retention only grows (`PutObjectRetention` before the row); `compliance → governance\|none` refused (`409 archive_immutability_downgrade_forbidden`); without Object Lock locked modes are refused and captures fail rather than store unlocked copies |
| Crypto-shred | Disposal sets `wrapped_dek = NULL` in its first transaction: every version and replica of the record's objects is undecryptable from that commit on                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Index (OpenSearch, archive-worker `index` role; api read-only).**

| Contract       | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Layout         | Template `<prefix>-v<n>` (`ARCHIVE_SEARCH_INDEX_PREFIX`, default `eg-archive`), yearly indices `<prefix>-v<n>-<yyyy>` by `received_at`, `dynamic: strict`, `_id` = message public id, routing = organization id                                                                                                                                                             |
| Tenant aliases | `<prefix>-t<organizationId>` (filter `term org_id`, routing) on every index of the active version; the api searches only through the alias derived from `TenantScope` and always adds the `org_id` term; no user input names an index                                                                                                                                       |
| Documents      | Metadata only (design §4.3): ids, times, direction, category, sources, status, sender/recipient addresses and domains, subject (text), Message-ID key, size, attachment count, names and SHA-256s, verdict, final action, risk, malicious, DLP matched, SPF/DKIM/DMARC, retain-until, legal hold, lock mode. Never a body, other header values, attachment content or a URL |
| Sync           | `archive_index_queue` rows in the same transaction as every record change; one ordered writer (`eg:archive-index-sync`) builds each document from the current row and sends one `_bulk`; the queue grows while OpenSearch is down (capture never waits). Per-day count reconciliation (`archive_index_checks`) and the rebuild CLI `dist/cli/reindex.js`                    |
| Freshness      | `GET /archive/messages` answers `X-EG-Index-Lag-Seconds`; eDiscovery searches report `indexComplete` / `incompleteReason` (`index_lag`, `unverified_days`, `mismatched_days`)                                                                                                                                                                                               |

**Retention, holds, disposal (archive-worker `lifecycle` role).**

| Contract  | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Retention | `archive-retention/1` policies by priority, else the settings' default policy; `retain_until = received_at + days` at capture; lazy extend-only recomputation at expiry (a policy increase protects existing records, a decrease applies to new captures only); capped by the plan limit `archive_retention_days` and `ARCHIVE_MAX_RETENTION_DAYS`                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Expiry    | Per record under `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, `decideExpiry`: effective retention later → extend (`policy_extended`); a matching active hold → extend by `ARCHIVE_HOLD_EXTENSION_DAYS`, `legal_hold = 1` (`held`); `expiry_action = review` → `expiry_review` (disposed only by `POST /archive/messages/{id}/dispose`); else disposal. One `archive.lifecycle_run` audit row per organization and run                                                                                                                          |
| Holds     | Created inside an eDiscovery case, immutable, `archive-hold/1` (custodians, from/to, directions; unparsable = match everything); evaluated authoritatively at expiry, at every disposal, at organization deletion and by the 5.3 quarantine delete/purge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Disposal  | One pipeline for expiry, reviewed disposal and the organization purge: `shredded` (decision transaction: `archive_disposals` row, record `disposing`, DEK nulled, index delete op) → `objects_deleted` (recorded versions, every other version under the record prefix, every version of the same keys in each `ARCHIVE_REPLICA_TARGETS` bucket) → `index_deleted` → `completed` (record row and, per the registry contract, the `message_identities` row deleted). `archive_disposals` is append-only and never dropped |
| Replica   | Content routes re-read the record status and DEK on the WRITER before decrypting, so replica lag never serves disposed content                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

**api surface (41 operations of step 8.1 plus the capture-issue read of step AI-5; full list in the [api reference](../api/README.md)).** `archive` tag: settings (`PUT` MFA, CAS), retention policies, key versions and rotation (MFA), status, capture issues, the capture issue of one message (`GET /archive/capture-issues/{messageId}`, step AI-5: any intent status in the list item's view; `journal:manage` or `archive:manage` as the list, API keys allowed, no plan gating, replica; an unknown message, one without an intent or another organization's → `404 archive_capture_issue_not_found`) and retry, disposal log, export signing keys, search (`archive:search`, `expensive`, masked, reveal-gated filters), detail, journal, security (frozen rounds through the live mapper), reveal (`message:reveal`, audited before the response), preview (`archive:retrieve`, MFA, never under a platform override, audited before decryption), download grants (MFA + re-authentication, single use, refused for malicious records) and `GET /archive/downloads/{token}`, reviewed disposal. `ediscovery` tag: legal holds (list, detail, release), cases (custodians, close), case holds, searches (worker-run, verified against MySQL), hits (masked) and review tags, exports (bounded ZIP of decrypted originals + `manifest.json` `archive-export-manifest/1` + Ed25519 `manifest.sig`, encrypted at rest, expiry `ARCHIVE_EXPORT_TTL_HOURS`) and export download grants. Tenant from the principal only; another organization's ids answer `404`; content routes refuse platform overrides (`403 archive_override_forbidden`).

**Metrics and alerts.** archive-worker: the 24 `archive_*` series of design A27 plus `archive_leader{job}` (no tenant, principal, address or id labels; scrape job `archive-worker`); api: `archive_search_requests_total{result}`, `archive_search_duration_seconds`, `archive_content_access_total{kind,result}`. Rules `infrastructure/monitoring/rules/archive.rules.yml` (13 rules with promtool tests): `ArchiveWorkerDown`, `ArchiveCaptureBacklog` (warn and page), `ArchiveCaptureLost`, `ArchiveCaptureFailures`, `ArchiveIntegrityFailure`, `ArchiveSweepStalled`, `ArchiveIndexLag`, `ArchiveReconcileMismatch`, `ArchiveDisposalStuck` (two), `ArchiveRootKeyRewrapPending`, `ArchiveExportFailures`.

**Not done in 8.1 (stated plainly):** content (body) search and attachment content indexing; per-attachment downloads; backfill of mail received before enablement; rescans linked after the outcome window; shortening retention of existing records; DEK re-wrap after a tenant key rotation; the AWS KMS root-key provider and SigV4 to OpenSearch (10.1); DR replica setup (10.2); public webhook events for archive actions; per-case access lists; journaling of SMTP-time refusals ([design §8](../plan/steps/23-journaling-archiving.md#8-not-in-scope-stated-plainly-and-where-it-goes)).

## 32. Account takeover and security incidents (step 8.4)

[Design](../plan/steps/24-account-takeover-core.md) (D1–D3, D14, D23, D24, §3, §4.1), [ADR-0070](adr/0070-ato-account-and-event-model.md) (accounts, events, `ato-worker`, topology v13), [ADR-0071](adr/0071-ato-detection-scoring-and-incidents.md), [ADR-0072](adr/0072-ato-response-safeguards-and-approvals.md), [ADR-0073](adr/0073-ato-enforcement-smtp-and-policy-facts.md) (SMTP enforcement, policy facts), [ADR-0074](adr/0074-ato-geolocation-and-ip-intelligence.md), [ADR-0075](adr/0075-security-incident-model-shared-ato-atp.md) (shared incident model). **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in step 8.4 (WP1–WP6, 2026-11-18) and verified locally: event schemas, aggregate types, topology v13, tables and shared vocabulary (WP1); producers in the api, smtp-adapter, email-worker and policy-engine (WP3); the libraries (WP2); the ato-worker (WP4); the api, notifications and webhooks (WP5); the portal and the stack suites `ato-smtp`, `ato-policy`, `ato-portal`, `ato-resilience` (WP6). Not verified: CI, a Kubernetes cluster apply, production volumes; identity-provider adapters are step 8.5. `ato_detection` is off for every organization until a platform operator enables it.

**Events** (strict zod schemas in `@email-gateway/event-contracts` `src/ato-events.ts`, registered in the registry; ids are decimal strings, times ISO 8601 UTC with milliseconds, every string bounded; no message content, password, token, SASL secret, raw username of a known account or recipient address):

| Event                           | Exchange         | Aggregate type (`aggregate_id`)                | Producer (8.4 WP)                                                                       | Bound to                                                   |
| ------------------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| `auth.login.recorded.v1`        | `email.events`   | `login` (`login_audit_logs.id`)                | api, same transaction as the login audit row (WP3)                                      | `ato.events`                                               |
| `auth.account.changed.v1`       | `email.events`   | `audit` (`audit_logs.id`)                      | api `AuditService.record` for `ATO_ACCOUNT_CHANGE_ACTIONS` (21 actions; WP3)            | `ato.events`                                               |
| `smtp.auth.recorded.v1`         | `email.events`   | `smtp_telemetry` (organization id)             | smtp-adapter telemetry flusher, ≤ 200 entries per batch (WP3)                           | `ato.events`                                               |
| `smtp.submission.recorded.v1`   | `email.events`   | `smtp_telemetry` (organization id)             | smtp-adapter telemetry flusher, ≤ 200 entries per batch (WP3)                           | `ato.events`                                               |
| `ato.account.risk.updated.v1`   | `email.security` | `ato_account` (`ato_accounts.id`)              | ATO store when the level changes (WP2/WP4)                                              | `email.webhooks`                                           |
| `security.incident.created.v1`  | `email.security` | `security_incident` (`security_incidents.id`)  | `IncidentStore`, every category (WP2)                                                   | `email.webhooks`                                           |
| `security.incident.updated.v1`  | `email.security` | `security_incident`                            | `IncidentStore` (status, severity, assignee, resolution; evidence at most every 15 min) | `email.webhooks`                                           |
| `ato.response.requested.v1`     | `email.security` | `ato_response` (`ato_response_actions.id`)     | ATO store, provider actions only (execute, rollback)                                    | `ato.responses`                                            |
| `ato.response.completed.v1`     | `email.security` | `ato_response`                                 | ATO store on every terminal state                                                       | `email.webhooks`                                           |
| `ato.restriction.changed.v1`    | `email.security` | `ato_restriction` (`ato_smtp_restrictions.id`) | ATO store (applied, lifted) and the expiry sweeper                                      | none: smtp-adapter outbox tail; `email.analytics` firehose |
| `ato.notification.requested.v1` | `email.security` | `ato_notification` (organization id)           | ATO store, first event of a throttle window                                             | `email.notifications`                                      |

Consumed existing event: `mail.message.scanned.v1` on `ato.mail` (outbound and internal rounds; inbound acked without work). Category, subject type and origin values of the incident events are **append-only** (ATP-4 adds values); consumers must ignore values they do not know. `ato.restriction.changed.v1` names a mailbox only by `mailboxSha256` (`atoMailboxSha256`, SHA-256 of the lower-cased address).

**Queues** (topology v13, [messaging-topology.md](messaging-topology.md#status)): `ato.events` (work, ledger `ato-worker.events`), `ato.mail` (work-lossy without dead-lettered heads, drop-head at 500 000, ledger `ato-worker.mail`, completeness from the catch-up over `email_messages` with the deterministic `atoMailKey(messageId, scanId)`), `ato.responses` (work, ledger `ato-worker.respond`, reconciled); broker user `eg-ato`; `email.webhooks` + 4 and `email.notifications` + 1 bindings.

**Shared vocabulary** (`@email-gateway/security-contracts`, IMPLEMENTED in WP1): `ATO_RISK_LEVELS`, `ATO_ACCOUNT_KINDS`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_CATEGORIES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_SUBJECT_TYPES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_LINK_TYPES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_ORIGINS`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_STATUSES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_ACTIVE_STATUSES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_SEVERITIES`, `SECURITY_INCIDENT_RESOLUTION_CODES`, `ATO_POLICY_FACTS_VERSION = 'ato-policy-facts/1'`, `atoSubjectHash(kind, subject)` (SHA-256 hex of `<mailbox|user|connector>:<subject>`; a mailbox subject is the lower-cased full address, a user or connector subject the decimal id) and `atoMailboxSha256(address)`. A unit test compares the lists with the Prisma enums; the equality with the event-contracts mirrors is asserted by `@email-gateway/security-incidents` (WP2).

**Account keys and storage.** One `ato_accounts` row per `(organization_id, kind, subject_hash)`; no directory data is copied and no new user, tenant or mailbox table exists (ADR-0070). Incidents live in the shared `security_incidents` / `security_incident_events` / `security_incident_links` tables with one active incident per category and subject (generated `active_marker` in the unique key); ATO detail in `ato_incident_details` (ADR-0075).

**Policy input: the optional `ato` block** (ADR-0073; implemented in WP3). `policy-input/1` gains an **optional** key `ato` = `{ version: 'ato-policy-facts/1', accountKnown, riskScore 0–100, riskLevel, incidentSeverity | null, incidentStatus | null }`, present only for outbound and internal rounds of an organization with `ato_detection` enabled and ATO mode not `off`; absent otherwise, so every stored `input_sha256` stays reproducible and `POLICY_INPUT_VERSION` stays `policy-input/1`. The policy-engine resolves it with two point reads in its evaluation transaction (`organization_features` + `ato_settings`; `ato_accounts` by `(organization_id, 'mailbox', atoSubjectHash('mailbox', sender))` + the active `security_incidents` row) and stores exactly the block it used in `policy_execution_logs.ato_facts` (column IMPLEMENTED in WP1). The four DSL fields `ato.risk_score`, `ato.risk_level`, `ato.incident_severity`, `ato.incident_status` are `strictOnly` (refused in exceptions and ALLOW/override rules); absent facts are unknown (three-valued logic).

**SMTP enforcement** (ADR-0073; implemented in WP3): active `ato_smtp_restrictions` are checked by the smtp-adapter at RCPT (`submission`, `relay`: `REJECT 5.7.1` or `DEFER_IF_PERMIT 4.7.1`) and in the SASL service (normal failure without verifying the secret), from a per-organization Redis cache invalidated by `ato.restriction.changed.v1`; lookup errors fail closed.

**Not done in WP1 (stated plainly):** no producer publishes these events, no consumer reads the three queues (`ato.mail` therefore fills with `scanned.v1` events up to its drop-head limit on a running stack), the `ato` policy block and fields, the stores, the API (`/api/v1/ato`, `/api/v1/incidents`), webhooks types, notifications and the portal are built by WP2–WP6.

### 32.1 Identity-provider adapters (step 8.5)

[Design](../plan/steps/26-ato-provider-adapters.md) (§0.2–§0.4, D2, D7–D11, §4.1–§4.3), [ADR-0082](adr/0082-ato-identity-provider-adapters-architecture.md)–[ADR-0086](adr/0086-ato-provider-simulators-and-contract-tests.md) (Proposed). **Status:** library contracts IMPLEMENTED in `@email-gateway/idp-adapters` (WP1–WP3, verified with unit tests, the in-process simulators and the 8.4 contract kit); the ato-worker runner, the api routes and the portal that use them are WP4–WP6 of step 8.5 (not built yet). No live provider tenant has been used.

| Contract                                    | Shape (binding)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ato-idp-binding/1`                         | `ato_provider_integrations.settings` (strict zod `idpBindingSettingsSchema`, defaults `DEFAULT_IDP_BINDING_SETTINGS`): `streams.<stream>.enabled`, `pollIntervalSeconds` 60–3600, `backfillDays` 0–30, `snapshot.sweepIntervalHours` 6–168 or `null`, `actions.<action>.enabled`, `mfaEnforcement.{groupId (Microsoft GUID), orgUnitPath (Google, ≤ 512, leading `/`)}`, `forwardingRemoval.mode` `disable`/`delete`; a companion stream only with its primary; changed with CAS on `row_version`      |
| `provider_state` v1                         | `ato_response_actions.provider_state` (`providerActionStateSchema`, ≤ 2 KiB, ids/booleans/enums/numbers only, never an address): `{ v: 1, phase: pre \| written \| rollback_pre \| rollback_written, pre: {…}, at, resultCode?, checks?, items?[≤ 12] }`; a repeated `execute` with `phase ≥ written` only confirms                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Cursor v1                                   | `ato_provider_streams.cursor`: base64url of strict JSON `{ v: 1, a (adapter version), seq (strictly monotonic), wm (watermark), ws?, we? (window), page? (Graph `$skiptoken`, Google `pageToken`, Office 365 `{ next?, blob? }`, sweep offset), sw? (sweep state), pq? (≤ 20 priority identity hashes) }`, ≤ 4 096 characters; another version or a malformed cursor ⇒ `cursor_invalid` and a restart at `now − overlap`                                                                               |
| `ProviderEvent.deviceLabel` (PX1)           | Optional `<browser> · <os>` from structured provider device data, ≤ 96 characters, refused when token-shaped (`providerEventSchema` of `@email-gateway/ato-detection`); used as the device label with `deviceIdHash` when no user agent is present                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Provider event normalization (8.4 contract) | OAuth scopes are 8.4 lower-case codes; Graph and Google scopes granting mail access map onto `mail_read`, `mail_send`, `full_access`, `offline_access` (Q1); Entra token risk detections carry `riskDetail = 'token_anomaly'` (Q2); provider ids that look token-shaped (≥ 40 base64-like characters) appear in `eventId` as `h` + 32 hex of their SHA-256; snapshot event ids are `snap:` + 32 hex                                                                                                    |
| `ato.notification.requested.v1` kind        | `provider_unhealthy` appended to `kind` (audience `tenant` only) with the optional `refs.integrationPublicId` (uuid; required exactly for that kind); opt-out preference kind `ato_provider_health` appended to `PREFERENCE_KINDS` (`@email-gateway/webhooks`). No new event type, binding or topology change (topology stays v14). The notification-worker dead-letters the kind (`notice_kind_not_supported`) until 8.5 WP4 adds its template and recipients                                         |
| 8.2 health check                            | `ato.identity_provider` (`@email-gateway/provider-integrations` `health/ato.ts`, provider `any`, periodic, severity **warning**): reads the connection's non-disabled `ato_provider_integrations` by `connector_ref`; no integration / paused by a user / never probed ⇒ `skipped`, `healthy` ⇒ `pass`, `degraded` > 1 h ⇒ `warn`, `unhealthy` ⇒ `fail` with evidence `{ health, integrationId, checkedAt, failingStreams ≤ 7, missingPermissions ≤ 10 }`; run by the integration-worker `health` role |
| 8.2 permission profiles                     | `m365.ato_telemetry`, `m365.ato_response`, `m365.ato_response_extended`, `google.ato_telemetry`, `google.ato_response`, `google.ato_response_extended` (`profiles/ato.ts`, purposes `ato_telemetry` / `ato_response`, platform keys `PROVIDER_M365_ATO_*_APP_{CLIENT_ID,CLIENT_SECRET,CERT_PATH,CERT_THUMBPRINT}` and `PROVIDER_GOOGLE_ATO_*_SA_{CLIENT_ID,KEY_PATH}`); 8.2's generic per-grant checks skip them (R2)                                                                                  |

## 33. Provider integrations (step 8.2)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/25-m365-google-connectors.md), [ADR-0076](adr/0076-provider-integration-framework.md) (connections, grants per permission profile, read-only mail-flow access), [ADR-0077](adr/0077-provider-credential-custody-and-encryption.md) (credential custody), [ADR-0078](adr/0078-provider-relay-tenant-attribution.md) (provider relay, tenant proof), [ADR-0079](adr/0079-connector-validation-probes-and-health.md) (validation, probes, health, topology v14), [ADR-0080](adr/0080-setup-wizard-explicit-authorization-and-guides.md) (wizard, explicit authorization, setup guides), [ADR-0081](adr/0081-provider-mail-flow-loop-prevention-and-failover.md) (loop prevention, failover). (§32 is reserved for step 8.4, built in parallel.)

| Contract                                   | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `integration.connection.health_changed.v1` | Exchange `email.events`, aggregate `provider_connection` (`aggregate_id` = the decimal `provider_connections.id`), producer the integration-worker's health role through `message_outbox`, only on a health transition, in the transaction that stores the `provider_health_events` row. Strict payload: `connectionId` (public UUID), `provider` (`microsoft_365` / `google_workspace`), `previousStatus`, `status` (`unknown` / `healthy` / `warning` / `critical`; previous ≠ status), `changedAt`, `failingChecks` (≤ 10 unique codes `^[a-z0-9_.]{1,64}$`). Bound to `email.webhooks` (topology v14); the webhook-worker projects it to the public webhook `integration.health_changed` and the alert `integration_unhealthy`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `provider-relay-proof/1`                   | `provider_connections.relay_proof` (NULL = the provider default). Microsoft 365 default `{ tenantHeader: 'required', dkimAligned: 'required' }`: every `X-MS-Exchange-CrossTenant-Id` value present (at least one) equals the connection's verified tenant id, and an aligned DKIM pass by a linked domain. Google Workspace default `{ dkimAligned: 'required', dkimMaxAgeSeconds: 3600, dkimReplayMax: 5 }`: an aligned pass with `t=` within `[now − 3600 s, now + 300 s]`, replayed at most 5 times within `PROVIDER_RELAY_DKIM_REPLAY_WINDOW_SECONDS`. Aligned pass: result `pass`, `d=` equal to or a parent of the header-From domain, `d` an active verified domain of the organization linked to the connection, no `l=`, `h=` covers `from`, `rsa-sha256` or `ed25519-sha256`, key ≥ 1024 bits. Overrides: `tenantHeader: 'off'` only for Microsoft 365 with `dkimAligned: 'required'` **and** `dkimMaxAgeSeconds` and `dkimReplayMax` (DKIM is then the only proof, with the replay bounds of Google); `dkimAligned` never `off` for Google; at least one rule always required; a change is an explicit-authorization apply (ADR-0080). Outcomes: `pass` → normal ingestion; `fail` → `550 5.7.26` for every recipient (post-queue case `provider_proof_failed`, ADR-0019 d5 as amended); `temperror` → `451 4.3.0` |
| `provider-validation/1`                    | `provider_validation_runs.results`: ≤ 64 entries, ≤ 64 KiB, each `{ code, subject, status: pass \| warn \| fail \| skipped \| error, severity: info \| warning \| critical, evidence (≤ 2 KiB, non-secret), remediation (setup-guide anchor) }`; `summary` = `{ pass, warn, fail, skipped, error }` counts. Attested customer-side settings are labelled as attestations, never as observations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `setup-guide/1`                            | `GET /provider-connections/{id}/setup-guide` (JSON; `?format=markdown` renders the same guide as `text/markdown`), generated by the pure `generateSetupGuide` of `@email-gateway/provider-integrations` from the connection's own values (linked domains and provider MX hosts, `GATEWAY_MX_HOSTS`, `GATEWAY_EGRESS_NETWORKS`, `GATEWAY_RELAY_HOSTS`, `GATEWAY_RELAY_TLS_NAME`, `GATEWAY_SPF_INCLUDE`, the probe address of a waiting run, the platform client id and profile permissions, the route token — masked `••••` + last 4 without `integration:manage` — and the attestation state). Customer-side steps carry attestation codes; no secret-shaped value ever appears in a guide                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `provider-attestations/1`                  | `provider_connections.attestations`: ≤ 32 entries `{ code, userId (public id), at }`, recorded by `POST /provider-connections/{id}/attestations` (audited `integration.attestation_recorded`). Statements of the customer's administrator, not proofs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Relay marker `sa:prelay:{queueId}` v1      | Redis, written by the smtp-adapter policy service at END-OF-MESSAGE on a provider-relay session before answering `DUNNO`: `{ v: 1, organizationId, connectionPublicId, provider, tenantId, outboundMode, tlsProtocol, tlsCipher, clientAddress }`, TTL `PROVIDER_RELAY_MARKER_TTL_SECONDS` (default 432000) but never below Postfix's `maximal_queue_lifetime` (5 d); not deleted after ingestion (a queue file may be delivered in several LMTP transactions); read by LMTP ingestion (queue id from XFORWARD IDENT). Redis error at write → `DEFER_IF_PERMIT 4.3.5`, at read → `451 4.3.0` (fail closed). A provider-egress client that resolves to no connector at END-OF-MESSAGE → `DEFER_IF_PERMIT 4.7.1` (never relayed without a marker). Replay counters: `sa:prelay:dkim:{sha256(b=)}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Route token header `X-EG-Route-Token`      | `base32(HMAC-SHA256(PROVIDER_ROUTE_TOKEN_KEY, "route-token:v1:" + connectionPublicId + ":" + version))[0..32]` (`deriveRouteToken`, `@email-gateway/mail-routing`). Prepended by the delivery-worker after `X-Gateway-Trace` only to `internal` deliveries to a domain linked to a Microsoft 365 connection with internal routing on and outbound `active`; a database error retries the reinjection, never sends without it. The guide's transport rules except it (rule A) and remove it before mailboxes (rule B). A routing marker, not a security credential (the proof is the control); masked for readers without `integration:manage`; rotation through `route_token_pending_version` after an attestation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 34. Journal intake and the Journaling / Archive split (step 8.6)

[Design and verification record](../plan/steps/27-journal-intake-and-archive-split.md), [ADR-0087](adr/0087-journal-intake-path.md) (journal-only intake path, state `journaled`), [ADR-0088](adr/0088-archive-cross-source-dedupe.md) (cross-source dedupe), [ADR-0089](adr/0089-journaling-archive-permission-split.md) (permission split, `compliance_officer`, grant-preserving migration), [ADR-0090](adr/0090-archive-only-service-profile.md) (archive-only plan and `serviceProfile`). DESIGNED; build in progress (step 8.6). The data model, lifecycle, event contracts, catalog and configuration keys below are built (WP1); the mail path, workers, api and portal are built by WP2–WP6. **No topology change** (stays v14).

**SMTP and Redis (smtp-adapter, inbound Postfix, Rspamd).**

| Contract                                              | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Intake domain and addresses                           | `JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN` (platform-owned; empty = intake off). Address `journal-<token>@<domain>`, `token` = first 26 characters of lower-case RFC 4648 base32 (no padding) of `HMAC-SHA256(JOURNAL_INTAKE_ADDRESS_SECRET, "eg-journal-intake-address/v1\0<sourcePublicId>\0<addressVersion>")`. The database stores `address_hash` = SHA-256 of the lower-case local part (and `previous_address_hash` until `previous_valid_until` after a rotation). Tenant domains equal to or under the intake domain are refused (`422 domain_reserved`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Header token `X-EG-Journal-Token`                     | Google Workspace sources only: 26-character base32 of `HMAC-SHA256(secret, "eg-journal-intake-header/v1\0<sourcePublicId>\0<addressVersion>")`, added by the customer's routing rule; stored as `header_token_hash` (SHA-256); every occurrence is removed from the stored EML before upload.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Postfix                                               | When `POSTFIX_JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN` is set: `relay_domains = <intake domain>, socketmap:…:relay_domain`, `transport_maps = inline:{ <intake domain> = lmtp:inet:${smtp_adapter_host}:10053 }`; otherwise unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| RCPT policy (`policy_context=inbound`, intake domain) | In order: (1) local part `journal-<26 base32>` and a non-`disabled` source by current or unexpired previous address hash, else `550 5.1.1` (`UNKNOWN_USER`); (2) organization active, features `archiving` + `journal_intake`, archiving enabled, source not `paused`, else `450 4.7.1 Journal intake temporarily unavailable` (`INTAKE_UNAVAILABLE`); (3) network: provider kinds — client in the bound connection's provider egress set (unavailable index `DEFER_IF_PERMIT 4.3.5`), connection bound and not disabled; on-premises kinds — client in the source's networks and in no provider egress set; client certificate fingerprint when configured; else `550 5.7.1 Journal intake sender not authorized` (`INTAKE_SENDER_NOT_AUTHORIZED`); (4) `requireTls` without TLS → `MUST_STARTTLS`; (5) intake-only transaction, else `452 4.5.3 Error: too many recipients` (`INTAKE_ONE_DESTINATION`); (6) rate `451 4.7.1` (`RATE_LIMITED`); (7) declared size above `min(source, plan, platform)` → `552 5.3.4` (`TOO_BIG_FOR_RECIPIENT`); (8) `DUNNO`. Sources are read through a cache (`JOURNAL_INTAKE_SOURCE_CACHE_SECONDS`, negative entries too). |
| Rspamd                                                | Settings rule `journal_intake`, selected by recipient domain = `RSPAMD_JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN`, applies no action (`reject`, `add header`, `rewrite subject`, `greylist`, `soft reject` = null); symbols are still computed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Journal LMTP `:10053` (`LMTP_JOURNAL_PORT`)           | Answers only `250` or `451` (never `5xx`). Recipient acceptance re-checks the source on the writer (every failure `451`); one intake recipient per transaction. Proof `fail` → nothing stored, `250 2.0.0 Ok: dropped` (accept-and-drop); `temperror` → `451`; duplicate ingest key → `250` duplicate. Ingest key `v2:` over `{mtaId}\|{queueId}\|journal\|{sha256}\|{recipientSet}`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Redis keys                                            | `sa:jintake:inst:<instance>` (transaction kind `src:<sourceId>` or `tenant`, TTL 3600 s), `sa:jintake:cnt:<sourceId>:<yyyymmdd>` (hash of the D2.8 counters, TTL 3 days, flushed every `JOURNAL_INTAKE_COUNTER_FLUSH_SECONDS` into `journal_intake_counters`), `sa:jintake:cnt:dirty` (set of counter hashes waiting for the flush), `sa:jintake:q:<queueId>` (end-of-message marker with the session's TLS and client-certificate facts for the LMTP stage, TTL 432 000 s), `sa:jintake:src:<addressHash>` (source cache JSON). Rate keys `sa:rl:jintake_src:<sourceId>:<minute>` (`JOURNAL_INTAKE_RATE_PER_SOURCE_PER_MINUTE`) and `sa:rl:jintake_org:<orgId>:<minute>` (plan `journal_intake_messages_per_minute`) through `FixedWindowLimiter`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Data (migration `20261120053633_journal_intake`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md)).**

| Contract                                        | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `email_messages.ingest_source`                  | `gateway` (default; every message before 8.6) or `journal_intake`. Every gateway-facing read filters `ingest_source = 'gateway'`; the report covering index is `email_messages_org_received_dir_verdict_src_idx (organization_id, received_at, direction, final_verdict, final_action, domain_id, ingest_source)` (replaces `…_verdict_idx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Lifecycle                                       | State `journaled` (appended to `MessageStatus`; final, not expirable, never a source of E19, E26, E28, E29). Edges, role `scanner`: E30 `persisted → journaled` (`intake_unscanned`, `intake_parse_failed`), E31 `scanned → journaled` (`intake_scan_complete`), E32 `scan_failed → journaled` (`intake_scan_failed`). The transition service refuses any edge into `journaled` for a message whose `ingest_source` is not `journal_intake` (`invalid/ingest_source_not_allowed`). `RecipientStatus` gains `journaled` (the recipients of an intake message). |
| `journal_intake_sources`                        | One row per customer sender configuration: kind (`microsoft_365_journal`, `google_workspace_copy`, `exchange_onprem_journal`, `smtp_bcc`), status (`pending_verification`, `active`, `paused`, `disabled` — terminal), bound `provider_connection_id` (provider kinds), `networks` (`intake-networks/1`), hashes only, health (`unknown`, `healthy`, `silent`, `failing`, `paused`), `silence_alert_minutes` (default 240, NULL = off), CAS `row_version`.                                                                                                    |
| `journal_intake_messages`                       | Partitioned monthly by `received_at` (6 months, core); one row per intake message: `kind`, `format` (`journal_report`, `copy`, `journal_report_unparsed`), `envelope` (`journal-envelope/1`), `proof` (`journal-intake-proof/1`), outer report SHA-256 and size, `scan_requested`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `journal_intake_counters`                       | Daily counters per source (`accepted`, `duplicates`, `refused_address`, `refused_network`, `refused_tls`, `refused_size`, `refused_rate`, `refused_unavailable`, `dropped_proof`, `unwrap_failed`, `bytes`), upserted additively by `(source_id, day)`; pruned after 400 days.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `archive_dedupe_keys`, `archive_record_sources` | Dedupe keys `(organization_id, message_id_hash, header_fingerprint)` + `body_sha256` per record or variant (transient, `ARCHIVE_DEDUPE_WINDOW_HOURS`); merged copies of a record (partitioned monthly by `archive_received_at`, never dropped; deleted with the record's disposal). `email_archives` gains `source_gateway`, `source_journal_intake`, `intake_source_id`, `merged_count`; `archive_capture_intents` gains `archive_received_at`, `merged`. The registry purge keeps `message_identities` rows referenced by `archive_record_sources`.         |

**Documents (strict zod, `@email-gateway/archive`).**

| Contract                                    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `journal-envelope/1`                        | `{ v: 1, sender: string\|null, onBehalfOf: string\|null, messageId: string\|null, recipients: [{ address, field: 'to'\|'cc'\|'bcc'\|'recipient'\|'envelope'\|'header', expandedFrom?, forwardedFrom? }] (≤ 10 000), truncated: boolean, envelopeMismatch: boolean }`.                                                                                                               |
| `journal-intake-proof/1`                    | `{ v: 1, network: 'provider_egress'\|'source_networks', tls: boolean, clientCert: 'match'\|'n/a', tenantHeader: 'match'\|'mismatch'\|'absent'\|'n/a', headerToken: 'match'\|'previous'\|'mismatch'\|'absent'\|'n/a', dkim: { result: <ProviderDkimResult>\|'n/a', domains: string[≤5] }, result: 'pass' }` (only passing proofs are stored).                                        |
| `intake-networks/1`                         | `{ v: 1, cidrs: string[1..32] }`; never overlapping a provider egress range; prefixes no shorter than /16 (IPv4) or /32 (IPv6); private ranges only in local/ci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `archive-dedupe/1`, `archive-fingerprint/1` | `messageIdHash = sha256("archive-dedupe/1\0<organizationId>\0<Message-ID without angle brackets and surrounding whitespace>")`; fingerprint = SHA-256 of the canonical JSON `{ date: epoch seconds, from: lower addr-spec, to: sorted unique lower To+Cc addr-specs, attachments: sorted attachment SHA-256 }` (body excluded). No Message-ID, unparsable Date or From → no dedupe. |
| Additive keys of 8.1 documents              | `archive-settings/1` + `intake: { scanEnabled?: boolean }`; `archive-journal/1` + optional `intake` block and `mergedFrom`; `archive-parties/1` recipients + optional `field`, `sources`; record `objects` + optional `merged: [{ messageId, receivedAt, source, journal, eml\|null }]` (≤ 8). Readers accept every 8.1 document without them.                                      |
| `journal-intake-guide/1`                    | `{ schema, kind, source: { id, displayName, kind }, address (masked unless `journal:manage`), headerToken?, sections: SetupGuideSection[], attestations, generatedAt }`; JSON or `text/markdown`.                                                                                                                                                                                   |

**Events (no topology change, v14).**

| Contract                          | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `mail.message.received.v1`        | Gains the optional `ingestSource: 'gateway' \| 'journal_intake'` (absent = `gateway`; the schema stays non-strict, so consumers built before 8.6 ignore it). The journal LMTP writes `journal_intake`; the email-worker parses intake messages and never writes `parsed.v1`-to-policy, `parse_failed.v1` or `scanned.v1` for them; the webhook-worker projects nothing for them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `mail.message.state_changed.v1`   | `state` / `previousState` may be `journaled` (`MESSAGE_STATES` gains it). Webhooks project nothing for a transition to `journaled`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `notification.alert_requested.v1` | New kind `journal_intake_silent` (severity `medium` for silent, `high` for failing) with the new optional ref `intakeSourceId` (public UUID), required for that kind. Producer: the archive-worker intake health loop through `message_outbox` (`aggregate_type = 'organization'`, `aggregate_id` = the decimal organization id), at most once per source per `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_HOURS` (via `notification_alert_windows`). The event-relay publishes every outbox row by its registered event type, whatever the producer, so the row reaches `email.policy` → `email.notifications` on the existing v14 binding. |

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "Journal intake and archive dedupe (step 8.6)"): `JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN` (api, smtp-adapter; Postfix `POSTFIX_JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN` and Rspamd `RSPAMD_JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN` = `${JOURNAL_INTAKE_DOMAIN}`), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ADDRESS_SECRET` (api; secret, 64 hex), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ENABLED`, `LMTP_JOURNAL_PORT` (10053), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ENVELOPE_MAX_BYTES`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_MAX_RECIPIENTS`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_SOURCE_CACHE_SECONDS`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_RATE_PER_SOURCE_PER_MINUTE`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_COUNTER_FLUSH_SECONDS` (smtp-adapter), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES` (api, smtp-adapter), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ROTATION_OVERLAP_DAYS`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_MAX_SOURCES_PER_ORG` (api), `JOURNAL_INTAKE_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`, `JOURNAL_INTAKE_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_HOURS`, `ARCHIVE_DEDUPE_WINDOW_HOURS`, `ARCHIVE_MAX_CONTENT_VARIANTS`, `ARCHIVE_MAX_MERGED_COPIES` (archive-worker).

**Permissions and roles (seed catalog, migration `journal_intake` §3.3).**

| Contract                     | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| New permissions              | `journal:read` ("View journaling settings, journal intake sources and their health"; in `TENANT_READ_PERMISSIONS`) and `journal:manage` ("Manage journaling capture settings and journal intake sources"), category `journaling`; assignable API key scopes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Re-scoped                    | `archive:read` = configuration, retention policies, key versions and status (no records); `archive:search` = search plus record metadata, journal and security views (masked); `archive:manage` = retention, immutability, keys, access settings, disposal of reviewed records. Record routes `GET /archive/messages/{id}`, `/journal`, `/security`, `POST …/reveal` require `archive:search` (reveal also `message:reveal`).                                                                                                                                                            |
| Capture issues               | `GET /archive/capture-issues`, `GET /archive/capture-issues/{messageId}` (step AI-5) and `POST /archive/capture-issues/{messageId}/retry` need `journal:manage` or `archive:manage` (API keys allowed; the retry also needs the plan feature `archiving`). Another organization's or an unknown message, or one without a capture intent → `404 archive_capture_issue_not_found`.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ARCHIVE_ACCESS_PERMISSIONS` | `archive:search`, `archive:retrieve`, `archive:export`, `archive:legal_hold`, `archive:manage`, `ediscovery:manage` (exported by `@email-gateway/database`). Not held by `organization_admin`; assigning or removing a role carrying any of them writes `rbac.archive_access_granted` / `rbac.archive_access_revoked`; a `user:manage` holder without them may do so only as a user in an MFA session and not on its own account (step 8.6 review).                                                                                                                                      |
| System roles (10)            | `organization_admin` = every tenant permission except `ARCHIVE_ACCESS_PERMISSIONS`; `domain_admin` + `journal:read`, `journal:manage`; `security_analyst` + `journal:read`; read-set holders (`platform_admin`, `compliance_auditor`, `read_only_user`) + `journal:read`; new `compliance_officer` = `organization:read`, `user:read`, `domain:read`, `journal:read`, `archive:read`, `ARCHIVE_ACCESS_PERMISSIONS`, `message:reveal`, `audit:read`, `report:read`.                                                                                                                       |
| Grant-preserving migration   | Every system `organization_admin` assignment in a customer organization (`organizations.kind = 'customer'`) whose user had no `compliance_officer` assignment got one audit row (`audit_logs`: actor `system`, actor id `migration:journal_intake`, action `rbac.archive_access_migrated`, resource `user` + public id, metadata `{ roleKey, reason: 'step_8_6_archive_access_split', fromRole: 'organization_admin' }`) and then the assignment (`granted_by_user_id` NULL). Idempotent; no assignment deleted. Verified with `node dist/migrations/cli.js effective-permissions export | compare` (no user lost a permission). |

**Plans and features (seed catalog).**
