**Registry** (§4.4 of the design): `testedBy` ATO and incident writes → `apps/api/integration/assistant-writes-ato.test.ts`; BEC, DLP and risk-scoring writes → `apps/api/integration/assistant-writes-detection.test.ts`; platform writes → `apps/api/integration/assistant-writes-platform-ops.test.ts`; reads → the tool sweep. Consistency rules add: the CAS field exists in the write's schema; `not_self_field` / `require_read_value` only with a `single` precondition; `redactParams` only on reads and only on existing paths.

### 35.5 Compliance and integrations tools (step AI-5)

[Design](../plan/steps/33-ai-chat-compliance-integrations.md) (§2.0 I22–I23, D1–D13, §3, §4), [ADR-0093 amendment AI-5](adr/0093-assistant-action-confirmation.md#amendment-ai-5-2026-11-25) (items (v)–(y)), [ADR-0091 amendment AI-5](adr/0091-assistant-architecture-loopback-tools.md#amendment-ai-5-2026-11-25) (item (z)). AI-5 enables the last planned operations as chat tools; every write is proposed by the model and runs only after the user confirms the server-authored card of §35.1. The engines are reused unchanged: archive and eDiscovery (§31), journal intake and the Journaling / Archive split (§34), provider connections, the wizard and impact tokens (§33). No table, column, enum value, environment key or migration (178 tables); nothing new is published or consumed: **topology stays v15**. DESIGNED: the new read below, this section and the ADR amendments are built (WP1); the registry, escalations, guards, specs, templates, the impact-token injection, `omitInputs`, outcome wording, prompt, portal and tests are built by the later work packages of the step.

**Tools (27).** The 26 AI-5 operations of the program design's Appendix A — **25 writes** (confirmation card only) and the side-effect-free read `provider_mail_flow_preview` — plus the **new read** `archive_admin_capture_issue` (below). Base risk (manifest `RISK_OVERRIDES`; before escalation):

| Module                   | Writes and base risk                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| archive (5)              | `archive_retention_create` **high** (override; was `low`: a new policy can take precedence and shorten or delete-on-expiry the mail it matches), `archive_retention_update` medium (escalates), `archive_retention_delete` high, `archive_admin_retry` medium, `archive_admin_update` medium (escalates)                                                                        |
| journaling (4)           | `journaling_update` medium (escalates), `intake_sources_update` low (escalates), `intake_sources_disable` high, `intake_sources_attest` medium                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ediscovery (7)           | `ediscovery_cases_create` low, `ediscovery_cases_update` low (escalates), `ediscovery_cases_close` medium, `ediscovery_cases_create_hold` high, `ediscovery_searches_create` low, `ediscovery_searches_review` medium, `ediscovery_exports_create` high                                                                                                                         |
| provider-connections (9) | `provider_connections_create` low, `provider_connections_update` low, `provider_connections_disconnect` high, `provider_attestations_record` medium, `provider_domains_replace` medium, `provider_grants_revoke` high, `provider_grants_verify` medium, `provider_health_check_now` **low** (override; was `medium`: queues a read-only check), `provider_mail_flow_apply` high |
| reads (2)                | `provider_mail_flow_preview` (side-effect-free POST, classified as a read; the allow-listed impact preview), `archive_admin_capture_issue` (new route)                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

After AI-5 (verified from the generated registry by the step): OpenAPI 428 operations; operation tools 354 (all enabled), native 2, not applicable 74 (9 critical); enabled tools 356 = 188 reads + 168 writes; `MISSING` 0; `ASSISTANT_PROGRAM_PHASE = 'AI-5'`. Tool names are the generator's; the generated registry (`packages/assistant/src/registry/operations.generated.ts`, [capability matrix](../assistant/UI_TO_CHAT_CAPABILITY_MATRIX.md)) is the authority. Per-tool preconditions, state fields, CAS fields and display fields are the design's [D6](../plan/steps/33-ai-chat-compliance-integrations.md#d6-confirmation-specs-25-writes). Still not applicable through chat: legal-hold release, archive disposal and archive key rotation (critical, portal only); archived-message previews, downloads, download grants and reveal; export download grants; intake source creation and address rotation; provider grant creation, credentials, OAuth consent and consent completion, validation runs.

**Invariants.**

- **I22 Compliance stays compliant through chat.** No chat tool releases, removes, shortens or narrows a legal hold, disposes of an archived record or rotates archive keys. Every confirmed change that shortens retention, makes expired records delete without review, changes which mail a retention policy applies to, changes the default retention or the immutability mode, turns archiving or journaling off, replaces capture settings (directions, included outcomes, scope, policy archiving, intake scanning, access), or pauses, disables, loosens or narrows an intake source (networks, TLS, client certificates, a lower size limit, the silence alert off) is `high` (password + TOTP re-authentication), by its base risk or by escalation. Placing a legal hold and creating an eDiscovery export are `high`.
- **I23 No archived content and no integration secret through chat.** No AI-5 tool returns a message body, an attachment, an export package or a download link; an export's file is downloaded only in the portal. Journal intake addresses are never revealed through chat (`omitInputs`); the Google delegation confirmation (`subjectEmail`) and validation runs stay in the portal. Provider impact tokens never reach the model: the server obtains them from a fresh preview and injects them; they are dropped from envelopes, cards, execution params, audit params and logs by the credential-key scrub and kept only in the stored confirmation request until it expires.

**Conditional escalation** (ADR-0093 (k), (v); effective risk = max(base, matching rows); fixed English warnings appended to the summary's warnings, dedup, cap 5; constants `AI5_ESCALATION_WARNINGS`):

| Tool                        | Condition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Risk   |
| --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ |
| `archive_retention_update`  | `below { path: 'body.retentionDays', field: 'retentionDays' }`                                                                                                                                                                                 | high   |
| `archive_retention_update`  | `becomes { path: 'body.onExpiry', field: 'onExpiry', value: 'delete', absentAs: 'delete' }`                                                                                                                                                    | high   |
| `archive_retention_update`  | `differs { path: 'body.criteria', field: 'criteria', absentAs: {} }` / `differs { path: 'body.priority', field: 'priority', absentAs: 100 }`                                                                                                   | high   |
| `archive_admin_update`      | `equals body.enabled false` / `present body.settings`                                                                                                                                                                                          | high   |
| `archive_admin_update`      | `differs { path: 'body.immutability', field: 'immutability' }` / `differs { path: 'body.defaultRetentionPolicyId', field: 'defaultRetentionPolicyId' }`                                                                                        | high   |
| `journaling_update`         | `equals body.enabled false` / `present body.settings`                                                                                                                                                                                          | high   |
| `intake_sources_update`     | `equals body.status 'paused'` / `present body.networks` / `equals body.requireTls false` / `present body.clientCertFingerprints` / `below { path: 'body.maxMessageBytes', field: 'maxMessageBytes' }` / `equals body.silenceAlertMinutes null` | high   |
| `ediscovery_cases_update`   | `present body.custodians`                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | medium |
| `ediscovery_exports_create` | `equals body.acknowledgeMalicious true` (adds the warning; the base risk is already high)                                                                                                                                                      | high   |

Read-relative kinds (ADR-0093 (v)): `below` (the numeric argument is lower than the read's number), `becomes` (the argument equals `value` and the read's value does not), `differs` (canonical JSON differs: sorted object keys, arrays in order, so a reordered list escalates). The argument is the value at `path`, or `absentAs` when the path is absent and `absentAs` is given (the route's `PUT` defaults); an absent argument without `absentAs` never matches. The read value is the precondition snapshot's `field`, which must be one of the spec's `stateFields` (consistency rule), so the confirm-time state digest proves it unchanged. **Fail closed:** a missing or `null` read value, a non-number for `below`, or a snapshot string that does not parse (a list or object cut by the snapshot caps) matches. `effectiveRisk(tool, spec, finalArgs, snapshot)`: the proposer passes the fresh snapshot, the confirm route's step 7a the stored `preconditions.snapshot` (`null` → every read-relative condition matches). Enabling archiving or journaling alone, renaming or lengthening a retention policy stay `medium`; renaming an intake source, raising its size limit, changing its silence alert interval, or resuming it stay `low`; lowering the size limit or turning the silence alert off is `high`.

**Proposal guards** (ADR-0093 (x); after-read guards on the `single` precondition; fixed English messages without values): `require_read_value.equals` may now be a number (strict equality).

| Message key                | Tool and guard                                                                                                      | The api's own refusal (stays the authority) |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `retention_default_policy` | `archive_retention_delete`: `require_read_value { field: 'isDefault', equals: false }` on `archive_retention_get`   | `409 archive_retention_policy_is_default`   |
| `capture_not_retryable`    | `archive_admin_retry`: `require_read_value { field: 'status', equals: 'failed' }` on `archive_admin_capture_issue`  | `409 archive_capture_retry_not_allowed`     |
| `case_active_holds`        | `ediscovery_cases_close`: `require_read_value { field: 'counts.activeHolds', equals: 0 }` on `ediscovery_cases_get` | `409 ediscovery_case_has_active_holds`      |

A refusal is `denied_guard` at proposal (no confirmation row, no loopback request) and `403 assistant_action_not_allowed` at confirm (re-evaluated on the fresh read).

**Server-filled impact token** (ADR-0093 (w)) for `provider_mail_flow_apply` (preview `change` from `body.change`) and `provider_connections_disconnect` (constant `change: { kind: 'disable' }`), both with the single precondition `provider_connections_get {id}`: `ConfirmationSpec.impactToken { previewTool: 'provider_mail_flow_preview', params: { id: 'id' }, change }`; `IMPACT_PREVIEW_TOOLS` = `['provider_mail_flow_preview']` (an enabled read; the api's precondition `get()` keeps refusing non-GET tools, a separate preview path accepts only this allow-list).

1. The executor, before the schema check, sets the placeholder `body.impactToken = 'pending-preview'` and `body.confirm = true` when absent.
2. The proposer, after the GET precondition read, sends the preview through the loopback (`POST`, the caller's bearer and correlation id, writer read) and merges its response under `preview` into the precondition target (state and display fields address `preview.<path>`; `preview.impactToken` is never a state or display field). A non-2xx preview fails the proposal with its coded error. Then `body.impactToken` ← the preview's token and `body.confirm` ← `true`, **always overriding** the model's values, before CAS injection and the final schema check.
3. At confirm the GET and the preview are re-run; the state digest over the state fields (`rowVersion`, `status` and the preview's change-set fields under `preview.`) must equal the stored one (`409 assistant_confirmation_state_changed`). The stored request keeps the proposal's token (bound into the action digest; its 900 s TTL outlives the 300 s confirmation); an expired or invalidated token fails the confirmation with the route's `409 impact_changed`.
4. The token is kept only in the stored confirmation request (≤ 15 minutes valid, bound to the user and the change set); never in envelopes, cards, the confirmation view, execution params, audit params or logs. Each preview is audited by the api (`integration.mail_flow_previewed`: one at proposal, one at confirm). The tool descriptions tell the model to pass only `change` (apply) or only `id` (disconnect).

**Omitted inputs** (ADR-0091 (z)): `OperationOverride.omitInputs` removes optional inputs from the generated input schema (query parameter from `properties` and `queryParams`; `body.<property>` from the body's `properties` and `required`), `describe_tool` and the catalogue line; a forced value is `denied_schema` without a loopback request. AI-5: `intake_sources_get` (`IntakeSourcesController_get_v1`) omits `reveal` (the intake address stays masked; changes an AI-1 tool), `provider_grants_verify` (`ProviderGrantsController_verify_v1`) omits `body.subjectEmail` (the Google delegation confirmation stays in the portal; a first-time Google verify answers the route's own `422`).

**CAS injection** (ADR-0093 (s)): `body.expectedRowVersion` ← `rowVersion` for retention update, archive settings, journaling settings, intake source update and case update; `body.rowVersion` ← `rowVersion` for the connection rename. The routes' own conflicts stay (`409 archive_retention_policy_conflict`, `409 archive_settings_conflict`, `409 journal_intake_source_conflict`, …).

**Outcome wording** (package `updates.ts`, portal `confirmation-card.tsx`): `QUEUED_202_TOOLS` gains `ediscovery_searches_create`, `ediscovery_exports_create`, `provider_health_check_now` (and any other AI-5 tool whose success is `202` queued work): "the user confirmed it; it was queued (HTTP 202)". An executed export: "the user confirmed it; the export was queued (HTTP 202). It is downloaded only on the case page in the portal."; the portal says "Export queued. Download it on the case page when it is ready; downloads never go through chat." with the link "Open the case". An executed search: "…queued (HTTP 202); follow it with `ediscovery_searches_get`". Denied cards of the three guards link to the page that resolves them (retention policies, legal holds, capture issues).

**Confirm route** codes unchanged: `403 assistant_action_not_allowed` now also for the three new guards; `409 assistant_confirmation_state_changed` also when the preview's change set changed; `409 assistant_confirmation_tool_changed` for a recomputed risk difference (including a read-relative one). Audit metadata `guard` values unchanged (`require_read_value`).

**New read** (thin controller on the existing `ArchiveOperationsService`; one non-locking lookup by message public id with the list's tenant filter and projection, from the read replica — the writer inside the assistant's precondition reads, as every replica read; no plan gating, as the list):

| Method | Path                                         | Operation id → tool                                                      | Permission / flags                                           | Not found                             |
| ------ | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/api/v1/archive/capture-issues/{messageId}` | `ArchiveAdminController_captureIssue_v1` → `archive_admin_capture_issue` | `journal:manage` or `archive:manage` (any), API keys allowed | `404 archive_capture_issue_not_found` |

The answer is the list item's view (`messageId`, `receivedAt`, `status`, `code`, `attempts`, `sources`, `nextCheckAt`, `createdAt`) for **any** intent status (`pending`, `captured_open`, `closed`, `skipped`, `lost`, `failed`), so a card can say "captured", "pending" or "lost". An unknown message, another organization's or one without a capture intent answers exactly like an unknown id; a non-uuid id `400`. Listed in the [api reference](../api/README.md) next to its list and in §31 and §34.

**Registry** (design §4.3): `testedBy` archive, journaling and eDiscovery writes (16) → `apps/api/integration/assistant-writes-compliance.test.ts`; provider-connection writes (9) → `apps/api/integration/assistant-writes-integrations.test.ts`; reads → the tool sweep. Consistency rules add: a read-relative escalation's `field` ∈ the spec's single-precondition `stateFields` and its `path` in the input schema; `impactToken` only with a `single` precondition and only `IMPACT_PREVIEW_TOOLS`, the write has `body.impactToken` and `body.confirm`; a server-filled `body.impactToken` satisfies the credential-key input rule, no other credential-pattern input does; `omitInputs` only optional, existing inputs; no state or display field with a credential-pattern key; all guards of a spec share one message key.

### 35.6 Hardening and operations (step AI-6)

[Design](../plan/steps/34-ai-chat-hardening-operations.md) (§2.0 I24–I27, D1–D13, §3, §4), [ADR-0096](adr/0096-assistant-async-report-jobs-session-job-token.md) (asynchronous report jobs with a job token of the user's live session), [ADR-0097](adr/0097-assistant-provider-routing-tenant-keys-pseudonymization.md) (provider routing and fallback, tenant-supplied keys, the `pseudonymized` level), amendments AI-6 of [ADR-0091](adr/0091-assistant-architecture-loopback-tools.md#amendment-ai-6-2026-11-26), [ADR-0092](adr/0092-llm-provider-abstraction-and-data-handling.md#amendment-ai-6-2026-11-26), [ADR-0094](adr/0094-assistant-conversation-storage-retention-audit.md#amendment-ai-6-2026-11-26) and [ADR-0095](adr/0095-assistant-reports-native-tools-static-html-email.md#amendment-ai-6-2026-11-26). AI-6 makes the assistant operable in production: tenant AI administration (usage per user and day, limits, a per-user budget, data sharing, an optional tenant-supplied provider key, feedback), a metadata-only conversation audit view, ratings of answers, content search of one's own conversations, the `pseudonymized` data-sharing level, asynchronous long-window reports, a secondary provider with health and breakers, and the `api-assistant` Deployment. **Status:** DESIGNED; WP1 built the data model (migration `20261125195835_ai_assistant_hardening`), the event and topology v16, the configuration keys, the ADRs and this contract; the package, api, OpenAPI, operations and portal work follow in WP2–WP7. No new chat tool: 356 enabled tools stay; the 11 routes below are classified `not_applicable` automatically; prompt `assistant-system/7`; registry program phase `AI-6`.

**Invariants** (added to I1–I23).

- **I24 Administrators see metadata, never conversation content (Q9 kept).** Audit and admin views return ids, timestamps, statuses, tool names, operation ids, decisions, risks, HTTP statuses, error codes, the stored redacted parameters, token counts and costs. Titles, message text, provider transcripts, result cards and report HTML are returned to their owner only. The only user-written text an administrator sees is a feedback comment the user submitted with a visible notice.
- **I25 Pseudonymized means the model never receives the value.** At `pseudonymized`, every e-mail address, IP address, domain and person name detected by the D3 rules is replaced by a per-conversation token before any text reaches the provider; the mapping lives only in MySQL under the conversation's content retention and in memory during a turn. Rendered answers, cards, audit rows and executed requests carry real values.
- **I26 Background work acts only as the user's live session, read-only, for one report.** A report job reads through the loopback with an access token minted for the requesting user's own session family, valid 5 minutes, accepted only for `GET` on the fixed report source routes; a revoked or expired session, a lost permission, a disabled assistant or an expired job ends it. No credential is stored or published.
- **I27 A tenant key is write-only.** Encrypted at rest with a dedicated key and row-bound AAD, decrypted only in api memory to call the provider, never returned, logged, audited, sent to the model, put in an event, a card or an example; validation and tests use local fakes only.

**Routes** (tag `assistant`, `@UserOnly()`, cursor v1 on the lists, all classified `not_applicable` in the registry automatically; never tools).

| Method | Path                                              | Operation id                                     | Permission            | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PUT    | `/assistant/settings/provider-key`                | `AssistantProviderKeyController_set_v1`          | `assistant:configure` | `@RequireMfa`, rate class `sensitive`, CAS `expectedRowVersion`. Body `{ provider: 'anthropic' \| 'openai', model, apiKey, expectedRowVersion }`; `apiKey` `writeOnly` (no `example`), 20–512 printable ASCII; `provider` ∈ `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_PROVIDERS`, `model` ∈ `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_MODELS`. Probed before it is stored (Anthropic `models.retrieve(model)`, OpenAI `GET {base}/models/{model}`, timeout `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_VALIDATION_TIMEOUT_MS`): `401/403` → `422 assistant_tenant_key_rejected`, network/5xx → `502 assistant_tenant_key_unverified`, nothing stored. `200` `TenantKeyView`                                                                            |
| DELETE | `/assistant/settings/provider-key`                | `AssistantProviderKeyController_remove_v1`       | `assistant:configure` | `@RequireMfa`, `sensitive`; deletes the row and sets `useTenantKey = false`; `204`; `404 assistant_tenant_key_not_found` when none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| POST   | `/assistant/settings/provider-key/validate`       | `AssistantProviderKeyController_validate_v1`     | `assistant:configure` | `@RequireMfa`, `sensitive`; re-probes the stored key; updates only `status`, `last_validated_at`, `last_error_code`; `200` `TenantKeyView`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| GET    | `/assistant/search`                               | `AssistantSearchController_search_v1`            | `assistant:use`       | Rate class `expensive`; `q` 2–200 characters after trimming, `cursor`, `limit`. Own non-deleted conversations only (owner scope incl. override id, as `GET /assistant/conversations`); message `content` and conversation `title`, case- and accent-insensitive (`utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci`), escaped `LIKE '%q%'`, newest first, over at most `ASSISTANT_SEARCH_SCAN_MAX_MESSAGES` recent messages, on the replica with `MAX_EXECUTION_TIME(ASSISTANT_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS)` (`503 assistant_search_timeout`). Items `{ conversationId, conversationTitle, messageId, role, seq, createdAt, snippet }` (snippet ≤ 160 characters around the first match, sanitized). Purged content is not found |
| PUT    | `/assistant/messages/{messageId}/feedback`        | `AssistantFeedbackController_set_v1`             | `assistant:use`       | Owner only; the message is an `assistant` message of a `completed` turn in a conversation whose content was not purged (else `404 assistant_message_not_found` / `409 assistant_feedback_not_allowed`). Body `{ rating: 'up' \| 'down', reason?: 'incorrect' \| 'incomplete' \| 'not_helpful' \| 'wrong_action' \| 'unsafe' \| 'too_slow' \| 'other', comment?: string ≤ 500 }` (sanitized like user text); upsert; `200` `FeedbackView`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| DELETE | `/assistant/messages/{messageId}/feedback`        | `AssistantFeedbackController_remove_v1`          | `assistant:use`       | Owner only; `204`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| GET    | `/assistant/admin/usage/users`                    | `AssistantAdminController_usageByUser_v1`        | `assistant:configure` | `from`, `to` (UTC days, window ≤ 92 days), `userId?`, cursor v1. Per user and day: user `{ id, displayName, email }`, turns, input/output/cache-read/cache-write tokens, total tokens, `estimatedCostMicros` (`null` when no price is known), `keySource`; totals per user and for the window. Cost is labelled "estimated"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET    | `/assistant/admin/feedback`                       | `AssistantAdminController_feedback_v1`           | `assistant:configure` | `from`, `to` (≤ 92 days), `rating?`, `reason?`, `userId?`, cursor v1. Items: rating, reason, comment, user, created/updated, conversation and message public ids, the turn's provider, model, prompt version, data-sharing level and the names of the tools the turn called                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET    | `/assistant/admin/conversations`                  | `AssistantAdminAuditController_conversations_v1` | `audit:read`          | `userId?`, `from?`, `to?`, `status?`, cursor v1, newest `last_turn_at` first; organization from the scope (override conversations of the organization included and flagged). Items: conversation public id, user, status, created, last turn, turn and segment counts, content purged, under platform override, totals (tokens, cost, tool calls, denied calls, confirmations by status, reports, feedback up/down). **No title**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| GET    | `/assistant/admin/conversations/{conversationId}` | `AssistantAdminAuditController_conversation_v1`  | `audit:read`          | The list header plus `turns[]`, `executions[]`, `confirmations[]`, `reports[]`, `feedback[]` (fields below); `404 assistant_admin_conversation_not_found`; audited `assistant.audit_viewed` (resource `assistant_conversation`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| GET    | `/assistant/admin/executions`                     | `AssistantAdminAuditController_executions_v1`    | `audit:read`          | `from`, `to` (≤ 92 days), `userId?`, `tool?`, `module?`, `decision?`, `status?`, `risk?`, `access?`, `correlationId?`, `conversationId?`, cursor v1: the execution rows of the detail view with user and conversation public ids. No content search across users (A6-2)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

**Audit detail fields** (metadata only, I24): `turns[]` `{ seq, status, provider, model, responseModel, keySource, dataSharing, fallbackFrom, promptVersion, registryVersion, rounds, toolCalls, tokens, costMicros, latencyMs, errorCode, startedAt, finishedAt }`; `executions[]` `{ id, seq, turnSeq, tool, operationId, method, access, risk, decision, status, httpStatus, errorCode, paramsRedacted (exactly as stored), resourceRef, latencyMs, correlationId, createdAt }`; `confirmations[]` `{ id, operationId, risk, requiresReauth, status, createdAt, expiresAt, decidedAt, resultHttpStatus, errorCode }`; `reports[]` `{ id, status, period, sections, omitted, sizeBytes, async, jobStatus, deliveries: { recipientKind, recipientDomain, status, errorCode, requestedAt, sentAt }[] }` (recipient domain only); `feedback[]` `{ messageId, rating, reason }` (no comment). Never a title, message, transcript, card or report HTML.

**`TenantKeyView`**: `{ provider, model, fingerprint, status: 'valid' | 'invalid' | 'unverified', setAt, setBy: { id, displayName } | null, lastValidatedAt, lastErrorCode }` — never the key or its ciphertext. **`FeedbackView`**: `{ messageId, rating, reason, comment, createdAt, updatedAt }`.

**Changed views** (additive; stream `assistant-stream/1` unchanged):

- **Settings** (`GET`/`PUT /assistant/settings`): `dataSharing: 'metadata' | 'pseudonymized'` (editable; applies from the next turn), `userDailyTokenBudget: number | null` (editable; 1 … the tenant's effective daily budget), `useTenantKey`, `tenantKeyFallbackToPlatform` (editable booleans; `useTenantKey = true` needs a stored key), `tenantKey: TenantKeyView | null`, `tenantKeysOffered: { enabled, providers, models }`, `effectiveLimits` (read-only: turns per user per minute, message length, tool rounds and calls per turn, turn deadline, platform daily cap set yes/no, tenant budget max, reports per user per day, report emails per user/tenant per day, confirmation TTL, retention bounds). `assistant.settings_updated` metadata gains the changed field names (never the key).
- **Status** (`GET /assistant/status`): `keySource: 'platform' | 'tenant'`, `fallbackActive: boolean`, `dataSharing`.
- **Messages** (`GET /assistant/conversations/{id}/messages`): assistant messages carry `feedback: { rating, reason, comment } | null` (owner view).
- **Report view** (`GET /assistant/reports/{reportId}`, §35.3): `status` gains `queued`; `job: { status: 'queued' | 'running' | 'succeeded' | 'failed' | 'cancelled', attempts, errorCode, expiresAt } | null`. The card polls every 5 s while `queued`/`running`, stopping at a terminal state or `expiresAt`.
- **Card kind `report`**: `report.status: 'queued' | 'available'`; a queued card has no figures.
- **Envelope of a queued report** (`report_generate` when `ASSISTANT_REPORT_JOBS_ENABLED` and the window is longer than `ASSISTANT_REPORT_SYNC_MAX_HOURS`): `status: 'ok'`, `httpStatus: 202`, `data: { ref, reportId, status: 'queued', title, period, sections }`, note "The report is being generated in the background and appears in the chat when it is ready. Do not state any figures for it." The execution row is `allowed`/`succeeded` with HTTP 202. Action updates of the next turn report "report_generate (call r<n>): the background report is ready" / "… failed (<code>)" (`ai_chat_report_jobs.reported_at`). The input schema of `report_generate` is unchanged (the server decides).
- **Turn start**: a per-user budget refusal answers `429 assistant_budget_exhausted` with `details.scope: 'user'`.

**Error codes** (new): `assistant_tenant_keys_disabled` 403 (platform gate off), `assistant_tenant_key_rejected` 422, `assistant_tenant_key_unverified` 502, `assistant_tenant_key_not_found` 404, `assistant_tenant_key_invalid` 503 (a turn whose tenant key was refused and no platform fallback is allowed), `assistant_message_not_found` 404, `assistant_feedback_not_allowed` 409, `assistant_search_timeout` 503, `assistant_admin_conversation_not_found` 404, `assistant_report_not_ready` 409 (`report_send_email` on a report that is not `available`); executor message code `assistant_unknown_placeholder` (decision `denied_schema`: "Unknown placeholder [EMAIL_99]; use a value from this conversation."); job error codes `assistant_report_job_expired`, `assistant_report_session_ended`, `assistant_report_not_allowed`, `assistant_report_job_failed`; `assistant_budget_exhausted` gains `details.scope: 'user'`.

**Audit actions** (new): `assistant.feedback_recorded` and `assistant.feedback_removed` (`{ messageId, conversationId, rating, reason }`, never the comment), `assistant.audit_viewed` (resource `assistant_conversation`), `assistant.report_job_queued` (with `assistant.tool_executed`), `assistant.provider_key_set`, `assistant.provider_key_removed`, `assistant.provider_key_validated`, `assistant.provider_key_invalidated` (`{ provider, model, fingerprint, outcome }`, never the key). `assistant.report_generated` of a job carries `{ async: true, jobId }` (actor = the user).

**Job token** (ADR-0096, I26): `TokenService.issueAssistantJobAccess({ userPublicId, organizationPublicId (home organization), sessionId, amr, overrideId?, jobId })` → an ordinary access JWT plus claim **`ajb: <jobPublicId>`**, `exp = now + 300 s`; never stored, logged or sent anywhere except as the `authorization` header of the loopback call. `AuthGuard`: a verified access token with `ajb` is accepted only for `GET` on a matched route path in `ASSISTANT_JOB_ROUTES` (`/api/v1/reports/{traffic,threats,url-clicks,delivery,processing,top-domains}`, `/api/v1/quarantine/summary`, `/api/v1/ato/overview`, `/api/v1/ato/accounts`, derived from the package's `REPORT_SOURCES`); anything else → `401 unauthorized` (metric `api_assistant_job_token_refusals_total`, warn log with the route template and job id only). Session revocation, permissions, rate limits, tenant scope and override checks run unchanged.

**Event** `assistant.report.generate_requested.v1` (`EventTypes.AssistantReportGenerateRequested`, schema `assistantReportGenerateRequestedV1` in `packages/event-contracts/src/assistant-events.ts`; [§6](#6-event-payloads)): exchange `email.policy`, aggregate type **`assistant_report_job`** (`aggregate_id` = the job's public id, also the `eventId`), envelope `organizationId` = the job's organization; payload strict `{ jobId: uuid, reportId: uuid, requestedAt: ISO date-time }` (ids only); producer the api in the turn's transaction (report row `queued`, job row, outbox row, execution and audit together); consumer the api-assistant `ReportJobConsumer` on **`assistant.reports`**. **Topology v16** ([messaging-topology.md](messaging-topology.md#status)): queue `assistant.reports` (work, home exchange `email.policy`, bindings the event + `retry.assistant.reports.#`, ledger `api-assistant.reports`, service `api-assistant`, retry `hold` 5 attempts 10 s–60 s within 30 min, policy `work`, not in `RECONCILE_QUEUES`); role user **`eg-assistant`** (configure `^$`, write `^email\.(policy|events)$`, read `^assistant\.reports$`, topics `email.policy` `^retry\.assistant\.reports\.`, `email.events` `^dead-letter\.assistant\.reports(\.|$)`); the `eg-ops` `email.policy` grant gains `assistant\.reports` in its retry alternation; 25 queues, 76 bindings, 25 policies. Consumer steps (idempotent through the ledger and row CAS): load the job (missing → ack; terminal → ack; past `expires_at` → `failed` `assistant_report_job_expired`), CAS `queued` (or `running` with an expired lease) → `running` with `attempts + 1` and a 5-minute lease, mint the job token and resolve the principal (`null` → `failed` `assistant_report_session_ended`), re-check availability, `assistant:use` and `report:read` (else `failed` `assistant_report_not_allowed`), compose through AI-R's `buildAndStore` with the job token, store, then one transaction: report `available`, job `succeeded`, audit; "no data" or a storage failure retries while `attempts < 5` and before `expires_at`, else `failed`.

**Storage** (migration `20261125195835_ai_assistant_hardening`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md#ai-assistant-9), tables 178 → **183**): `ai_chat_settings` gains `data_sharing` value `pseudonymized`, `user_daily_token_budget`, `use_tenant_key`, `tenant_key_fallback_to_platform`; `ai_chat_turns` gains `data_sharing`, `key_source` (`platform`/`tenant`), `fallback_from` and index `(organization_id, user_id, started_at)`; `ai_chat_conversations` index `(organization_id, last_turn_at, id)`; `ai_chat_tool_executions` indexes `(organization_id, user_id, created_at)` and `(organization_id, tool_name, created_at)`; `ai_chat_reports.status` gains `queued`; new tables `ai_chat_usage_user_daily` (backfilled from the turns), `ai_chat_feedback` (`comment` content, purged), `ai_chat_pseudonyms` (`value` content; rows deleted at the content purge), `ai_chat_provider_keys` (ciphertext only) and `ai_chat_report_jobs`. Retention: [retention.md](../database/retention.md#planned-feature-owned-purges).

**Redis keys** (prefix `as:`): `as:tok:u:<userPublicId>:<yyyymmdd>` (per-user daily tokens, TTL 48 h, seeded from `ai_chat_usage_user_daily` on a miss); `as:brk:<provider>` (platform primary, as before), `as:brk:<provider>:fb` (platform secondary), `as:brk:t:<organizationPublicId>` (a tenant key; never opens a platform breaker).

**Pseudonymization** (D3, I25): tokens `[EMAIL_n]`, `[IP_n]`, `[DOMAIN_n]`, `[PERSON_n]` (per kind and conversation, stable for the conversation's content lifetime; `[KIND_REDACTED]` beyond `ASSISTANT_PSEUDONYMS_MAX_PER_CONVERSATION`); inbound text that already matches the token grammar is neutralized to `(KIND_n)`; applied to the context block (organization name → "Your organization"), the user message, action updates, recap text and every string of every tool envelope after the minimizer; tool-call arguments are de-mapped before refs, bindings and Ajv (unknown bracketed token → `denied_schema` `assistant_unknown_placeholder`); streamed text is de-mapped with a 16-character hold-back; the stored assistant message is de-mapped, `provider_transcript` keeps the tokens. Unchanged by the level: cards, confirmation summaries, report HTML and figures, audit rows, executed requests, `describe_tool` and the catalogue.

**Provider routing** (D6, D7, ADR-0097): candidates per turn: tenant key (stored, `valid`, `useTenantKey`) → platform primary (`ASSISTANT_PROVIDER`/`ASSISTANT_MODEL`) → platform secondary (`ASSISTANT_FALLBACK_PROVIDER`/`ASSISTANT_FALLBACK_MODEL`); with a tenant key the platform candidates follow only with `tenantKeyFallbackToPlatform`. First candidate with a closed breaker wins; none → `503 assistant_provider_unavailable`. One same-turn switch when the first model call fails with a breaker-qualifying error before any text delta and before any tool ran. A provider or model change against the segment's last turn starts a new segment from the deterministic recap. The tenant and per-user budgets count every turn; the platform daily cap counts `key_source = platform` turns only. Turns record `provider`/`model` used, `key_source` and `fallback_from`.

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "AI assistant hardening (step AI-6)"; working values appended to the local `.env`; RabbitMQ keys in the RabbitMQ section; start-up errors name keys, never values):

| Key                                                  | Service                            | Bounds / default                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ASSISTANT_FALLBACK_PROVIDER`                        | api                                | `none` \| `fake` \| `anthropic` \| `openai` \| `azure_openai`, `none`; must differ from `ASSISTANT_PROVIDER` unless `ASSISTANT_FALLBACK_MODEL` differs from `ASSISTANT_MODEL`; `fake` refused with `NODE_ENV=production` while enabled; needs its provider's credentials while enabled (Azure: the `ASSISTANT_AZURE_OPENAI_*` keys) |
| `ASSISTANT_FALLBACK_MODEL`                           | api                                | model id (1–64 of `[A-Za-z0-9._:-]`); required for an `anthropic`/`openai` secondary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEYS_ENABLED`                      | api                                | bool, `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_PROVIDERS`                     | api                                | csv, non-empty subset of `anthropic,openai`, `anthropic,openai`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_MODELS`                        | api                                | csv of 1–32 model ids, `claude-opus-5-5,claude-sonnet-5-5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (+`_PREVIOUS`) | api                                | 64 hex (`__GENERATE_64__`); required when tenant keys are enabled; `_FILE` accepted; the previous key (decrypt only) must differ                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ASSISTANT_TENANT_KEY_VALIDATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | api                                | 1 000–30 000, `10000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ASSISTANT_PSEUDONYMS_MAX_PER_CONVERSATION`          | api                                | 100–50 000, `5000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ASSISTANT_SEARCH_SCAN_MAX_MESSAGES`                 | api                                | 1 000–200 000, `20000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ASSISTANT_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | api                                | 500–10 000, `3000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ASSISTANT_REPORT_JOBS_ENABLED`                      | api                                | bool, `true`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ASSISTANT_REPORT_SYNC_MAX_HOURS`                    | api                                | 24–744, `168`, ≤ `REPORTS_MAX_RANGE_DAYS` × 24                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ASSISTANT_REPORT_JOB_TTL_SECONDS`                   | api                                | 300–7 200, `1800`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ASSISTANT_REPORT_JOBS_CONSUMER`                     | api                                | bool, `false` (local `.env`: `true`; Compose's api container: always `false`); requires `RABBITMQ_ASSISTANT_URL`                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ASSISTANT_REPORT_JOB_CONCURRENCY`                   | api                                | 1–8, `2`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RABBITMQ_ASSISTANT_USER` / `_PASSWORD` / `_URL`     | api (api-assistant), rabbitmq-init | `eg-assistant`, `__GENERATE__`, `amqp://…/${RABBITMQ_VHOST}` like the other role users (`_URL` optional for the api, `_FILE` accepted; Compose builds the container URL)                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ARCHIVE_EXPORT_TTL_HOURS`                           | archive-worker, **api**            | existing key, 1–8 760, `168` (the api states it in the eDiscovery export confirmation card, D13)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_ASSISTANT_INTERNAL_URL`                         | admin-portal                       | origin URL; empty = `API_INTERNAL_URL` (the BFF proxies `/api/v1/assistant/*` there, D10)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

**Metrics** (api registry, no organization or user labels; D8): `assistant_feedback_total{rating}`, `assistant_report_jobs_total{outcome=queued|succeeded|failed|expired|cancelled|retried}`, `assistant_report_job_seconds`, `assistant_llm_requests_total{provider,role,outcome}` (`role` = `primary|fallback|tenant`), `assistant_llm_seconds{provider,role}`, `assistant_provider_switches_total{from,to,reason}`, `assistant_provider_fallback_active`, `assistant_provider_breaker_open{provider,role}`, `assistant_tenant_key_validations_total{outcome=valid|rejected|unverified}`, `assistant_pseudonyms_total{kind}`, `assistant_search_seconds`, `api_assistant_job_token_refusals_total`; `assistant_budget_refusals_total{scope}` gains `user_tokens`.

## 36. API-mode protection (step API-1)

[Design note](../plan/steps/35-api-mode-protection.md) (program API-1..API-3, step API-1 fully specified), [ADR-0098](adr/0098-protection-modes-and-detect-mode-copies.md) (protection modes, detect-mode copies; supersedes ADR-0087 and ADR-0025 in part), [ADR-0099](adr/0099-mailbox-access-consent-and-custody.md) (mailbox access consent and custody; supersedes ADR-0076 and ADR-0077 in part), [ADR-0100](adr/0100-api-mode-intake-mailbox-worker.md) (mailbox-worker, change feeds, per-mailbox copies, dedupe, lag, coverage). Migration `20261126022823_api_mode_protection` ([database README](../database/README.md#step-api-1-migration-20261126022823_api_mode_protection)). Status: **DESIGNED**; WP1 (data model, catalog, events, topology v17, lifecycle, configuration, ADRs, this section) is built; the libraries, the mailbox-worker, the worker changes, the api routes and the portal follow in WP2–WP6.

Step API-2 adds post-delivery remediation, mailbox-located quarantine entries and release as a provider restore: see [§37](#37-api-mode-remediation-step-api-2) (N1 is amended there by ADR-0102: a mailbox entry of a copy is released as a restore to the Inbox, never a delivery).

Step API-3 adds retroactive clawback, search and purge, internal (east-west) mail and reported phishing: see [§38](#38-api-mode-clawback-search-and-purge-internal-mail-and-reported-phishing-step-api-3) (ADR-0104 supersedes ADR-0100 D7.4 in part: internal journal recipients are recorded as `direction = internal` copies; topology v18).

Step API-4 adds detect-mode policy evaluation of copies, detection alerts and webhooks, mailbox scope, ownership through the bound provider tenant and the API-mode add-domain wizard: see [§39](#39-api-mode-parity-and-onboarding-step-api-4) (**N1 is amended by ADR-0105**: copies with copy verdict authority are policy-evaluated in detect mode — `policy_execution_logs.evaluation_mode = detect`, no lifecycle edge, delivery, hold, gateway quarantine entry, policy copy or `mail.policy.decided.v1` — and by ADR-0106: their detections raise alerts and the `mailbox.*.detected` webhooks; every other N1 guarantee stays; topology v19).

**Invariants (design §2.0).** N1 API mode never delivers, forwards or bounces (copies never reach policy, delivery, reinjection, quarantine release, customer webhooks or a rescan); N2 a mode change never changes inline mail flow; N3 the tenant comes from the principal (api), the provider connection row (mailbox-worker) or a stored subscription id plus a constant-time client-state hash match (notifications only request a sync); N4 no content in MySQL (copies are EMLs in the mail bucket; cursors are opaque and never returned); N5 secrets never leave custody; N6 content is fetched only to scan a copy no other path delivered for that mailbox, no backfill; N7 every provider call is budgeted per connection and API family; N8 idempotent at every hop.

**Data (migration `api_mode_protection`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md#api-mode-protection-step-api-1)).**

| Contract                                                                      | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Protection mode                                                               | `organizations.protection_mode` ENUM(`inline`,`api`,`hybrid`) NOT NULL DEFAULT `inline`; `domains.protection_mode` same ENUM NULL (inherit); both with `protection_mode_changed_at`. Effective mode = `COALESCE(domains.protection_mode, organizations.protection_mode)`. Prisma enum `ProtectionMode`. "API mode active" = feature `api_mode_protection` effective and ≥ 1 non-deleted domain effective `api`/`hybrid`                                                                                                                                                   |
| Ingest source                                                                 | Prisma `MessageIngestSource` gains `mailbox_api` (appended) on `email_messages.ingest_source` and `archive_record_sources.source`. Non-gateway = `journal_intake` or `mailbox_api`; every gateway view keeps `ingest_source = 'gateway'`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `api_mode_connections` (`ApiModeConnection`)                                  | One row per 8.2 provider connection used for API mode (UNIQUE `provider_connection_id`), created lazily; feeds (`feeds_enabled` default off, `feed_mode` `poll`/`notify`), inventory status, `mailboxes_total`, health (`api-mode-health/1` in `health_checks`), `throttled_until`, CAS `row_version`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `api_mailboxes` (`ApiMailbox`), `api_mailbox_addresses` (`ApiMailboxAddress`) | Mailbox per (connection, provider user id) or discovered from a journal envelope (`provider_user_id` NULL); status, coverage, feed state, opaque `feed_cursor`, Graph `subscription_id` (UNIQUE) and `client_state_hash` (SHA-256 only), lease. Addresses (lower case, primary/alias) UNIQUE per organization                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `api_message_copies` (`ApiMessageCopy`)                                       | PARTITIONED RANGE COLUMNS(`received_at`), 6 months, core. One row per scanned copy and mailbox: `source` (`journal`/`graph`/`gmail`), `seen_journal`, `seen_api`, `direction`, `message_id_hash`, `provider_message_id`, `provider_delivered_at` + `delivered_basis`, `status` (`pending`, `scanned`, `scan_failed`, `parse_failed`, `too_large`), `verdict` (`MessageVerdict`), `risk_score`, `verdict_at`, `lag_ms`, `verdict_authority` (`copy`/`inline`) and the inline message link. UNIQUE `(message_id, received_at, mailbox_id)`; `public_id` indexed, not unique |
| `api_copy_dedupe_keys` (`ApiCopyDedupeKey`)                                   | Transient claim per `(mailbox_id, message_id_hash)` (UNIQUE): `claimed_by` (`journal`/`graph`/`gmail`), copy ref (NULL while a feed claim is in flight, `claim_expires_at`); pruned after `API_MODE_DEDUPE_WINDOW_HOURS`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `api_mode_counters` (`ApiModeCounter`)                                        | Hourly counters per connection (UNIQUE `(api_connection_id, hour)`), upserted additively from the Redis hash; lag buckets 60/300/900/3600 s and above, sums and maxima; pruned after 400 days                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Permissions (§3.3)                                                            | `remediation:read` (category `remediation`; tenant read set: `platform_admin`, `compliance_auditor`, `read_only_user`; plus `security_analyst`, `domain_admin`, `organization_admin`, `super_admin`) and `remediation:write` (`organization_admin`, `security_analyst`, `super_admin`). Granted by the migration (idempotent, additive) and the seed catalog alike. `remediation:purge` is API-3                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Feature                                                                       | `api_mode_protection` (catalog `FEATURE_KEYS`, not operator-enabled): on in `enterprise` and `dedicated`, off in `trial`, `standard`, `archive_only`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Events (topology v17).**

| Contract                          | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `mail.message.copy_scanned.v1`    | New (registry `EventTypes.MessageCopyScanned`, schema `messageCopyScannedV1`). Exchange `email.security` (the exchange of `mail.message.scanned.v1`), routing key `org.<orgId>.mail.message.copy_scanned.v1`, aggregate `message` (`aggregate_id` = the message public id). Producer: the email-worker through the outbox in the E30–E32 transaction of a `journal_intake` / `mailbox_api` copy (also `parse_failed` when the copy's scan was requested); never for gateway mail, and such a copy never gets `scanned.v1`. Strict payload: `{ messageId: decimal string, messagePublicId: uuid, receivedAt: ISO, ingestSource: 'journal_intake' \| 'mailbox_api', lifecycleRound: 0–65535, scanId: decimal string \| null, outcome: 'scanned' \| 'scan_failed' \| 'parse_failed', verdict: MessageVerdict \| null, riskScore: 0–1000 \| null, completedAt: ISO }`; `parse_failed` carries no scan, verdict or score, `scanned`/`scan_failed` name the round, `scanned` carries a verdict. Consumer: mailbox-worker `copies` (queue `email.mailbox`) |
| `mail.message.received.v1`        | `ingestSource` gains `mailbox_api` (optional; the schema stays non-strict). Written by the mailbox-worker's `MailboxCopyRepository.commit` for fetched feed copies                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `mail.message.state_changed.v1`   | Unchanged; `journaled` now also ends `mailbox_api` copies (E30–E32, reasons unchanged; `@email-gateway/message-lifecycle` `COPY_INGEST_SOURCES`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `notification.alert_requested.v1` | New kind `api_mode_unhealthy` (severity `medium` degraded, `high` failing) with the new optional ref `apiModeConnectionId` (the `api_mode_connections` public UUID), required for that kind. Producer: the mailbox-worker `health` role through the outbox (`aggregate_type = 'organization'`), at most once per connection per `API_MODE_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_HOURS`; recipients hold `remediation:read`; never addresses                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

**Topology v17** (`TOPOLOGY_VERSION = 17`, [messaging topology](messaging-topology.md)): v16 + the work queue **`email.mailbox`** (quorum, home exchange `email.security`, policy `work` (reject-publish), bindings `org.*.mail.message.copy_scanned.v1` on `email.security` and `retry.email.mailbox.#`; no dot-prefix of another queue name), ledger consumer `mailbox-worker.copies` (service `mailbox-worker`), retry `hold` 5 attempts 10 s–60 s within 24 h. **Reconciled**: `RECONCILE_QUEUES` gains `email.mailbox` (the event is the only path that finalizes a pending copy row), so the copies consumer writes its ledger row for every event it handles (skipped events included) and records retry and dead-letter hops (`PrismaConsumerProgress`). Role user **`eg-mailbox`** (`RABBITMQ_MAILBOX_USER` / `_PASSWORD`): configure `^$`, write `^email\.(security|events)$`, read `^email\.mailbox$`, topics `email.security` `^retry\.email\.mailbox\.` and `email.events` `^dead-letter\.email\.mailbox(\.|$)`. `eg-ops` replay: its `email.security` topic grant gains `email\.mailbox` in the retry alternation. Counts: 6 exchanges, 26 queues, 78 bindings (73 queue + 5 exchange), 26 policies, 14 role users; vhost description `email-gateway topology v17`.

**Documents.**

| Document            | Shape                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api-mode-health/1` | `{ v: 1, checks: [{ code, status: 'pass' \| 'warn' \| 'fail', evidence? (non-secret, ≤ 512 chars) }] (≤ 16) }` in `api_mode_connections.health_checks`; checks `connection.active`, `intake.present`, `intake.journal_health`, `mailbox_access.grant`, `coverage.percent`, `lag.p95_1h`, `throttling.1h`, `feeds.errors`, `domains.api_mode_mx` (design D11.3) |
| `api-mode-guide/1`  | `{ schema, provider, connection: { id, displayName }, sections: SetupGuideSection[], attestations, journalSource?: { id, kind, addressMasked }, platformClientId?, permissions: string[], generatedAt }` (JSON or `text/markdown`)                                                                                                                             |
| Wizard view         | `computeApiModeWizard`: steps `domains`, `connection`, `journal`, `mailbox_access`, `feeds`, `monitor`; status `not_started \| in_progress \| done \| blocked \| skipped`, blockers, `nextAction` (design D12.2)                                                                                                                                               |

**Redis (mailbox-worker).** `mbx:rb:<apiConnectionPublicId>:<family>:s<epochSecond>` (INCR + EXPIRE 5; families `graph`, `gmail`, `google_directory`; limits `API_MODE_*_REQUESTS_PER_SECOND`), `mbx:throttle:<apiConnectionPublicId>:<family>` (TTL = `Retry-After`, capped at 600 s; honoured before every call of that family), `mbx:cnt:<apiConnectionId>:<yyyymmddhh>` (hash, TTL 3 days, flushed every `API_MODE_COUNTER_FLUSH_SECONDS` into `api_mode_counters` with upsert-add).

**Identifiers.**

| Identifier         | Derivation                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Copy ingest key    | `v2:mbx:` + SHA-256 hex of `<connectionPublicId>\|<mailboxPublicId>\|<providerMessageId>` (`message_identities.ingest_key`; a duplicate returns the existing message)                                       |
| EML object ref     | First 32 hex of SHA-256 of `mbx\|<connectionPublicId>\|<mailboxPublicId>\|<providerMessageId>\|<organizationId>`; key `org/<org>/eml/<yyyy>/<mm>/<dd>/<objectRef>.eml`                                      |
| Dedupe hash        | `api-copy/1`: SHA-256 hex of `"api-copy/1\0<organizationId>\0<Message-ID without angle brackets and surrounding whitespace>"`; no Message-ID → no dedupe                                                    |
| Graph client state | `api-mode-graph-client-state/1`: first 48 characters of base64url(HMAC-SHA256(`API_MODE_NOTIFICATION_SECRET`, `"api-mode-graph-client-state/1\0<mailboxPublicId>\0<version>"`)); stored as SHA-256 hex only |

**Audit actions.** `organization.protection_mode_changed` `{ from, to, reason, inheritingDomains, effectiveApiDomains }`, `domain.protection_mode_changed` `{ fromConfigured, toConfigured, fromEffective, toEffective, reason }`, `api_mode.settings_updated`, `api_mode.inventory_sync_requested`, `api_mode.mailbox_resync_requested`, `api_mode.health_changed` (system, `{ connectionId, from, to, failingChecks }`); grants keep their 8.2 actions. Metadata holds enums and ids only (`reason` is untrusted text, metadata only).

**Operations (api, design D12; tags `api-mode` and the public `provider-notifications`).**

| #   | Method + path                                              | Permission                                | Summary                                                                                                |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1   | `GET /organizations/{id}/protection-mode`                  | `organization:read`                       | Mode, `changedAt`, entitlement, domains per effective mode                                             |
| 2   | `PUT /organizations/{id}/protection-mode`                  | `organization:manage`, Idempotency-Key    | Change the default mode (MX conflict over every domain that would become effective `api`), audited     |
| 3   | `PUT /domains/{domainId}/protection-mode`                  | `domain:manage`, Idempotency-Key          | Set or `inherit` a domain's mode (MX conflict `409`), audited                                          |
| 4   | `GET /api-mode/overview`                                   | `remediation:read`                        | Entitlement, activity, per-connection health, coverage, feeds, grant, lag, throttling, copies today    |
| 5   | `GET /api-mode/connections`                                | `remediation:read`                        | Every 8.2 connection with its API-mode row (defaults when absent), grant status, journal sources       |
| 6   | `GET /api-mode/connections/{connectionId}`                 | `remediation:read`                        | One connection + wizard + health checks + 7-day counters                                               |
| 7   | `GET /api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/setup-guide`     | `remediation:read`                        | `api-mode-guide/1` JSON or `text/markdown`                                                             |
| 8   | `PUT /api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/settings`        | `remediation:write`, Idempotency-Key, CAS | `{ feedsEnabled, feedMode }`; needs the active mailbox-access grant; audited                           |
| 9   | `POST /api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/inventory-sync` | `remediation:write`, Idempotency-Key      | Request a mailbox inventory (`202`); audited                                                           |
| 10  | `GET /api-mode/mailboxes`                                  | `remediation:read`                        | Cursor list with filters; never cursors or subscription ids                                            |
| 11  | `GET /api-mode/mailboxes/{mailboxId}`                      | `remediation:read`                        | Mailbox, aliases, last 20 detection summaries                                                          |
| 12  | `POST /api-mode/mailboxes/{mailboxId}/resync`              | `remediation:write`, Idempotency-Key      | Clear the cursor (re-initialize, no backfill), request a sync (`202`); audited                         |
| 13  | `GET /api-mode/detections`                                 | `remediation:read`                        | Cursor list of `api_message_copies` (≤ 31-day window), subject and sender masked (ADR-0062)            |
| 14  | `GET /api-mode/detections/{detectionId}`                   | `remediation:read`                        | One copy + up to 10 risk reason codes                                                                  |
| 15  | `POST /provider-notifications/graph`                       | Public                                    | Graph validation handshake echo or change notifications → `sync_requested_at` only; always `202`       |
