| 16  | `POST /provider-notifications/graph/lifecycle`             | Public                                    | Lifecycle events (`reauthorizationRequired`, `subscriptionRemoved`, `missed`) → renewal or sync; `202` |

Additive changes to existing routes: `POST /domains` + optional `protectionMode` (`api` requires `inboundEnabled: false`); domain and organization views + `protectionMode`; the 8.2 grant routes + the `remediation:write` gate for `mailbox_remediation` profiles; the 8.6 journaling routes + the relaxed entitlement and wizard skip (`api_mode`); organization usage + `apiCopiesToday`. New error codes: `api_mode_not_enabled` (403), `protection_mode_mx_points_to_gateway` (409), `protection_mode_inbound_conflict` (422), `remediation_permission_required` (403), `api_mode_mailbox_access_required` (409), `api_mode_notifications_unavailable` (422), `api_mode_connection_conflict` (409), `api_mode_connection_not_found` / `api_mode_mailbox_not_found` / `api_mode_detection_not_found` (404). Assistant: the new reads are enabled read tools; the new writes are `not_applicable` until API-3; the `provider-notifications` tag is not applicable (design D15).

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "API-mode protection (step API-1)", design D16): `MAILBOX_WORKER_HTTP_PORT`, `MAILBOX_WORKER_HOST_PORT`, `MAILBOX_WORKER_ROLES`, `RABBITMQ_MAILBOX_USER`, `RABBITMQ_MAILBOX_PASSWORD` (secret), `RABBITMQ_MAILBOX_URL`, `API_MODE_NOTIFICATION_SECRET` (secret, 64 hex), `API_MODE_GRAPH_NOTIFICATION_URL`, `API_MODE_GRAPH_URL`, `API_MODE_GMAIL_URL`, `API_MODE_GOOGLE_ADMIN_URL`, `API_MODE_ALLOW_HTTP_ENDPOINTS`, `API_MODE_GRAPH_SUBSCRIPTION_MINUTES`, `API_MODE_SUBSCRIPTION_RENEW_BEFORE_MINUTES`, `API_MODE_FEED_TICK_MS`, `API_MODE_FEED_BATCH`, `API_MODE_FEED_POLL_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_FEED_SAFETY_POLL_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_FETCH_CONCURRENCY`, `API_MODE_FETCH_CONCURRENCY_PER_CONNECTION`, `API_MODE_GRAPH_REQUESTS_PER_SECOND`, `API_MODE_GMAIL_REQUESTS_PER_SECOND`, `API_MODE_DIRECTORY_REQUESTS_PER_SECOND`, `API_MODE_MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES`, `API_MODE_INVENTORY_INTERVAL_HOURS`, `API_MODE_MAX_MAILBOXES_PER_CONNECTION`, `API_MODE_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`, `API_MODE_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_HOURS`, `API_MODE_JOURNAL_COVERAGE_DAYS`, `API_MODE_COVERAGE_WARN_PERCENT`, `API_MODE_COVERAGE_FAIL_PERCENT`, `API_MODE_LAG_WARN_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_LAG_FAIL_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_THROTTLE_WARN_PER_HOUR`, `API_MODE_DEDUPE_WINDOW_HOURS`, `API_MODE_HYBRID_LINK_WINDOW_HOURS`, `API_MODE_COUNTER_FLUSH_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_NOTIFICATION_RATE_PER_MINUTE`, `PROVIDER_M365_MAILBOX_APP_CLIENT_ID`, `PROVIDER_M365_MAILBOX_APP_CLIENT_SECRET`, `PROVIDER_M365_MAILBOX_APP_CERT_PATH`, `PROVIDER_M365_MAILBOX_APP_CERT_THUMBPRINT`, `PROVIDER_GOOGLE_MAILBOX_SA_CLIENT_ID`, `PROVIDER_GOOGLE_MAILBOX_SA_KEY_PATH`; `RECONCILE_QUEUES` gains `email.mailbox`.

## 37. API-mode remediation (step API-2)

[Design note](../plan/steps/36-api-mode-remediation.md) (decisions R1–R17, invariants R-I1–R-I6; the note 35 invariants N1–N9 hold, N1 amended), [ADR-0101](adr/0101-api-mode-remediation-policy-and-mailbox-actions.md) (remediation policy and mailbox actions), [ADR-0102](adr/0102-mailbox-located-quarantine-entries-release-as-restore.md) (mailbox-located quarantine entries, release as provider restore; supersedes ADR-0046, ADR-0047 and ADR-0098 in part). Migration `20261126173221_api_mode_remediation` ([database README](../database/README.md#step-api-2-migration-20261126173221_api_mode_remediation)). Status: **DESIGNED**; WP1 (data model, configuration, ADRs, this section) is built; the libraries, simulators, the mailbox-worker `remediate` role, the api routes, the policy-engine guards and the portal follow in WP2–WP7. Out of scope by product-owner decision (2026-11-26, design §12.2): tenant policy evaluation on copies, `threat_detected` alerts for API-mode detections, a coverage-by-mode page and any wizard or domain-page change.

Step API-3 extends this section: clawbacks and admin purges execute only as `api_remediation_actions` rows (triggers `clawback`, `purge`, column `clawback_id`), and ADR-0103 amends R-I2 (hard delete in an approved portal-only purge) and R-I3 (clawback decisions treat hybrid copies with copy authority): see [§38](#38-api-mode-clawback-search-and-purge-internal-mail-and-reported-phishing-step-api-3).

Step API-4 extends this section: ADR-0105 supersedes ADR-0101 decision 1 in part — the decision gains a policy lane (`policyMapping`, stronger action wins, tenant allow override, decision record `api-remediation-decision/2`, `review` entries for require approval), the skips `outbound_not_remediable` and `policy_allow_override`, and remediation policies gain a detect-only period: see [§39](#39-api-mode-parity-and-onboarding-step-api-4).

**Invariants (design §2).** R-I1 never claim a mailbox state that did not happen (copy state and mailbox entries exist only after the provider call succeeded or the target state was found reached; simulated and skipped decisions are recorded as such); R-I2 never irreversible by default (hard delete needs `API_MODE_HARD_DELETE_ENABLED=true` and an explicit tenant rule; never in a default or preset, never manual, never from the assistant); R-I3 inline decides first (`verdict_authority = inline` copies are never remediated automatically); R-I4 one action per (copy, action, generation) (`action_key` unique, `INSERT IGNORE`, state re-read before acting); R-I5 tenant from trusted context (api: the principal, foreign ids `404`; worker: the action row, re-checked against copy, mailbox and connection before every provider call); R-I6 no content in MySQL.

**Data (migration `api_mode_remediation`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md#step-api-2-changes-api-mode-remediation)).**

| Contract                                            | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api_remediation_policies` (`ApiRemediationPolicy`) | One row per organization (`domain_id` NULL) and per overridden domain: UNIQUE `(organization_id, scope_key)` with the STORED generated `scope_key = COALESCE(domain_id, 0)` (read-only); `mode` (`detect_only`, `enforce`), `document` (`api-remediation-policy/1`), `document_sha256` (canonical JSON SHA-256), CAS `row_version`, `updated_by_user_id` (no FK). FK organizations and domains RESTRICT. No organization row = the platform default P1. Written by the api only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `api_remediation_actions` (`ApiRemediationAction`)  | One row per mailbox action (not partitioned): `action`, `generation`, UNIQUE `action_key`, `trigger`, `status`, `reason_code`, `decision` (`api-remediation-decision/1` for `trigger = policy`, `{ requestedAction, reason }` otherwise), reversal links (`reverses_action_id`, `reversed_by_action_id`), `quarantine_entry_id`, `release_log_id`, requester (`requested_by_type`, `requested_by_user_id`, `request_reason`), `attempts`, `locate_attempts`, `next_attempt_at`, `lease_until`, `last_error_code`, `last_http_status`, `provider_refs`, timings, CAS `row_version`. FK `api_mode_connections` and `api_mailboxes` RESTRICT; no other FK. Terminal rows pruned after `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_RETENTION_DAYS` (400)                              |
| Copy state                                          | `api_message_copies.remediation_state` (`none`, `pending`, `simulated`, `remediated`, `restored`, `failed`, `skipped`), `remediation_action` (in effect or last attempted), `remediation_generation` (completed reversals), `last_action_id`, `remediation_updated_at`; index `(organization_id, remediation_state, received_at)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Mailbox and connection                              | `api_mailboxes.quarantine_container_id` / `tag_container_id` (opaque, cached, re-ensured once on `404`), `containers_checked_at`; `api_mode_connections.remediation_paused` / `remediation_paused_at` (policy actions wait, restores and admin actions run)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Counters                                            | `api_mode_counters` + `remediation_succeeded`, `remediation_failed`, `remediation_skipped`, `remediation_simulated`, `restores_succeeded`, `restores_failed`, `remediation_throttled`, `remediation_latency_count`, `remediation_latency_sum_ms`, `remediation_latency_max_ms` (latency = `completed_at − verdict_at` of policy actions)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Quarantine entries                                  | `quarantine_messages.location` (`gateway` default, `mailbox`), `mailbox_container` (`quarantine_folder`, `junk`, `deleted_items`), `api_copy_id` + `api_copy_received_at`, `api_mailbox_id`, `api_remediation_action_id` (no FKs); indexes `(organization_id, location, quarantined_at, id)`, `(api_copy_id, api_copy_received_at)`. For `location = mailbox`: `origin = api_remediation`, `lifecycle_round` = the copy's remediation generation, `message_id` = the copy's registry message, `eml_object_key` = the copy's EML. Appended: `QuarantineOrigin` + `api_remediation`; `QuarantineActionType` + `mailbox_remediated`, `mailbox_restored`, `mailbox_restore_failed`, `mailbox_deleted`; `QuarantineReleaseStatus` + `restored`, `restore_failed` |
| RBAC, features                                      | None new: `remediation:read` / `remediation:write` (API-1); the quarantine permissions apply unchanged to mailbox entries; feature `api_mode_protection`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

**Documents.**

| Document                         | Shape                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `api-remediation-policy/1`       | `{ v: 1, rules: [{ id: string (1..48, [a-z0-9_-], unique), when: { verdicts: MessageVerdict[] (1..9, unique; not clean/unknown), minLevel?, maxLevel? ('low'\|'medium'\|'high'\|'critical', max ≥ min), hardBlock?: boolean, minScore?: 0..1000 }, action: 'move_quarantine'\|'move_junk'\|'tag'\|'soft_delete'\|'hard_delete'\|'none' }] (1..16, first match wins), remediateScanFailures?: false }`; strict (unknown keys refused); `when.policyDispositions` reserved and refused (`unsupported_until_policy_evaluation`; since step API-4 refused with `use_policy_mapping`, tenant policy dispositions map through the document-level `policyMapping`, §39). Platform default P1: `p1-high-confidence-malware-phishing-hb` (malware, phishing, `hardBlock: true` → `move_quarantine`), `p1-high-confidence-malware-phishing` (malware, phishing, `minLevel: critical` → `move_quarantine`), `p1-other-malware-phishing` (→ `tag`), `p1-suspicious` (suspicious, bec → `tag`); presets P1, `DETECT_ONLY_START`, `STRICT` (P1 + spam → `move_junk`) |
| `api-remediation-decision/1`     | `{ v: 1, policy: { scope: 'platform_default'\|'organization'\|'domain', publicId: string\|null, rowVersion: int\|null, sha256 }, mode, ruleId, inputs: { verdict, riskScore, level, hardBlocks (≤ 9), verdictAuthority }, policyOutcome: null, notes: string[] }` on the action row (`trigger = policy`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `provider_refs`                  | `{ before: string[] (≤ 5), after: string[] (≤ 5), container?: 'quarantine_folder'\|'junk'\|'deleted_items'\|'inbox' }`; opaque provider message ids only, never returned by the api                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Quarantine entry `location` view | `{ kind: 'gateway' } \| { kind: 'mailbox', container, mailboxAddress, provider, connectionId, detectionId, remediationId, remediationStatus }` on entry list and detail (self-service without `connectionId`); list filter `location=gateway\|mailbox`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Overview `remediation` block     | `{ policyMode, policySource, paused, succeeded1h, failed1h, succeeded24h, failed24h, skipped24h, simulated24h, restores24h, throttled24h, backlog: { pending, oldestPendingAgeSeconds }, latencyP50Seconds?, latencyAvgSeconds24h }` on `GET /api-mode/overview` and the connection views                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

**Vocabulary.**

| Term                     | Values                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Actions                  | `move_quarantine`, `move_junk`, `tag`, `soft_delete`, `hard_delete`; reversals `restore_inbox`, `remove_tag`                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Triggers                 | `policy`, `admin`, `release` (quarantine release or approval), `end_user` (self-release), `system`                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Statuses                 | `pending → in_progress → succeeded \| failed \| pending (retry)`; `cancelled`; `skipped` and `simulated` terminal at insert                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Skip reasons             | `inline_authority`, `out_of_scope`, `hard_delete_disabled`, `remediation_paused`, `no_mailbox_access`                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Result and failure codes | `already_in_target`, `hard_delete_unsupported_provider`, `message_not_found`, `mailbox_access_revoked`, `provider_forbidden`, `provider_rejected`, `provider_unavailable` (retries exhausted), `integrity_mismatch`, `entry_not_releasing`, `internal_error` (outcome not writable within the attempt budget) |

**Identifiers.** `action_key` = lower-case hex SHA-256 of `"api-remediation/1\0<copyPublicId>\0<action>\0<generation>"` (generation as a decimal integer; `@email-gateway/mailbox-api` `remediationActionKey`).

**Audit actions.** `api_mode.remediation_policy_updated` `{ scope, from: { mode, sha256 }, to: { mode, sha256 }, reason }`, `api_mode.remediation_requested`, `api_mode.restore_requested`, `api_mode.remediation_retry_requested`, `api_mode.remediation_executed` (system, `{ actionId, action, trigger, result, reasonCode, copyId, mailboxId, connectionId }`, ids and codes only); `api_mode.settings_updated` metadata gains `remediationPaused`. Quarantine audit actions are unchanged (release and approval flows of mailbox entries record as step 5.3).

**Health checks** appended to `api-mode-health/1` (the `checks` cap of 16 holds: 13 checks): `remediation.failures_1h` (failed / (succeeded + failed) over the last hour with ≥ 5 attempts: above `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_FAILURE_WARN_PERCENT` warn, above `_FAILURE_FAIL_PERCENT` fail), `remediation.backlog` (oldest pending policy/admin/release action older than `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_BACKLOG_WARN_MINUTES` warn, `_BACKLOG_FAIL_MINUTES` fail), `remediation.access` (effective policy `enforce` but no active mailbox grant → warn), `remediation.paused` (paused → warn). Status transitions use the existing `api_mode.health_changed` audit row and alert kind `api_mode_unhealthy`.

**Events and topology.** No new event, queue, binding, policy or role user: **topology v17 unchanged**. Decisions run inside the existing `email.mailbox` consumer (`mail.message.copy_scanned.v1`, ledger `mailbox-worker.copies`); execution is a DB-claimed table. A mailbox entry's release writes **no** `mail.quarantine.release_requested.v1`. `notification.alert_requested.v1` kind `api_mode_unhealthy` is unchanged (the alert lists the failing check titles).

**Redis.** The existing keys only: `mbx:rb:<apiConnectionPublicId>:<family>:s<epochSecond>` (remediation shares the tenant's per-family budget with feeds and inventory), `mbx:throttle:<apiConnectionPublicId>:<family>` (honoured before every remediation call; a 429/503 sets it), `mbx:cnt:<apiConnectionId>:<yyyymmddhh>` with the new hash fields `remediation_succeeded`, `remediation_failed`, `remediation_skipped`, `remediation_simulated`, `restores_succeeded`, `restores_failed`, `remediation_throttled`, `remediation_latency_count`, `remediation_latency_sum_ms`, `remediation_latency_max_ms`.

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "API-mode remediation (step API-2 …)", design R17; nothing secret): `MAILBOX_WORKER_ROLES` default `copies,feeds,inventory,health,remediate`; `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_TICK_MS`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_BATCH`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_LEASE_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_CONCURRENCY`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_CONCURRENCY_PER_CONNECTION`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_LOCATE_ATTEMPTS`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_BACKOFF_MAX_SECONDS`, `API_MODE_QUARANTINE_FOLDER_NAME`, `API_MODE_QUARANTINE_FOLDER_HIDDEN`, `API_MODE_GMAIL_QUARANTINE_LABEL`, `API_MODE_TAG_NAME`, `API_MODE_HARD_DELETE_ENABLED` (mailbox-worker and api), `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_FAILURE_WARN_PERCENT`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_FAILURE_FAIL_PERCENT`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_BACKLOG_WARN_MINUTES`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_BACKLOG_FAIL_MINUTES`, `API_MODE_REMEDIATION_RETENTION_DAYS`.

## 38. API-mode clawback, search and purge, internal mail and reported phishing (step API-3)

[Design note](../plan/steps/37-api-mode-clawback-reporting.md) (decisions C1–C20, invariants C-I1–C-I7; note 35 N1–N9 and note 36 R-I1–R-I6 hold, R-I2 and R-I3 amended by ADR-0103), [ADR-0103](adr/0103-api-mode-clawback-search-and-purge.md) (retroactive clawback and search and purge; supersedes ADR-0101 decisions 3 and 4 in part), [ADR-0104](adr/0104-api-mode-internal-mail-and-reported-phishing.md) (internal mail and user-reported phishing; supersedes ADR-0100 D7.4 in part). Migration `20261127013832_api_mode_clawback_reporting` ([database README](../database/README.md#step-api-3-migration-20261127013832_api_mode_clawback_reporting)). Status: **DESIGNED**; WP1 (data model, RBAC, events, topology v18, configuration, ADRs, this section) is built; the libraries, the mailbox-worker `clawback` and `reports` roles, the email-worker late signals, the notification-worker feedback, the api routes, the assistant tools, the portal and the acceptance suite follow in WP2–WP7. Out of scope by product-owner decision (2026-11-26, design §12.2): PO-D1 (wizard and domain page) and PO-D2 (tenant policy evaluation on copies, `threat_detected` alerts for API-mode detections, a coverage-by-mode page).

**Invariants (design §2).** C-I1 one action engine (a clawback never calls a provider: it writes `api_remediation_actions` rows run by the `remediate` role; the `clawback` and `reports` roles make no provider call); C-I2 the hit list is frozen (targets are materialized once; submit, approval and restore act on those rows only); C-I3 never weaken by clawback (escalation only; never replaces a stronger remediation, never restores, never touches a copy restored after the signal); C-I4 two people above the threshold (more than the organization's `purge_approval_mailboxes` distinct eligible mailboxes, any hard-delete purge and any automatic clawback above its cap need a second user holding `remediation:purge` who is not the requester or submitter; API keys never approve); C-I5 reports never act by themselves (only an analyst's decision creates a clawback; report-mailbox copies are never remediated); C-I6 content-free state; C-I7 tenant from trusted context (api: the principal, foreign ids `404`; workers: the event envelope's organization echo-checked on every read; a platform indicator is fanned out per API-active organization by the worker).

**Data (migration `api_mode_clawback_reporting`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md#step-api-3-changes-api-mode-clawback-and-reports)).** 196 tables (+5, none partitioned).

| Contract                                      | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api_clawbacks` (`ApiClawback`)               | One row per clawback: `trigger` (`late_verdict`, `threat_intel`, `campaign`, `report`, `admin`), `signal_key` (C2.5; NULL for admin / report / campaign) with UNIQUE `(organization_id, signal_key)` (automatic signals use `INSERT IGNORE`), `signal` (`api-clawback-signal/1`), `criteria` (`api-clawback-criteria/1`), `criteria_sha256`, `decision_mode` (`policy`, `explicit`), `action` (explicit: `move_quarantine`, `move_junk`, `soft_delete`, `hard_delete`), signal facts (`signal_verdict`, `signal_level` `low`..`critical`, `signal_hard_blocks`), `status` (14 values, below), `cancel_requested_at`, `restore_status`, target / mailbox / restore counts, approval (`requires_approval`, `approval_reasons`, `approval_status`, `approval_requested_at`, `approval_expires_at`, `approval_decided_at`, `approved_by_user_id`, `approval_decision_reason`, `approver_permissions_hash`), requester (`requested_by_type`, `requested_by_user_id`, `submitted_by_user_id`, `request_reason`, `submitted_at`), `report_id`, seed copy, `signal_at`, claim (`lease_until`, `next_step_at`), `last_error_code`, CAS `row_version`. FK organizations RESTRICT only. Terminal rows (with targets) pruned after `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_RETENTION_DAYS` (400) |
| `api_clawback_targets` (`ApiClawbackTarget`)  | One row per matched copy: UNIQUE `(clawback_id, copy_id, copy_received_at)` (`INSERT IGNORE` in pages of 1 000), connection, mailbox, copy and registry message (no FKs), `match_keys` (JSON array of `message_id`, `attachment`, `url`, `url_domain`, `sender_subject`, `sender`, `sender_domain`), `status` (`matched`, `skipped`, `simulated`, `queued`, `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled`), `reason_code`, `planned_action` (`ApiCopyRemediationAction`), `action_id`, `restore_status` (`none`, `queued`, `succeeded`, `failed`, `skipped`), `restore_reason_code`, `restore_action_id`. FK `api_clawbacks` CASCADE                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `api_user_reports` (`ApiUserReport`)          | One row per report e-mail: UNIQUE `(report_copy_id, report_copy_received_at)` (idempotent intake), `reporter_address` (lower case; prefix index 100), reporter mailbox / user (no FK), `status` (`received`, `parsing`, `scanning`, `pending_triage`, `in_review`, `resolved`, `rejected`), `reject_reason`, `identification` (`attachment`, `inline`, `none`, `unsupported`), `original` (`api-report-original/1`), `original_message_id_hash`, `group_key`, the original's copy and registry message, `original_ingested`, scan facts (`scan_verdict`, `scan_risk_score`, `scan_level` `RiskLevel`), `assessment`, `analyst_verdict`, `analyst_note` (untrusted), triage, `clawback_id`, `ack_feedback` / `result_feedback` (`none`, `queued`, `skipped`), `reported_at`, claim, CAS `row_version`. FK organizations, `api_mode_connections` and `api_mailboxes` (report mailbox) RESTRICT. Terminal rows pruned after `API_MODE_REPORT_RETENTION_DAYS` (400)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `api_mode_org_settings` (`ApiModeOrgSetting`) | PK + FK `organization_id` RESTRICT; `internal_mail_enabled` (1), `auto_clawback` (`enforce`, `review`, `off`; `enforce`), `auto_max_mailboxes` (500), `purge_approval_mailboxes` (50), `clawback_lookback_days` (14), `ti_min_confidence` (80), `report_ack_enabled` (1), `report_feedback_enabled` (1), CAS `row_version`, `updated_by_user_id`, `purge_approval_raised_by` (principal key of the last raise) and `purge_approval_before_raise` (review fix migration 46: that principal's purges are decided against the earlier value). No row = the defaults (`ORG_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS`, `resolveOrgSettings` of `@email-gateway/mailbox-api`, equal to the column defaults)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `api_mode_cursors` (`ApiModeCursor`)          | Platform table: `name` PK (`ti:threat_indicators`, `ti:malware_signatures`), `position` (`{ createdAt, id }`), `updated_at`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Existing tables                               | `api_remediation_actions.trigger` + `clawback`, `purge` (appended) and `clawback_id` (no FK) + index `(clawback_id, status)`; `api_message_copies.verdict_revision` (late signals that raised the stored verdict); `api_mailboxes.report_mailbox` / `report_mailbox_at` + index `(organization_id, report_mailbox)`; `api_mode_counters` + `internal_copies`, `clawback_targets_queued`, `clawback_targets_succeeded`, `clawback_targets_failed`, `reports_received`, `reports_resolved`; `threat_indicators` and `malware_signatures` index `(created_at, id)` (TI sweep cursor)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| RBAC, features                                | New permission **`remediation:purge`** (category `remediation`; organization_admin, security_analyst, super_admin; not in the tenant read set; catalog 77): create a search / clawback (admin, campaign, report), submit, approve, deny, cancel, restore (restore also needs `quarantine:release`). `remediation:read`: clawback, report, settings and report-mailbox reads; `remediation:write`: org settings, report mailboxes, triage. No new feature (`api_mode_protection`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Purge order                                   | Organization purge (`API_MODE_PURGE_ORDER`): `api_clawback_targets` (cascade), `api_clawbacks`, `api_user_reports` before `api_remediation_actions`, `api_mailboxes`, `api_mode_connections`; `api_mode_org_settings` before the organization                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

**Documents.**

| Document                  | Shape                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api-clawback-signal/1`   | `{ v: 1, kind: 'sandbox_revision'\|'url_dynamic'\|'threat_indicator'\|'malware_signature'\|'admin'\|'campaign'\|'report', refs: { messageId?, receivedAt?, scanId?, revision?, indicatorId?, signatureId?, reportId?, seedCopyId?, campaignRef? (reserved for ATP-4; refused `unsupported_until_atp4`) }, facts: { verdict, level, hardBlocks (≤ 9), score? } \| null, signalAt }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `api-clawback-criteria/1` | `{ v: 1, window: { from, to } (to − from ≤ API_MODE_CLAWBACK_MAX_WINDOW_DAYS; from ≥ now − 186 days), keys: { messageIdHashes?: hex64[1..10], attachmentSha256?: hex64[1..20], urlSha256?: hex64[1..20], urlDomains?: domain[1..5] (TI only), senders?: address[1..5] (TI only), senderDomains?: domain[1..5] (TI only), senderSubject?: { sender, subjectKey (1..128) } }, directions: ('inbound'\|'internal')[1..2], includeReportMailboxes: false }`; at least one key; keys combine with OR; `senderSubject` needs a window ≤ 7 days unless it comes from a campaign seed; strict zod in `@email-gateway/mailbox-api` |
| `api-report-original/1`   | `{ messageIdHash?, sender?, subjectKey?, date?, attachmentSha256 (≤ 20), urlSha256 (≤ 50), extraOriginals }`; hashes, normalized keys, the sender address and the date only, never bodies                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Overview blocks           | `GET /api-mode/overview` (organization and per connection) gains `internal: { copies24h, enabled }`, `clawback: { active, reviewRequired, pendingApproval, completed24h, targetsSucceeded24h, targetsFailed24h, oldestActiveAgeSeconds }`, `reports: { pendingTriage, inReview, received24h, resolved24h, oldestPendingAgeSeconds, reportMailboxes }` (counters + bounded live counts, capped 10 000)                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Clawback approval view    | `GET /api-mode/clawbacks/{id}`: requester, submitter, approver, `approvalReasons`, `approvalStatus`, `approvalExpiresAt`, `canSubmit` / `canApprove` hints; criteria shown with hashes truncated, the sender and the subject key                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Events** (registered in `EVENT_REGISTRY`, schemas in `packages/event-contracts/src/events.ts`, tests in `api-mode-clawback-events.test.ts`).

| Event                               | Producer                                                                                                                   | Exchange / aggregate                                      | Queue (ledger)                                                | Payload (strict)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `mail.threat.late_signal.v1`        | email-worker (`sandbox-finalizer.ts` risk revision 1, `url-results.ts` late dynamic verdict), in the producing transaction | `email.security` / `message` (decimal message id)         | `email.mailbox` (`mailbox-worker.copies`)                     | `kind` (`sandbox_revision`, `url_dynamic`), `messageId`, `receivedAt`, `scanId`, `revision` (≥ 1 for `sandbox_revision`; null for `url_dynamic`), `verdict` (`MessageVerdict`), `level` (`RiskLevel`), `hardBlocks` (≤ 9, `^[a-z0-9_.:-]{1,64}$`, unique), `attachmentSha256`, `urlSha256` (lower-case hex64, ≤ 20 each, unique; `url_dynamic`: ≥ 1 URL hash, no attachment hash; `sandbox_revision`: no URL hash), `signalAt` |
| `mail.report.feedback_requested.v1` | api (triage `resolved`), mailbox-worker `reports` (leaving `received`)                                                     | `email.policy` / `organization` (decimal organization id) | `email.notifications` (`notification-worker.report-feedback`) | `reportId` (uuid), `kind` (`received`, `resolved`), `requestedAt`; no address, subject, verdict or text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `notification.alert_requested.v1`   | api / mailbox-worker                                                                                                       | unchanged                                                 | unchanged                                                     | kinds + **`api_mode_purge_approval`** (required ref `clawbackId`; new optional ref `clawbackId: uuid`; severity `medium`, `high` for hard delete; recipients holding `remediation:purge`) and **`api_mode_report_received`** (no required ref; at most once per organization per `API_MODE_REPORT_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_MINUTES`; recipients holding `remediation:write`)                                                         |

**Topology v18.** `TOPOLOGY_VERSION = 18` = v17 + two bindings: `org.*.mail.threat.late_signal.v1` (`email.security`) → `email.mailbox` and `org.*.mail.report.feedback_requested.v1` (`email.policy`) → `email.notifications`. 26 queues, **80** bindings, 26 policies, 14 role users; no permission change (the relay publishes every `org.` key; `eg-mailbox` reads its queue). `email.mailbox` stays reconciled: the copies handler writes its ledger row for every late signal, the acked ones included ([messaging topology](messaging-topology.md#api-mode-clawback-events-step-api-3)).

**Vocabulary.**

| Term                      | Values                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Clawback triggers         | `late_verdict`, `threat_intel`, `campaign`, `report`, `admin`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Clawback statuses         | `searching`, `ready`, `review_required`, `pending_approval`, `queued`, `running`, `completed`, `completed_with_errors`, `no_match`, `too_broad`, `denied`, `expired`, `cancelled`, `failed` (transitions: design C5.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Approval                  | threshold per submitter: `min(purge_approval_mailboxes, purge_approval_before_raise)` for the principal who raised it, else the stored value; reasons `mailbox_threshold`, `hard_delete`, `auto_cap`; statuses `pending`, `approved`, `denied`, `expired`, `cancelled`; refusals `api_mode_purge_approval_forbidden` (reasons `api_key`, `missing_permission`, `requester`), `api_mode_purge_approval_not_pending`                                                           |
| Restore                   | clawback `restore_status` `none`, `requested`, `running`, `completed`, `completed_with_errors`; target restore codes `approval_required`, `refused`, `not_reversible`, `entry_<state>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Target statuses and codes | `matched`, `skipped`, `simulated`, `queued`, `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled`; codes `policy_none`, `simulated`, `no_mailbox_access`, `out_of_scope`, `mailbox_inactive`, `report_mailbox`, `internal_mail_disabled`, `hard_delete_disabled`, `remediation_in_progress`, `already_remediated`, `restored_after_signal`, plus every §37 result and failure code                                                                                                             |
| Action triggers           | + `clawback` (automatic; waits while the connection is paused), `purge` (admin / report / campaign; runs like `admin`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Remediation skip reasons  | + `internal_mail_disabled` (decision vocabulary, C8.5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Report statuses           | `received`, `parsing`, `scanning`, `pending_triage`, `in_review`, `resolved`, `rejected`; identification `attachment`, `inline`, `none`, `unsupported`; reject reasons `external_reporter`, `loop`, `auto_submitted`, `rate_limited`, `analyst_rejected`, `too_large`, `parse_failed`; assessments `malicious`, `suspicious`, `clean`, `unknown`; analyst verdicts `malicious`, `phishing_simulation`, `spam`, `clean`, `inconclusive`; feedback `none`, `queued`, `skipped` |
| Signal hard blocks        | `hb.clawback.url`, `hb.clawback.threat_intel`, `hb.clawback.analyst`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Copy direction            | `internal` journal copies recorded when `internal_mail_enabled` (C8); copies-handler results `recorded_internal`, `outbound_only`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

**Identifiers.** `signal_key` = lower-case hex SHA-256 of the NUL-separated tuple with version prefix `api-clawback-signal/1`: late verdict `(…, 'late', messageId, receivedAt, kind, revision ?? urlSha256)`, threat intelligence `(…, 'ti', 'threat_indicators'|'malware_signatures', rowId)`. Report original ingest key `v2:rpt:` + SHA-256 of the report public id (`ingest_source = mailbox_api`).

**Audit actions.** `api_mode.clawback_created`, `api_mode.clawback_submitted`, `api_mode.clawback_approval_requested`, `api_mode.clawback_approved`, `api_mode.clawback_denied`, `api_mode.clawback_cancelled`, `api_mode.clawback_restore_requested`, `api_mode.clawback_completed` (system), `api_mode.clawback_restore_completed` (system), `api_mode.clawback_expired` (system), `api_mode.settings_org_updated` (from / to), `api_mode.report_mailbox_updated`, `api_mode.report_triaged`, `api_mode.report_clawback_requested`. Ids, counts, codes and hashes; never subjects, bodies or criteria text beyond the sender address.

**Health checks** appended to `api-mode-health/1` (15 of 16 allowed): `clawback.stalled` (a `queued` / `running` clawback of the connection's organization with targets on the connection older than `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_STALLED_WARN_MINUTES` warn, `_FAIL_MINUTES` fail), `reports.backlog` (connections owning a report mailbox: oldest `pending_triage` report older than `API_MODE_REPORT_TRIAGE_WARN_HOURS` warn; never fail). Same `api_mode_unhealthy` alert.

**Redis.** The existing `mbx:cnt:<apiConnectionId>:<yyyymmddhh>` hash gains `internal_copies`, `clawback_targets_queued`, `clawback_targets_succeeded`, `clawback_targets_failed`, `reports_received`, `reports_resolved`. No new key.

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "API-mode clawback and reports (step API-3 …)", design C17; nothing secret): `MAILBOX_WORKER_ROLES` default `copies,feeds,inventory,health,remediate,clawback,reports`; mailbox-worker `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_TICK_MS` (5000), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_LEASE_SECONDS` (120), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_MAX_TARGETS` (20000), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_QUEUE_BATCH` (500), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_MAX_PENDING_PER_CONNECTION` (1000), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_TI_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (60), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_TI_BATCH` (100), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_STALLED_WARN_MINUTES` (60), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_STALLED_FAIL_MINUTES` (240), `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_RETENTION_DAYS` (400), `API_MODE_REPORT_RETENTION_DAYS` (400), `API_MODE_REPORTS_TICK_MS` (5000), `API_MODE_REPORT_MAX_BYTES` (26214400), `API_MODE_REPORTS_PER_REPORTER_PER_HOUR` (30), `API_MODE_REPORT_TRIAGE_WARN_HOURS` (24), `API_MODE_REPORT_ALERT_MIN_INTERVAL_MINUTES` (60), `API_MODE_REPORT_SCAN_TIMEOUT_MINUTES` (60; review fix: an ingested original's overdue scan → triage with the report copy's scan); mailbox-worker and api `API_MODE_CLAWBACK_MAX_WINDOW_DAYS` (31), `API_MODE_PURGE_APPROVAL_TTL_HOURS` (72); api `API_MODE_REPORT_MAILBOXES_MAX` (5); existing `API_MODE_HARD_DELETE_ENABLED`.

## 39. API-mode parity and onboarding (step API-4)

[Design note](../plan/steps/38-api-mode-parity-onboarding.md) (decisions A1–A12 part A, parity; B1–B10 part B, onboarding; invariants P-I1–P-I7; note 35 N1–N9 hold with N1 amended by ADR-0102 and ADR-0105, note 36 R-I1–R-I6 and note 37 C-I1–C-I7 hold), [ADR-0105](adr/0105-api-mode-copy-policy-evaluation-and-policy-lane.md) (detect-mode policy evaluation of copies and the policy lane of remediation; supersedes ADR-0098 decisions 5 and 7, ADR-0044, ADR-0101 decision 1 and ADR-0104 decision 1 in part), [ADR-0106](adr/0106-api-mode-detections-alerts-webhooks.md) (API-mode detections in alerts and webhooks; supersedes ADR-0054 decision 8 in part), [ADR-0107](adr/0107-api-mode-onboarding-provider-ownership-scope-detect-only.md) (ownership through a bound provider tenant, mailbox scope, the detect-only start, the mode-specific wizard; supersedes step 2.1 D4 / 8.2 I6, ADR-0076 decision 1 and ADR-0098 decision 3 in part). Migration `20261127120100_api_mode_parity_onboarding` (migration 47; [database README](../database/README.md#step-api-4-migration-20261127120100_api_mode_parity_onboarding)). Status: **DESIGNED**; WP1 (data model, events, topology v19, configuration, ADRs, this section) is built; the `@email-gateway/policy-runtime` extraction, the mailbox-api policy lane, the webhook types, the mailbox-worker evaluation, the api routes, the integration-worker ownership check, the webhook and notification workers, the portal and the acceptance suite follow in WP2–WP7. Product-owner decision 2026-11-27 ("yes do before start atp"): PO-D1 and PO-D2 of notes 36/37 §12.2 are built here; ATP-1 is not started by this step.

**Invariants (design §2).** P-I1 detect means detect (a copy evaluation never applies a lifecycle edge, never writes `email_messages.final_verdict` / `final_action`, a delivery approval, a gateway quarantine entry, a policy copy object, `mail.policy.decided.v1`, `mail.message.archive_requested.v1` or a sender non-delivery notice; its only effects are the detect row, copy columns, API-2 remediation actions, ALERT requests and the detection event); P-I2 same engine, same facts (`@email-gateway/policy-runtime` + `@email-gateway/policy-dsl` exactly as the policy-engine; parity test on `inputSha256` and the disposition); P-I3 inline decides first (copies with `verdict_authority = inline` are never policy-evaluated, remediated automatically, or produce a detection event or alert); P-I4 never weaker than API-2 by accident (the policy lane only adds or strengthens; the only weakening is a tenant allow override on a level-only verdict; a policy failure falls back to the verdict lane); P-I5 outbound is never touched in a mailbox; P-I6 ownership is never weakened (bound tenant only, first-to-verify-wins and `verified_name` uniqueness unchanged, provider-proven domains never prove a binding); P-I7 tenant from trusted context, content-free state.

**Data (migration `api_mode_parity_onboarding`, [schema reference](../database/schema-reference.md#step-api-4-changes-api-mode-parity-and-onboarding)).** 197 tables (+1, not partitioned; 42 partitioned).

| Contract                                            | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `policy_execution_logs` (partitioned)               | + `evaluation_mode` (`PolicyEvaluationMode`: `inline` default, `detect`). Detect rows: `delivered_object = none`, `delivery` NULL, `object_key` NULL, `action` = `lifecycleOutcome(result, direction).finalAction` (recorded, never applied), action statuses from the A5 post-delivery map, `evaluation_seq` = the copy's lifecycle round (0); the unique `(message_id, evaluation_seq, received_at)` makes a replay reuse the stored row. No new index                                                                                                                                                             |
| `api_message_copies` (partitioned)                  | `direction` + `outbound` (appended; `ApiCopyDirection`); + `policy_state` (`ApiCopyPolicyState`: `none` default, `evaluated`, `inline`, `disabled`, `failed`, `not_applicable`), `policy_disposition` (`PolicyDisposition`, NULL), `policy_tagged` (0), `dlp_action` (`DlpAction`, NULL). No new index                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `api_mailboxes`                                     | + `scope_state` (`ApiMailboxScopeState`: `in_scope` default, `excluded`), `scope_selected` (0), `scope_evaluated_at`; index `(domain_id, scope_state)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `api_mode_org_settings`                             | + `policy_evaluation_enabled` (1), `outbound_detection_enabled` (1) (= `ORG_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `api_remediation_policies`                          | + `detect_only_until`, `detect_only_then` (`ApiDetectOnlyThen`: `remind`, `enforce`), `detect_only_started_at`, `detect_only_notified_at` (all NULL); index `(detect_only_until)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `api_mode_counters`                                 | + `policy_evaluations`, `policy_errors`, `outbound_detections`, `copy_detections` (INT UNSIGNED, 0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `domains`                                           | + `ownership_method` (`DomainOwnershipMethod`: `dns_txt`, `provider_tenant`; NULL while never verified); backfill `dns_txt` for every `verified` / `active` / `suspended` domain (asserted: none left NULL); every ownership win writes it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `domain_verifications`                              | `method` + `provider_tenant` (appended; `DomainVerificationMethod`); + `provider_connection_id` (no FK), `provider_result` (`DomainProviderCheckResult`: `verified`, `unverified`, `absent`, `alias`, `error`), `provider_checked_at`, `provider_error_code` (≤ 64); index `(method, status, provider_checked_at)`; `token` stays NOT NULL (provider rows store the fixed marker `provider-tenant`, never a secret)                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `api_mode_domain_settings` (`ApiModeDomainSetting`) | New, not partitioned: `public_id` (UUIDv7, unique), FK `organization_id` RESTRICT, `domain_id` UNIQUE FK `domains` CASCADE, `scope_kind` (`ApiModeScopeKind`: `all` default, `departments`, `mailboxes`), `scope_department_ids` (JSON, `tenant_departments.public_id` 1..20), `scope_changed_at`, `scope_recompute_requested_at`, `first_copy_id` / `first_copy_received_at` / `first_copy_at` (no FK), CAS `row_version`, `updated_by_user_id` (no FK); indexes `(organization_id)`, `(scope_recompute_requested_at)`. Created lazily with `INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`; no row = scope `all`, no first copy |
| RBAC, features, plans                               | None (no new permission, feature or plan change; the seed catalog is unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Purge order                                         | `ApiModeTenantDataRepository.purgeOrganization` deletes `api_mode_domain_settings` with the other API-mode tables before domains (the domain FK cascades anyway); detect rows follow the `policy_execution_logs` retention (6 months) and organization purge; provider verification rows follow the domain (FK cascade)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

**Documents.**

| Document                                     | Shape                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api-remediation-policy/1` + `policyMapping` | Optional strict object (additive; absent = the default): `quarantine`, `require_approval`, `reject` ∈ `move_quarantine`, `move_junk`, `soft_delete`, `tag`, `none` (default `move_quarantine`); `allow_tagged` ∈ `tag`, `none` (default `tag`); `hard_delete` never allowed; `when.policyDispositions` stays refused with issue code `use_policy_mapping`; presets carry no mapping                                                                                                                                                             |
| `api-remediation-decision/2`                 | `/1` fields + `policyOutcome: { disposition, tagged, overrideApplied, decisionSource, decisiveRuleRef \| null, floorReasons (≤ 8), dlpAction \| null, actionTypes (unique, ≤ 9) } \| null` + `lanes: { verdict: { ruleId, action } \| null, policy: { key: 'quarantine'\|'require_approval'\|'reject'\|'allow_tagged', action } \| null, winner: 'verdict'\|'policy'\|null, suppressedBy: 'policy_allow_override' \| null }` + `postDelivery: [{ type, status, reason }]` (≤ 9); readers accept `/1` and `/2` (one zod union)                   |
| `api-mode-scope/1`                           | `{ kind: 'all' } \| { kind: 'departments', departmentIds: uuid[1..API_MODE_SCOPE_MAX_DEPARTMENTS (20)] } \| { kind: 'mailboxes' }` (strict zod in `@email-gateway/mailbox-api`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Protection-coverage view                     | `GET /api-mode/protection-coverage`: `{ entitled, organizationMode, totals: { inline, api, hybrid }, capabilities: [{ code, inline, api, hybrid ('yes'\|'no'\|'partial'), noteCode }], items: [{ domainId, name, status, effectiveMode, inherited, inline: { applies, inboundEnabled, outboundEnabled, mxPointsToGateway }, api: { applies, connection, intake, scope, mailboxes: { inScope, covered, gap, total }, remediation: { source, mode, detectOnlyUntil }, policyEvaluation, lastCopyAt } \| null, gaps }], nextCursor }`; counts only |
| Domain onboarding view                       | `GET /api-mode/domains/{domainId}/onboarding`: `{ domainId, effectiveMode, steps: [{ key: 'ownership'\|'connect'\|'intake'\|'mailboxes'\|'action'\|'review', status: 'not_started'\|'in_progress'\|'done'\|'blocked'\|'skipped', blockers, nextAction }], ownership, connection, intake, scope, action, firstCopy: { status: 'waiting'\|'received', copy, waitingSinceMinutes, hints: [{ code, severity, link }] } }` computed by the pure `computeDomainOnboarding` from server state only                                                     |
| `api-mode-health/1`                          | + check `policy.errors_1h` (16 of 16 allowed): `policy_errors / (policy_evaluations + policy_errors)` over the last hour with ≥ 10 evaluations > 5 % warn, > 50 % fail; same `api_mode_unhealthy` alert                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

**Policy actions after delivery** (`POST_DELIVERY_ACTION_MAP`, design A5): `QUARANTINE`, `REQUIRE_APPROVAL` (a `review` mailbox entry: restore needs an approver), `REJECT` → the mapped mailbox action, status `requested` (`mapped_to_mailbox_action`); `TAG` / `WARN` → `allow_tagged`; `ALERT` → `requested`; a reject's sender notice → `not_applicable` (`post_delivery_no_bounce`); header edits, subject prefix, WARN banner → `post_delivery_no_header_edit`; `URL_REWRITE` → `post_delivery_no_rewrite`; `ARCHIVE` → `archive_follows_journaling`; encrypt / redirect → `post_delivery_no_encrypt` / `post_delivery_no_redirect`; any non-`ALERT` action of an outbound copy → `outbound_already_sent`. Policy-lane quarantine entry reason: the verdict mapping when the verdict lane would have chosen the same action, else `dlp` when a `policy.floor.dlp_*` reason decided, else `policy`.

**Events** (registered in `EVENT_REGISTRY`, schemas in `packages/event-contracts/src/events.ts`, tests in `api-mode-parity-events.test.ts`).

| Event                                   | Producer                                                                                                                                                     | Exchange / aggregate                             | Queue (ledger)                             | Payload (strict)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `mail.message.copy_detected.v1`         | mailbox-worker `copies` (the `copy_scanned.v1` ledger transaction; `correlation_id` from that event), once per registry copy message with a detection (A8.1) | `email.security` / `message` (message public id) | `email.webhooks` (`webhook-worker.fanout`) | `messageId` (decimal), `messagePublicId`, `receivedAt`, `ingestSource` (`journal_intake`, `mailbox_api`), `copySource` (`journal`, `graph`, `gmail`), `direction` (`inbound`, `internal`, `outbound`), `protectionMode` (`api` when any counted copy's domain is effective api, else `hybrid`), `verdict` (`MessageVerdict` \| null), `riskScore` (0..1000 \| null), `policy: { state ('evaluated', 'disabled', 'failed'), disposition \| null, decisionSource \| null, overrideApplied, actionTypes (`PolicyActionType`, unique, ≤ 9) }` (evaluated ⇔ disposition and source set; other states carry none), `dlp: { action ('alert', 'require_approval', 'quarantine', 'block'), verdict (code ≤ 32), categories (codes, unique, ≤ 8) } \| null`, `copies: { counted (≥ 1), remediation: { pending, simulated, skipped, none } (sum = counted) }`, `detectionIds` (copy public ids, unique, 1..20, ≤ counted), `decidedAt` |
