| `mail.policy.notification_requested.v1` | policy-engine (inline; never sets the new field) and mailbox-worker `copies` (detect policy ALERT, once per registry message, `lifecycleRound` 0)            | unchanged                                        | unchanged (`notification-worker.policy`)   | + optional `evaluationMode` (`inline`, `detect`; absent = `inline`); `detect` only with `kind = policy_alert` (never `sender_non_delivery`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `notification.alert_requested.v1`       | webhook-worker fanout (`threat_detected` also for `mailbox.threat.detected`; `dlp_detected`), mailbox-worker health leader (`api_mode_detect_only_ended`)    | unchanged                                        | unchanged                                  | kinds + **`dlp_detected`** (required refs `webhookEventId`, `messageId`, `receivedAt`; recipients holding `dlp:read`; tenant settings `alerts.dlpDetected { enabled (true), minSeverity 'low'\|'medium'\|'high' (medium), throttleMinutes 15..1440 (60) }`; opt-out kind `dlp_detected`) and **`api_mode_detect_only_ended`** (required ref `remediationPolicyId`, a new optional `uuid` ref; recipients holding `remediation:write`; severity `low` for `remind`, `medium` for `enforce`; once per period)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

**Topology v19.** `TOPOLOGY_VERSION = 19` = v18 + one binding: `org.*.mail.message.copy_detected.v1` (`email.security`) → `email.webhooks` (ledger `webhook-worker.fanout`, unchanged). 26 queues, **81** bindings, 26 policies, 14 role users; no permission change (the relay publishes every `org.` key; the webhook-worker user reads its queue) ([messaging topology](messaging-topology.md#api-mode-parity-events-step-api-4)).

**Public webhooks** (catalogue v1 additions per ADR-0054, ADR-0106; rows in [§30](#30-webhooks-and-notifications-step-62)): `mailbox.threat.detected` (source `copy_detected.v1` with a threat verdict, and `mail.attachment.sandbox_completed.v1` `malicious` of a copy message; `data = { message, detection: { stage 'scan'|'sandbox', verdict, riskScore, reasonCodes (≤ 32), protectionMode, direction, copySource, policyDisposition, mailboxCount, remediation \| null, attachment \| null, detectionIds (≤ 20), decidedAt } }`; severity as `email.threat.detected`; filters `directions`, `domainIds`, `minSeverity`, `verdicts`) and `mailbox.dlp.detected` (source `copy_detected.v1` with `dlp` not null; `data = { message, dlp: { action, verdict, categories, protectionMode, direction, policyDisposition, mailboxCount, remediation \| null, detectionIds, decidedAt } }`; severity `block`/`quarantine` high, `require_approval` medium, `alert` low; filters `directions`, `domainIds`, `minSeverity`). `email.threat.detected` v1 is unchanged and no longer projects sandbox verdicts of copies.

**Vocabulary.**

| Term                     | Values                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Policy evaluation mode   | `inline`, `detect` (`PolicyEvaluationMode`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Copy policy state        | `none`, `evaluated`, `inline`, `disabled`, `failed`, `not_applicable` (`ApiCopyPolicyState`; A1 skip table)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Copy direction           | + `outbound` (outbound detection copies on the sender's mailbox; never remediated, clawback target or coverage sighting)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Remediation skip reasons | + `outbound_not_remediable`, `policy_allow_override`; `out_of_scope` also for `mailboxInScope = false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Post-delivery reasons    | `mapped_to_mailbox_action`, `post_delivery_no_bounce`, `post_delivery_no_header_edit`, `post_delivery_no_rewrite`, `archive_follows_journaling`, `post_delivery_no_encrypt`, `post_delivery_no_redirect`, `outbound_already_sent` (statuses `requested`, `not_applicable`)                                                                                      |
| Ownership                | method `dns_txt`, `provider_tenant` (`DomainOwnershipMethod`); provider check results `verified`, `unverified`, `absent`, `alias`, `error` (`DomainProviderCheckResult`); verification method + `provider_tenant`                                                                                                                                               |
| Scope                    | kinds `all`, `departments`, `mailboxes` (`ApiModeScopeKind`); mailbox states `in_scope`, `excluded` (`ApiMailboxScopeState`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Detect-only              | then `remind`, `enforce` (`ApiDetectOnlyThen`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Capability codes (A10)   | `pre_delivery_blocking`, `hold_for_review`, `post_delivery_removal`, `retroactive_clawback`, `internal_mail`, `outbound_dlp_enforce`, `outbound_dlp_detect`, `tenant_policies`, `url_rewriting`, `message_tagging`, `user_reports`, `alerts_webhooks`                                                                                                           |
| Gap codes (A10)          | `inline_mx_not_at_gateway`, `inline_not_active`, `api_no_connection`, `api_no_mailbox_access`, `api_no_intake`, `api_coverage_gaps`, `api_mx_points_to_gateway`, `api_detect_only`, `api_policy_evaluation_off`                                                                                                                                                 |
| First-copy hints (B8)    | `domain_not_active`, `mode_not_api`, `connection_missing`, `connection_not_bound`, `mailbox_access_missing`, `journal_source_missing`, `journal_source_unverified`, `journal_source_silent`, `journal_seen_no_domain_recipient`, `feeds_disabled`, `feeds_initializing`, `no_mailboxes_in_scope`, `connection_throttled`, `copies_failing`, `send_test_message` |

**Audit actions.** `domain.provider_verification_requested` (`{ connectionId }`); `domain.verified` metadata + `method` (`dns_txt`, `provider_tenant`; `connectionId` for the provider); `api_mode.scope_updated` (`{ domainId, from, to, departments, mailboxes, reason }`); `api_mode.remediation_policy_auto_enforced` (system; `{ policyId, scope, simulatedSinceStart }`); `api_mode.detect_only_period_ended` (system); `api_mode.settings_org_updated` metadata + `policyEvaluationEnabled`, `outboundDetectionEnabled`; `api_mode.remediation_policy_updated` metadata + the `policyMapping` SHA-256 and `detectOnly`.

**HTTP.** New routes: `POST /domains/{domainId}/ownership/provider-check` (`domain:manage`; Idempotency-Key; rate class `sensitive`; `DOMAIN_PROVIDER_CHECKS_PER_HOUR` per organization, `429` + `Retry-After`; `202 { verificationId, status: 'pending' }`), `GET`/`PUT /api-mode/domains/{domainId}/scope` (`remediation:read` / `remediation:write`, Idempotency-Key, CAS `expectedRowVersion`), `GET /api-mode/protection-coverage` (`domain:read`, cursor, `limit` ≤ 100), `GET /api-mode/domains/{domainId}/onboarding` (`domain:read`). Changed: detections (`direction` `outbound`, `policyState`, `policyDisposition`, `policyTagged`, `dlpAction` and filters; detail `policy`, `dlp`), remediation detail (decision v1 and v2), settings (+ the two switches), remediation policy (`policyMapping`, `detectOnly: { until (now + 1 h … now + 30 d), then } | null` with `mode = detect_only` only), overview (`policy`, `outbound`, `scope` blocks), `GET /policies/executions?mode=inline|detect|all` (default `inline`), domain views (`ownership` block with `providerCheckAvailable`), `PUT /provider-connections/{id}/domains` (accepts `verified` domains). Errors: `domain_provider_connection_unavailable` (409), `domain_not_pending` (409), `connection_not_found` (404), `api_mode_scope_department_invalid` (422), `api_mode_scope_domain_not_api` (409), `api_mode_scope_conflict` (409, CAS), `api_mode_detect_only_invalid` (422); department delete refused while a scope references it (`409 department_in_use`, reference `api_mode_scope`). Copies stay `404` on every gateway message route (N1).

**Redis.** The `mbx:cnt:<apiConnectionId>:<yyyymmddhh>` hash gains `policy_evaluations`, `policy_errors`, `outbound_detections`, `copy_detections`; the api fixed window `rl:domain-provider-check:<organizationId>`.

**Configuration keys** (`.env.example` section "API-mode parity and onboarding (step API-4 …)"; nothing secret): mailbox-worker `API_MODE_POLICY_EVALUATION_ENABLED` (true; platform kill switch), `API_MODE_SCOPE_REFRESH_MINUTES` (15); api `API_MODE_DETECT_ONLY_DEFAULT_DAYS` (7), `API_MODE_SCOPE_MAX_MAILBOXES` (5000), `API_MODE_FIRST_COPY_HINT_AFTER_MINUTES` (10), `DOMAIN_PROVIDER_CHECKS_PER_HOUR` (10), `DOMAIN_PROVIDER_CHECK_TTL_MINUTES` (60); api and mailbox-worker `API_MODE_SCOPE_MAX_DEPARTMENTS` (20); integration-worker `PROVIDER_OWNERSHIP_CHECK_BATCH` (10). The mailbox-worker also reads `POLICY_PLATFORM_DEFAULT` and `POLICY_SNAPSHOT_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES` (same defaults and validation as the policy-engine).

**Metrics.** `api_mode_policy_evaluations_total{result}`, `api_mode_policy_evaluation_seconds` (0.01/0.05/0.1/0.5/1/5), `api_mode_copy_detections_total{kind="threat|dlp|policy",direction}`, `api_mode_outbound_detections_total`, `api_mode_scope_mailboxes{state}`, `api_mode_detect_only_policies{then}`; no organization labels. Prometheus rule `ApiModePolicyEvaluationErrors` (error ratio > 0.05 for 15 min, warning).
